*comment ============================================================================
*comment SHARED SMALL-COMBAT ENGINE
*comment All dice math (attack rolls, damage, spell slots, temp HP, locks)
*comment lives in startup.txt -- this file just wires those primitives into
*comment a playable round-by-round loop and owns the mechanical shell every
*comment fight shares (the choice skeleton, the enemy-turn resolution). Every static narration text pool this
*comment file selects from (weapon hits/misses/finishers, enemy type flavor, spell flavor) lives in
*comment combat_prose.txt instead (split out 2026-09-28 to keep this file mechanics-focused) -- reached with
*comment *gosub_scene combat_prose <label>, never a bare *gosub, same as every other cross-file call in this
*comment codebase. See combat_prose.txt's own header for the full list and the calling convention.
*comment
*comment Calling convention: a scene starts a fight with
*comment   *gosub_scene combat fight_<name>
*comment and gets back combat_outcome set to "victory", "rescued", or
*comment "fled" once it returns. Nothing in here prints start-of-fight or
*comment end-of-fight prose -- that's deliberate: the kill/retreat and
*comment whatever NPC's reaction to it read as one continuous narrative beat
*comment when the calling quest scene writes both together, instead of a
*comment jarring cut between this file's text and theirs.
*comment
*comment ALLY POOL: combat_ally1-4_* (startup.txt) plus a reusable type library (apply_ally_type/
*comment apply_ally_roster, near fight_library below) -- a scene sets combat_ally_lib_1 (and optionally _2/_3/_4)
*comment to a type id ("archer", "guard") before calling fight_library, same convention as combat_lib_1 for
*comment enemies. Ranged allies attack every turn regardless of distance; melee allies close their own
*comment combat_ally<N>_range (independent of the player's combat_enemy_range) one tier a turn before they can
*comment strike. fight_narrate_ally_attack narrates either direction (ally-vs-enemy, and enemy-vs-ally via
*comment interception below) -- both sides are named purely by epithet, so one label covers both.
*comment
*comment ADDING A FIGHT: use fight_library (near the end of this file). Set
*comment combat_lib_1 (and _2 through _6) to library type ids, set combat_is_nonlethal if it should end in surrender, then *gosub_scene combat fight_library.
*comment Nothing in this file needs editing, and no prose is written per fight. Every fight in the game is built this way;
*comment none is hand-authored. Only a foe no library type fits should be written out by hand, as below.
*comment
*comment Hand-authored alternative: write a new *label fight_<name> block below
*comment that sets the enemy's stats (combat_enemy_*), combat_enemy_epithet,
*comment and SIX flavor triplets, each as _1/_2/_3 (picked at random per hit,
*comment see pick_weapon_flavor / fight_narrate_enemy_turn -- a fight that
*comment only ever prints one fixed sentence per outcome reads as a stuck
*comment record across a multi-round fight):
*comment   combat_flavor_weapon_hit_1/2/3, _miss_1/2/3       (player, melee)
*comment   combat_flavor_weapon_hit_ranged_1/2/3, _miss_ranged_1/2/3
*comment     (player, ranged -- needs its own wording, not melee text with
*comment      ${weapon} swapped in, which reads wrong for a bow/crossbow)
*comment   combat_flavor_enemy_hit_1/2/3, _miss_1/2/3         (enemy, slot 1
*comment     and the default/fallback for slot 2 and beyond)
*comment All three variants for a given line should vary the actual PHYSICAL
*comment ACTION (a thrust vs. a feint-and-cut vs. closing distance under a
*comment guard), not just reshuffle adjectives around the same motion.
*comment Optionally, a distinctly-named multi-enemy fight can also give any slot 2 and beyond its OWN triplets
*comment (combat_flavor_enemy2_hit/miss_1/2/3 through _enemy6_, startup.txt) instead of falling back to the shared
*comment one -- see fight_granary_rats' three rats. (The Dissonant Whispers lines are
*comment no longer per fight: the hub writes them, naming the foe.) Then
*comment *gosub fight_setup and *goto fight_round_hub. Everything else -- every spell/cantrip/
*comment ability option, the enemy's attack resolution, retreat/rescue
*comment handling -- is already shared and needs no duplication.
*comment
*comment Enemies drawn from the type library (apply_enemy_type, see ENEMY TYPE LIBRARY below) need none of the enemy
*comment flavor above: their hit, miss and move-2 prose lives once per type in flavor_type_<name> (eight hit/miss
*comment pairs and three move-2 pairs), and a fight only sets a per-slot variable to replace one line with local color.
*comment
*comment Every cantrip (Fire Bolt, Ray of Frost, Shocking Grasp, Eldritch
*comment Blast, Chill Touch, Vicious Mockery) and buff/utility spell below is
*comment its OWN bespoke *choice option with its own real description and
*comment RAW secondary effect where one maps cleanly onto this engine
*comment (Ray of Frost/Vicious Mockery both set combat_enemy_disadvantage,
*comment matching what the dossier's print_ability_card already promises the
*comment player -- see choicescript_stats.txt). This used to be a single
*comment generic "Channel ${attack_cantrip_name}" option reading four shared
*comment variables (has_attack_cantrip/attack_cantrip_name/_stat/
*comment _dice_sides, populated by a detect_attack_cantrip primitive) --
*comment that was more reusable to write but broke two things: every cantrip
*comment read identically ("the working..."), and the *else-chained
*comment detection only ever exposed the FIRST matching cantrip slot, so a
*comment build with two attack cantrips could never reach the second one in
*comment combat at all. Deliberately avoid an enemy-specific wording in these
*comment options -- they describe the PLAYER's own casting motion, not the
*comment creature (parametrize with ${combat_enemy_epithet} instead) -- so
*comment new ones never need a per-fight override, same as the buff spells.
*comment ============================================================================

*label fight_setup
*gosub_scene equipment expire_timed_weapon_magic
*gosub_scene character_progression refresh_class_resource_maxs
*gosub_scene combat_dice combat_player_sync
*set combat_player_bane_expires_round 0
*set combat_player_concentrating "none"
*set combat_conc_result false
*set combat_conc_save_triggered false
*set combat_conc_save_banner ""
*set combat_conc_ended_banner ""
*set ensnaring_armed false
*set ensnaring_expires_round 0
*comment STALE SLOTS (2026-10-21, from the dev hub: summoned Blight Twigs showed in the next fight, and enemies of a fight the player fled did too). A slot beyond this fight's count keeps whatever hit points the last fight left in it, and the sidebar lane and anything that scans every slot see a living combatant there. Zero every ally and enemy slot above the counts before the fight is built; the slots in use are filled by the library or the hand-authored setup as always.
*temp fss_e combat_enemy_count
*temp fss_a combat_ally_count
*temp fss_i 1
*label fight_setup_stale_loop
*if (fss_i <= combat_enemy_slot_cap)
  *if (fss_i > fss_e)
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_hp")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_max_hp")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_blinded")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_blinded_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_slowed")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_off_guard")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_off_guard_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_off_guard_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_pinned")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_pinned_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_pinned_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_hobbled")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_hobbled_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_hobbled_src")} ""

    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_dazed")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_dazed_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_dazed_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_off_balance")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_off_balance_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_off_balance_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_bleeding")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_bleeding_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_bleeding_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_slowed_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_frightened")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_frightened_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_frightened_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_blinded_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_slowed_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_restrained")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_restrained_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_restrained_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_paralyzed")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_paralyzed_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_paralyzed_src")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_spell_1")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cantrip")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_blessed")} false
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_commanded")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_spell_2")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_spell_3")} ""
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_uses_1")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_uses_2")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_uses_3")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_chance_1")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_chance_2")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_chance_3")} 0
    *gosub_scene combat_abilities ability_clear_slot (("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_")
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_dc")} 0
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_cast_atk_bonus")} 0

    *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_spell_base_id")} ""
  *set {(("combat_enemy" & fss_i) & "_concentrating")} "none"
  *set fss_i (fss_i + 1)
  *goto fight_setup_stale_loop

*comment Stat overrides (see startup.txt): a scene that wants a tougher or weaker fighter than the library
*comment type sets combat_enemy_hp_override, _ac_override and _atk_override (and the combat_enemy2_ / combat_enemy3_
*comment versions) before calling the fight. They are applied here, after every slot has been built and before slot 1 is
*comment snapshotted, so they work on any type and on any fight label. The special move's to-hit follows the basic attack's
*comment (combat_abilities ability_refresh_variant derives it from the final attack bonus), so a +2 captain is +2 on every swing. 999 is "not set"; each is put back to 999 once used.
*if (not(combat_enemy_hp_override = 999))
  *set combat_enemy_hp combat_enemy_hp_override
  *set combat_enemy_max_hp combat_enemy_hp_override
*if (not(combat_enemy_ac_override = 999))
  *set combat_enemy_ac combat_enemy_ac_override
*comment replay-ok: the override is consumed (set back to 999) a few lines below, so a replay finds 999 and skips this block.

  *set combat_enemy_atk_bonus combat_enemy_atk_override

*comment ENEMY DEFENSE OVERRIDES (2026-10-31): applies <prefix>nonmagic_override ("immune", "resist", then any of "silvered" and "adamantine" as the exceptions) and the <prefix>resist_override, _vulnerable_override and _immune_override lists (any number of damage types) to the slot named by combat_defense_prefix
*comment ("combat_enemy_" for slot 1 before its snapshot, "combat_enemyN_" for the rest), then puts both overrides back to empty. A script because the strings are split into words. replay-ok: the overrides are emptied here, so a replay finds them empty and changes nothing.
*label apply_defense_override
*script var p = String(stats.combat_defense_prefix); var nm = String(stats[p + "nonmagic_override"] || "").split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); if (nm.length) { stats[p + "nonmagic"] = nm[0]; stats[p + "nonmagic_silvered"] = nm.indexOf("silvered") > 0; stats[p + "nonmagic_adamantine"] = nm.indexOf("adamantine") > 0; } ["resist", "vulnerable", "immune"].forEach(function (k) { var w = String(stats[p + k + "_override"] || "").split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); if (w.length) stats[p + k + "_types"] = w.join(" "); stats[p + k + "_override"] = ""; }); stats[p + "nonmagic_override"] = "";
*return

*label apply_slot_stat_override
*comment One slot's hp/ac/atk override, applied and reset, via name indirection instead of a hand-copied block

*set {(aso_p & "hp_override")} 999
*set {(aso_p & "ac_override")} 999
*set {(aso_p & "atk_override")} 999

*set {(aso_p & "creature_type_override")} ""

*label compute_cast_dc
*comment Derives <prefix>cast_dc = 8 + prof + stat modifier (unless cast_dc_override > 0) and <prefix>cast_atk_bonus = prof + stat modifier when any spell is assigned. If cast_stat is empty, picks the highest of Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma.
*params cdc_prefix cdc_stat cdc_dc_ov
*if (cdc_dc_ov > 0)
  *set {(cdc_prefix & "cast_dc")} cdc_dc_ov
*elseif ({(cdc_prefix & "cast_dc")} > 0)
  *return
*if ((({(cdc_prefix & "cast_spell_1")} = "") and ({(cdc_prefix & "cast_spell_2")} = "")) and ({(cdc_prefix & "cast_spell_3")} = ""))
  *if (cdc_dc_ov <= 0)
    *set {(cdc_prefix & "cast_dc")} 0
  *set {(cdc_prefix & "cast_atk_bonus")} 0
  *return
*temp cdc_score 0
*if (cdc_stat = "intelligence")
  *set cdc_score {(cdc_prefix & "intelligence")}
*elseif (cdc_stat = "wisdom")
  *set cdc_score {(cdc_prefix & "wisdom")}
*elseif (cdc_stat = "charisma")
  *set cdc_score {(cdc_prefix & "charisma")}
*elseif (cdc_stat = "strength")
  *set cdc_score {(cdc_prefix & "strength")}
*elseif (cdc_stat = "dexterity")
  *set cdc_score {(cdc_prefix & "dexterity")}
*elseif (cdc_stat = "constitution")
  *set cdc_score {(cdc_prefix & "constitution")}
*else
  *temp cdc_int {(cdc_prefix & "intelligence")}
  *temp cdc_wis {(cdc_prefix & "wisdom")}
  *temp cdc_cha {(cdc_prefix & "charisma")}
  *set cdc_score cdc_int
  *if (cdc_wis > cdc_score)
    *set cdc_score cdc_wis
  *if (cdc_cha > cdc_score)
    *set cdc_score cdc_cha
*gosub_scene startup score_to_mod cdc_score
*temp cdc_prof {(cdc_prefix & "prof")}
*if (cdc_prof <= 0)
  *set cdc_prof combat_lib_prof_bonus
*if (cdc_dc_ov <= 0)
  *set {(cdc_prefix & "cast_dc")} ((8 + cdc_prof) + sm_mod)
*set {(cdc_prefix & "cast_atk_bonus")} (cdc_prof + sm_mod)
*return

*label apply_enemy_spell_base
*comment Replaces an enemy slot's base attack with an at-will attack spell when assigned, moving the body's own weapon into sidearm_* (Step 3c-2).
*comment Since 3.6 step 2 (2026-11-18) it reads the data: the first assigned cantrip with enemy lines (spell_book.json combat.npc_lines) that is an attack-roll spell
*comment or a save for damage (Poison Spray: since 3.6 step 3 a save, as for the player; load_enemy_base_save sets the save on each sync). Its reach class becomes the slot's, so a caster with a
*comment long-reach cantrip keeps its distance as an archer does; a touch cantrip makes it a melee attacker. The id goes in spell_base_id for the prose and the
*comment spell picker. Damage is one die, as at character level 1 (an enemy's cantrip does not scale with the player's level).
*comment The slot's own cantrip field (cantrip, set by cantrip_override, 2026-11-20) is read first and takes none of the three cast slots, so a caster
*comment can carry three leveled spells and still have an at-will attack to fall back on. A cantrip in a cast slot still works as before.
*params aesb_slot
*temp aesb_p (("combat_enemy" & aesb_slot) & "_")
*temp aesb_id ""
*temp aesb_own {(aesb_p & "cantrip")}
*if (not(aesb_own = ""))
  *gosub_scene combat_spells spell_facts_load aesb_own
  *if (((spell_fact_slot_level = 0) and (spell_fact_npc_lines)) and ((spell_fact_resolution = "attack") or ((spell_fact_resolution = "save") and (spell_fact_dice_sides > 0))))
    *set aesb_id aesb_own
*temp aesb_k 1
*label apply_enemy_spell_base_loop
*if ((aesb_k <= 3) and (aesb_id = ""))
  *temp aesb_c {(aesb_p & ("cast_spell_" & aesb_k))}
  *if (not(aesb_c = ""))
    *gosub_scene combat_spells spell_facts_load aesb_c
    *if (((spell_fact_slot_level = 0) and (spell_fact_npc_lines)) and ((spell_fact_resolution = "attack") or ((spell_fact_resolution = "save") and (spell_fact_dice_sides > 0))))
      *set aesb_id aesb_c
  *set aesb_k + 1
  *goto apply_enemy_spell_base_loop
*set {(aesb_p & "spell_base_id")} aesb_id
*if (not(aesb_id = ""))
  *gosub_scene combat_spells spell_facts_load aesb_id
  *if (not({(aesb_p & "spell_base_applied")}))
    *set {(aesb_p & "spell_base_applied")} true
    *set {(aesb_p & "sidearm_weapon_type")} {(aesb_p & "weapon_type")}
    *set {(aesb_p & "sidearm_atk_bonus")} {(aesb_p & "atk_bonus")}
    *set {(aesb_p & "sidearm_dmg_dice_sides")} {(aesb_p & "dmg_dice_sides")}
    *set {(aesb_p & "sidearm_dmg_bonus")} {(aesb_p & "dmg_bonus")}
    *set {(aesb_p & "sidearm_dmg_type")} {(aesb_p & "dmg_type")}
    *set {(aesb_p & "sidearm_dmg_dice")} 1
    *set {(aesb_p & "sidearm_save_stat")} ""
    *set {(aesb_p & "sidearm_save_dc")} 0
    *set {(aesb_p & "sidearm_save_half")} false
  *if (spell_fact_reach = "touch")
    *set {(aesb_p & "weapon_type")} "melee"
  *else
    *set {(aesb_p & "weapon_type")} "ranged"
    *set {(aesb_p & "reach")} spell_fact_reach
  *set {(aesb_p & "dmg_dice_sides")} spell_fact_dice_sides
  *set {(aesb_p & "dmg_type")} spell_fact_dmg_type
  *set {(aesb_p & "dmg_bonus")} 0
  *set {(aesb_p & "atk_bonus")} {(aesb_p & "cast_atk_bonus")}
  *if (aesb_slot = 1)
    *set combat_enemy_sidearm_weapon_type combat_enemy1_sidearm_weapon_type
    *set combat_enemy_sidearm_atk_bonus combat_enemy1_sidearm_atk_bonus
    *set combat_enemy_sidearm_dmg_dice_sides combat_enemy1_sidearm_dmg_dice_sides
    *set combat_enemy_sidearm_dmg_bonus combat_enemy1_sidearm_dmg_bonus
    *set combat_enemy_sidearm_dmg_type combat_enemy1_sidearm_dmg_type
    *set combat_enemy_sidearm_dmg_dice 1
    *set combat_enemy_sidearm_save_stat ""
    *set combat_enemy_sidearm_save_dc 0
    *set combat_enemy_sidearm_save_half false
    *set combat_enemy_weapon_type combat_enemy1_weapon_type
    *set combat_enemy_dmg_dice_sides combat_enemy1_dmg_dice_sides
    *set combat_enemy_dmg_type combat_enemy1_dmg_type
    *set combat_enemy_dmg_bonus combat_enemy1_dmg_bonus
    *set combat_enemy_atk_bonus combat_enemy1_atk_bonus
*return

*label cri_is_better_than_best
*comment Compares initiative candidate cri_ibt_a against candidate cri_ibt_b (params: 1-indexed positions into
*comment the combat_init_* scratch arrays, startup.txt) for combat_roll_initiative's selection sort below. Output
*comment cri_ibt_result: true if A should be placed ahead of B. Tie-break order, all deterministic so it's
*comment testable: higher initiative roll, then higher raw DEX score (5e's own tiebreak), then player over ally
*comment over enemy, then ascending slot number.
*params cri_ibt_a cri_ibt_b
*temp cri_ibt_a_score {("combat_init_score_" & cri_ibt_a)}
*temp cri_ibt_b_score {("combat_init_score_" & cri_ibt_b)}
*set cri_ibt_result false
*if (cri_ibt_a_score > cri_ibt_b_score)
  *set cri_ibt_result true
*elseif (cri_ibt_a_score = cri_ibt_b_score)
  *temp cri_ibt_a_dex {("combat_init_dex_" & cri_ibt_a)}
  *temp cri_ibt_b_dex {("combat_init_dex_" & cri_ibt_b)}
  *if (cri_ibt_a_dex > cri_ibt_b_dex)
    *set cri_ibt_result true
  *elseif (cri_ibt_a_dex = cri_ibt_b_dex)
    *temp cri_ibt_a_kind {("combat_init_kind_" & cri_ibt_a)}
    *temp cri_ibt_b_kind {("combat_init_kind_" & cri_ibt_b)}
    *temp cri_ibt_a_rank 1
    *temp cri_ibt_b_rank 1
    *if (cri_ibt_a_kind = "player")
      *set cri_ibt_a_rank 3
    *elseif (cri_ibt_a_kind = "ally")
      *set cri_ibt_a_rank 2
    *if (cri_ibt_b_kind = "player")
      *set cri_ibt_b_rank 3
    *elseif (cri_ibt_b_kind = "ally")
      *set cri_ibt_b_rank 2
    *if (cri_ibt_a_rank > cri_ibt_b_rank)
      *set cri_ibt_result true
    *elseif (cri_ibt_a_rank = cri_ibt_b_rank)
      *temp cri_ibt_a_slot {("combat_init_slot_" & cri_ibt_a)}
      *temp cri_ibt_b_slot {("combat_init_slot_" & cri_ibt_b)}
      *if (cri_ibt_a_slot < cri_ibt_b_slot)
        *set cri_ibt_result true
*return

*label combat_roll_initiative
*comment Rolled ONCE per fight, from fight_setup after every slot is built -- not every round, 5e's own rule.
*comment Candidates are the player plus every POPULATED ally slot (1..combat_ally_count) and enemy slot
*comment (1..combat_enemy_count); an unpopulated slot beyond that never gets an entry, so a 1-enemy fight builds
*comment a 2-long order (player + that one enemy), not an 11-long one padded with empty slots. Builds
*comment combat_turn_kind_1..N/_slot_1..N (startup.txt), consumed by combat_next_turn below.
*comment Whole-body replay lock (2026-09-30, real bug caught live via refresh_fuzz_test.js on a fresh random seed):
*comment completely unguarded until now -- fight_setup calls this once, up front, then falls straight through
*comment combat_begin_round/announce_turn_order into combat_next_turn's own dispatch of everyone who acts before
*comment the player, all on the SAME page (no *choice pause yet). A refresh at the first REAL pause (however many
*comment turns deep that surprise round went) replays from the fight's own entry point and reaches this call
*comment again, rerolling every combatant's initiative from scratch -- a completely different turn order, which
*comment cascades into different attackers, different targets, different damage, the works. Structurally this
*comment already only runs once per fight in normal forward play (nothing else ever calls it again mid-fight), so
*comment the standard whole-body self-lock (combat_weapon_attack_locked's shape, this file) is exactly right: no
*comment per-slot index needed, and once a fight moves past its first page this lock is simply never consulted
*comment again, harmlessly. announce_turn_order (below) stays unlocked -- it only reads combat_turn_kind_N/
*comment _slot_N back and prints them, so it reprints correctly off whatever this lock preserved.
*if (not(combat_init_locked)) or (not(locked_combat_init_page_id = page_id))
  *comment OPENING DISTANCE (2026-10-02) now runs in fight_setup, before the lane is built (2026-10-16), so the sidebar strip shows an enemy at Close on the first frame.
  *temp cri_n 0
  *set cri_n + 1
  *set combat_init_kind_1 "player"
  *set combat_init_slot_1 0
  *set combat_init_dex_1 dexterity
  *temp cri_roll 1
  *rand cri_roll 1 20
  *comment Assassinate (the Assassin, rogue level 3, PHB'24; 2026-11-22): advantage on initiative, the higher of two d20s. It counts only in an ambush; any other fight opens on the player.
  *if (feature_assassinate)
    *temp cri_roll_b 1
    *rand cri_roll_b 1 20
    *if (cri_roll_b > cri_roll)
      *set cri_roll cri_roll_b
  *gosub_scene startup score_to_mod dexterity
  *set combat_init_score_1 (cri_roll + sm_mod)
  *comment Dread Ambusher's Initiative Bonus (the Gloom Stalker, ranger level 3): the Wisdom modifier too. It counts only in an ambush; any other fight opens on the player.
  *if (feature_dread_ambusher)
    *set combat_init_score_1 + wis_mod
  *comment THE PLAYER ACTS FIRST UNLESS AMBUSHED (2026-10-02, from the fight log: a level-1 wizard with 7 HP lost 8 of 11 fights, often to damage taken before the first action;
  *comment two bad initiative rolls took the whole HP pool before a turn). A fight the story marks as an ambush (combat_enemy_advantage, set by the calling scene or by the
  *comment dungeon's darkness) keeps the honest roll, so an ambush still hurts; every other fight opens on the player's turn. Allies and enemies still sort among themselves.
  *if (not(combat_enemy_advantage))
    *set combat_init_score_1 99
  *temp cri_i 1
  *label cri_ally_loop
  *if (cri_i <= combat_ally_count)
    *set cri_n + 1
    *set {("combat_init_kind_" & cri_n)} "ally"
    *set {("combat_init_slot_" & cri_n)} cri_i
    *temp cri_a_dex {(("combat_ally" & cri_i) & "_dexterity")}
    *set {("combat_init_dex_" & cri_n)} cri_a_dex
    *temp cri_a_roll 1
    *rand cri_a_roll 1 20
    *gosub_scene startup score_to_mod cri_a_dex
    *set {("combat_init_score_" & cri_n)} (cri_a_roll + sm_mod)
    *set cri_i + 1
    *goto cri_ally_loop
  *temp cri_j 1
  *label cri_enemy_loop
  *if (cri_j <= combat_enemy_count)
    *set cri_n + 1
    *set {("combat_init_kind_" & cri_n)} "enemy"
    *set {("combat_init_slot_" & cri_n)} cri_j
    *temp cri_e_dex {(("combat_enemy" & cri_j) & "_dexterity")}
    *set {("combat_init_dex_" & cri_n)} cri_e_dex
    *temp cri_e_roll 1
    *rand cri_e_roll 1 20
    *gosub_scene startup score_to_mod cri_e_dex
    *set {("combat_init_score_" & cri_n)} (cri_e_roll + sm_mod)
    *set cri_j + 1
    *goto cri_enemy_loop
  *set combat_init_count cri_n
  *comment Stale "used" flags from an earlier fight's sort must be cleared before this one's -- these are
  *comment persistent *create'd storage, not fresh *temps, so they don't reset themselves between calls.
  *temp cri_reset_i 1
  *label cri_reset_loop
  *if (cri_reset_i <= 11)
    *set {("combat_init_used_" & cri_reset_i)} false
    *set cri_reset_i + 1
    *goto cri_reset_loop
  *comment Selection sort, descending: repeatedly scan every not-yet-placed candidate for the best remaining one
  *comment (cri_is_better_than_best above), place it, mark it used. Simpler and safer than an insertion sort's
  *comment shifting step, and with at most 11 candidates the O(n^2) cost is nothing.
  *temp cri_placed 0
  *label cri_sort_loop
  *if (cri_placed < combat_init_count)
    *temp cri_best 0
    *temp cri_k 1
    *label cri_scan_loop
    *if (cri_k <= combat_init_count)
      *if (not({("combat_init_used_" & cri_k)}))
        *if (cri_best = 0)
          *set cri_best cri_k
        *else
          *gosub cri_is_better_than_best cri_k cri_best
          *if (cri_ibt_result)
            *set cri_best cri_k
      *set cri_k + 1
      *goto cri_scan_loop
    *set cri_placed + 1
    *set {("combat_turn_kind_" & cri_placed)} {("combat_init_kind_" & cri_best)}
    *set {("combat_turn_slot_" & cri_placed)} {("combat_init_slot_" & cri_best)}
    *set {("combat_init_used_" & cri_best)} true
    *goto cri_sort_loop
  *set combat_turn_count combat_init_count
  *comment Reset every position's first-pass flag true for this fight (startup.txt) -- combat_next_turn clears one
  *comment for good the instant that position dispatches, so a stale "already went" from a previous fight can never
  *comment suppress this fight's surprise round.
  *temp cri_fp_i 1
  *label cri_fp_reset_loop
  *if (cri_fp_i <= 11)
    *set {("combat_turn_first_pass_" & cri_fp_i)} true
    *set cri_fp_i + 1
    *goto cri_fp_reset_loop
  *set combat_init_locked true
  *set locked_combat_init_page_id page_id
*return

*comment RAIN (2026-11-23, BG3's rule): a fight under the open sky (combat_open_sky, set by the fight or its caller) in wet weather (weather_wet: Rain,
*comment Sleet, Storm; snow never soaks) soaks every living combatant at the start of every round, so all of them stay Wet while it lasts (2, and
*comment condition_tick takes one off at the end of each one's turn), and puts out any fire on anyone, fire patches included. Fixed-value sets, so a replayed page changes nothing.
*label combat_rain_soak
*if (not((combat_open_sky) and (weather_wet)))
  *return
*set combat_player_wet 2
*set combat_player_burning 0
*if (combat_terrain1_type = "fire")
  *set combat_terrain1_type "none"
*if (combat_terrain2_type = "fire")
  *set combat_terrain2_type "none"
*if (combat_terrain3_type = "fire")
  *set combat_terrain3_type "none"
*if (combat_terrain4_type = "fire")
  *set combat_terrain4_type "none"
*temp crs_i 1
*label combat_rain_soak_ally_loop
*if (crs_i <= combat_ally_slot_cap)
  *if ({(("combat_ally" & crs_i) & "_hp")} > 0)
    *set {(("combat_ally" & crs_i) & "_wet")} 2
    *set {(("combat_ally" & crs_i) & "_burning")} 0
  *set crs_i + 1
  *goto combat_rain_soak_ally_loop
*set crs_i 1
*label combat_rain_soak_enemy_loop
*if (crs_i <= combat_enemy_slot_cap)
  *if ({(("combat_enemy" & crs_i) & "_hp")} > 0)
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & crs_i) & "_wet")} 2
    *set {(("combat_enemy" & crs_i) & "_burning")} 0
  *set crs_i + 1
  *goto combat_rain_soak_enemy_loop
*return

*comment The opening page's one line for the rain (the state only; the Wet pips and their hover cards carry the rule).
*label combat_rain_banner
*if ((combat_open_sky) and (weather_wet))
  [b][🌧️ ${weather} | Everyone is Wet][/b]
  *line_break
*return

*label announce_turn_order
*comment One line, once, right after combat_roll_initiative builds the order -- the only player-visible
*comment confirmation the turn queue exists at all. A real name per position (bare "You" for the player, matching
*comment how the banner already says "You:" not the character's own name). Printed once because the order never
*comment changes for the rest of the fight -- a defeated combatant's turn is simply skipped when reached.
*temp ato_line ""
*temp ato_i 1
*label announce_turn_order_loop
*if (ato_i <= combat_turn_count)
  *temp ato_kind {("combat_turn_kind_" & ato_i)}
  *temp ato_slot {("combat_turn_slot_" & ato_i)}
  *temp ato_name "You"
  *if (ato_kind = "ally")
    *set ato_name {(("combat_ally" & ato_slot) & "_name")}
  *if (ato_kind = "enemy")
    *set ato_name {(("combat_enemy" & ato_slot) & "_name")}
  *if (ato_line = "")
    *set ato_line ato_name
  *else
    *set ato_line ((ato_line & ", ") & ato_name)
  *set ato_i + 1
  *goto announce_turn_order_loop
*comment A blank source line, not *line_break: this is always the very first thing combat.txt prints on a fresh
*comment entry, so the engine's paragraph buffer is always empty here -- *line_break would take its "no open
*comment paragraph" fallback (a bare <br> outside any <p>) every time, which stacks with the calling scene's own
*comment closing <p> margin into a bigger gap than a normal paragraph break. A blank line just closes whatever's
*comment open (a no-op when there's nothing to close), so the calling scene's last line and this banner get
*comment ordinary, single-size paragraph spacing either way.

[b][⚡ Turn Order: ${ato_line}][/b]
*line_break
*return

*comment BOOMING BLADE TRIGGER (2026-10-11): called by the enemy turn at each place an enemy moves of its OWN accord (a close, a dash, a fall back, a disengage, a terrain step, a chase step), right after the move
*comment and before any attack. If the enemy in combat_active_target is sheathed (combat_enemyN_booming), the flag is spent, it takes 1d8 thunder through the usual resistance and vulnerability check, and
*comment the new HP is written back to its slot. combat_boom_killed tells the caller it died, and the caller jumps to cnt_boom_tail so the dead enemy never reaches its attack. A forced move (Shove) never
*comment calls this. The flag is cleared as it is spent, so a refresh that replays the page cannot take the damage twice.
*comment replay-ok: the sheathed flag is cleared as it is spent, so a replay cannot take the damage twice (see the header).
*label fight_booming_trigger
*set combat_boom_killed false
*set combat_boom_last_text ""
*set combat_boom_last_banner ""
*comment quicktest's coverage walk reaches this label with combat_active_target still 0, and there is no combat_enemy0_booming, so the slot indirection is guarded (the same fix fight_pick_enemy_move needed).
*if (combat_active_target > 0)
  *temp fbt_name (("combat_enemy" & combat_active_target) & "_booming")
  *temp fbt_on {(fbt_name)}
  *if (fbt_on)
    *set {(fbt_name)} false
    *comment The move-damage dice by character level come from the spell data (cantrip_scale and base_move_dice in tools/spell_book.json, worked out by spell_facts_load): 1d8, 2d8 from level 5, 3d8 from 11, 4d8 from 17.
    *gosub_scene combat_spells spell_facts_load "booming_blade"
    *gosub_scene combat_spells roll_spell_dice spell_fact_move_count spell_fact_move_sides
    *set combat_player_dmg_type "thunder"
    *set combat_player_dmg_dealt spell_roll_total
    *gosub_scene combat_dice apply_outgoing_damage_resist_vuln
    *set combat_enemy_hp - combat_player_dmg_dealt
    *if (combat_enemy_hp < 0)
      *set combat_enemy_hp 0
    *gosub fight_sync_target_out
    *if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
      *set combat_boom_last_text "$!{combat_enemy_epithet} moves, and the thunder along them breaks loose in a hammering crack that finishes the work."
      *set combat_boom_last_banner "[b][⚡ Booming Blade: ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} Thunder damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix} | @{combat_is_nonlethal 🏳️|💀} ${combat_enemy_name} @{combat_is_nonlethal Beaten|Defeated}][/b]"
      *set combat_boom_killed true
    *else
      *set combat_boom_last_text "$!{combat_enemy_epithet} moves, and the thunder along them breaks loose in a hammering crack."
      *set combat_boom_last_banner "[b][⚡ Booming Blade: ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} Thunder damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}][/b]"
    *if (not(combat_save_quiet))
      ${combat_boom_last_text}
      *line_break
      ${combat_boom_last_banner}
      *line_break
*return

*comment EXTRA ATTACK (2026-10-11, for Captain Trent in Varren's Errand; an assigned ability since 3.4a): an enemy holding an extra_attack ability (tools/ability_book.json) follows its first
*comment attack at the player with more, at the same attack bonus and with no special move. Two weapons (ability_extra_attack_weapon):
*comment   "own" (Multiattack): its own weapon again. The scratch fields are re-read from the slot (fight_sync_target_in), so a special move on the first blow does not carry over, and a cornered
*comment   archer stabs with its sidearm again (fight_enemy_quickdraw), as its first blow did. Melee or ranged.
*comment   "offhand" (Trent's knife): a second weapon, the ability's die and damage type, so a captain with a long blade and a knife strikes twice a turn without doubling his damage.
*comment Only the active-target SCRATCH fields change, the same transient-override shape the ranged quick-draw uses, so the slot's own durable stats are back next turn with no restore step. Called by name
*comment after the enemy's attack on the player, once combat_abilities ability_extra_attacks has set ability_extra_attack_n, _weapon, _sides and _type.
*label fight_enemy_extra_attack
*comment Each strike runs on its OWN page: resolve_enemy_attack and fight_narrate_enemy_turn lock their dice and their text per slot per page_id, so a second call on the first attack's page would
*comment only replay the first roll. A page break gives it a fresh page_id (and a refresh replays only this page), at the cost of one click.
*temp feea_n ability_extra_attack_n
*temp feea_weapon ability_extra_attack_weapon
*temp feea_sides ability_extra_attack_sides
*temp feea_type ability_extra_attack_type
*temp feea_i 1
*label fight_enemy_extra_attack_loop
*if ((feea_i <= feea_n) and (hp_current > 1))
  *if (combat_enemy_weapon_type = "ranged")
    *page_break Another shot follows…
  *elseif (feea_i = 1)
    *page_break The second blow follows…
  *else
    *page_break Another blow follows…
  *if (feea_weapon = "own")
    *gosub fight_sync_target_in
    *if ((combat_enemy_weapon_type = "ranged") and (combat_ranged_cornered))
      *gosub fight_enemy_quickdraw
  *set combat_enemy_atk_save_stat ""
  *set combat_enemy_atk_save_dc 0
  *set combat_enemy_atk_save_half false
  *set combat_enemy_atk_dmg_dice 1
  *set combat_enemy_save_halved false
  *if (feea_weapon = "offhand")
    *set combat_enemy_dmg_dice_sides feea_sides
    *set combat_enemy_dmg_type feea_type
  *set combat_enemy_move2_chance 0
  *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_enemy_attack
  *gosub_scene combat_prose fight_narrate_enemy_turn
  *set combat_enemy_used_sidearm false
  *set feea_i + 1
  *goto fight_enemy_extra_attack_loop
*return

*comment AN ENEMY'S ATTACK-ROLL SPELL (COMBAT_ENGINE_PLAN 3.6): a leveled spell from its cast_spell_* places (Ray of Sickness, Scorching Ray), at the player. The use is spent,
*comment the spell's numbers replace the body's (load_enemy_spell_profile), and each projectile is its own attack roll on its own page, as the extra attacks are (the dice
*comment and the text lock per slot per page). It stops once the player is down to 1 HP, as they do. The body's own fields come back at the end (fight_sync_target_in).
*label fight_enemy_cast_attack
*params feca_p
*gosub_scene combat_spells spend_npc_spell_use feca_p combat_enemy_cast_place
*gosub_scene combat_spells load_enemy_spell_profile feca_p combat_enemy_cast_spell
*temp feca_n spell_fact_projectiles
*comment Magic Missile's darts are one packet (load_enemy_spell_profile), so one page.
*if ((feca_n < 1) or (combat_enemy_atk_auto))
  *set feca_n 1
*gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_enemy_attack
*gosub_scene combat_prose fight_narrate_enemy_turn
*comment DISSONANT WHISPERS' STEP PROVOKES (RAW): the foe resolve_enemy_attack picked before moving the player swings now, on its own page (each attack locks its dice
*comment per slot per page, and that foe may already have attacked on this one). Then the caster's fields come back for the rest of its turn.
*if ((combat_dw_oa_page = page_id) and (combat_dw_oa_slot > 0))
  *temp feca_caster combat_active_target
  *temp feca_oa combat_dw_oa_slot
  *page_break The whispers drive you back…
  *set combat_dw_oa_slot 0
  *comment A plain weapon swing: the cast is set aside while it resolves (its lines and numbers belong to the caster), and comes back after.
  *temp feca_spell combat_enemy_cast_spell
  *temp feca_auto combat_enemy_atk_auto
  *set combat_enemy_cast_spell ""
  *set combat_enemy_atk_auto false
  *set combat_oa_candidate feca_oa
  *gosub resolve_opportunity_attack
  *set combat_active_target feca_caster
  *gosub fight_sync_target_in
  *set combat_enemy_cast_spell feca_spell
  *set combat_enemy_atk_auto feca_auto
*temp feca_i 2
*label fight_enemy_cast_attack_loop
*if ((feca_i <= feca_n) and (hp_current > 1))
  *page_break Another ray follows…
  *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_enemy_attack
  *gosub_scene combat_prose fight_narrate_enemy_turn
  *set feca_i + 1
  *goto fight_enemy_cast_attack_loop
*gosub fight_enemy_cast_area 0
*set combat_enemy_atk_auto false
*gosub fight_sync_target_in
*return

*comment AN ENEMY'S AREA SPELL (3.6 step 5, 2026-11-19): after the main target (the player, or fcar_primary_ally when an ally took it), everyone else inside the spell's area makes
*comment their own save, as the player's own area spells hit every foe in theirs. The area is from the data (spell_fact_target): "engaged" (Burning Hands, Thunderwave, Arms of Hadar)
*comment is everyone at the caster's own position; "spot" (Shatter) is everyone at the main target's position. Each extra target is on its own page, since the attack's dice and text
*comment lock per enemy slot per page, as the extra rays do, and each rolls its own damage. The spell's numbers must still be loaded (load_enemy_spell_profile), and
*comment combat_enemy_cast_spell still set, for the lines. Riders (Thunderwave's push, Arms of Hadar's lost reaction) land on the player only.
*label fight_enemy_cast_area
*params fcar_primary_ally
*temp fcar_shape spell_fact_target
*if ((not(fcar_shape = "engaged")) and (not(fcar_shape = "spot")))
  *return
*temp fcar_caster_pos {(("combat_enemy" & combat_active_target) & "_position")}
*temp fcar_center combat_lane_player_position
*if (fcar_shape = "engaged")
  *set fcar_center fcar_caster_pos
*elseif (fcar_primary_ally > 0)

*comment ============================================================================
*comment MULTI-ENEMY TARGETING (up to 3 simultaneous enemy slots)
*comment Slot 1/2/3 (combat_enemy1_*/2_*/3_*, startup.txt) are each enemy's
*comment real, permanent stats. The canonical combat_enemy_* names (no
*comment number) are a scratch mirror of whichever slot is "active" --
*comment that's what every shared primitive in startup.txt (resolve_player_
*comment hit, resolve_enemy_attack, etc.) actually reads and writes, so they
*comment never needed to learn about multiple enemies at all. These three
*comment subroutines are the entire seam: sync the active slot in before a
*comment primitive touches it, sync it back out after, and pick which slot
*comment is active (skipping defeated ones, slot order 1/2/3).
*comment ============================================================================

*label fight_sync_target_out
*temp fsto_p (("combat_enemy" & combat_active_target) & "_")
*comment The damage since the last write-back, for an enemy's concentration save (3.9, 2026-11-18). Every blow the player lands on an enemy passes through
*comment the scratch combat_enemy_hp and back out here, so this is the one place they are all seen. Two blows written back together make one save.
*temp fsto_dmg ({(fsto_p & "hp")} - combat_enemy_hp)
*set {(fsto_p & "hp")} combat_enemy_hp

  *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_end fsto_p
*elseif (fsto_dmg > 0)
  *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_damage_check fsto_p fsto_dmg 8
*return

*comment Legacy fights use 0 Engaged, 1 Close, 2 Mid-Range, 3 Long Range; expanded6 uses 0 Engaged, 1 Close,
*comment 2 Near, 3 Mid-Range, 4 Far, 5 Long Range. A melee-only combatant closes exactly one
*comment tier per Push Forward (player) or per turn (an enemy, combat_next_turn) -- never straight to Engaged --
*comment so a fight that opens someone at Mid-Range or Long Range costs multiple turns to reach, not one. Ranged
*comment attacks otherwise work at every tier alike, which is why nothing here ever needs a ranged combatant to
*comment move. **The one exception: a ranged attack against a target at range 0 is made at disadvantage** ("Ranged
*comment Attacks in Close Combat"). It applies to a weapon, a spell, or any other means, to the ENEMY
*comment (resolve_enemy_attack, combat_dice.txt) and to the PLAYER (resolve_weapon_attack_choice below and the
*comment cantrip options, via spell_range_of) alike, and it is unconditional -- one foe or eight is the same
*comment single disadvantage. Melee attacks are never penalised for being in melee, so closing on an archer is no
*comment longer pure punishment for the player, and a ranged combatant finally has a reason to want distance.
*comment See resolve_enemy_attack's header for the full reasoning.
*label combat_size_rank
*comment Converts the five 5e size categories into an ordered value. The output is durable because callers need
*comment to compare two sizes across labels; unknown future labels safely behave as Medium until mapped here.
*params csr_size
*set combat_size_rank_value 2
*if (csr_size = "tiny")
  *set combat_size_rank_value 0
*if (csr_size = "small")
  *set combat_size_rank_value 1
*if (csr_size = "large")
  *set combat_size_rank_value 3
*if (csr_size = "huge")
  *set combat_size_rank_value 4
*if (csr_size = "gargantuan")
  *set combat_size_rank_value 5
*return

*label combat_check_shove_size
*comment 2014 5e's Shove permits a target no more than one size larger; it does not add a size modifier to the
*comment Athletics-vs-Athletics/Acrobatics contest. This project retains its existing STR/DEX defense DC but uses
*comment the RAW size gate before the roll.
*set combat_shove_size_allowed true
*set combat_shove_size_msg ""
*gosub combat_size_rank player_size
*temp ccs_player_rank combat_size_rank_value
*gosub combat_size_rank combat_enemy_size
*if (combat_size_rank_value > (ccs_player_rank + 1))
  *set combat_shove_size_allowed false
  *set combat_shove_size_msg "${combat_enemy_name} is ${combat_enemy_size}, too large for you to Shove."
*return

*label spell_range_of
*comment ============================================================================
*comment SPELL REACH TAG LOOKUP
*comment Resolves a spell's reach from check_skill, the variable every attack option already sets to the
*comment spell's display name before it rolls. Generated from tools/spell_book.json -- edit spell_book.json,
*comment never this block (node tools/gen_spells.js regenerates it).
*comment
*comment WHY resolve from check_skill: the tag answers two DIFFERENT questions, and combat.txt asks them
*comment separately. Collapsing them is a real bug -- a touch spell at Mid-Range must be UNAVAILABLE, not
*comment offered at a disadvantage.
*comment   can the caster reach the target at all?  -> spell_reach_result gates whether the option is offered
*comment   is the shot spoiled by a hostile in reach? -> spell_penalty_result sets disadvantage
*comment
*comment TAGS (three, so the first spell needing close or touch costs nothing to add later):
*comment   ranged  any tier; DISADVANTAGE while ANY living enemy is Engaged with the player (the close-combat ranged
*comment           penalty, same for a weapon, a thrown weapon, or a spell; the target itself can be far). This is the
*comment           ONLY tag that can take a penalty.
*comment   close   Close (1) or Mid-Range (2) only -- never Engaged. A melee enemy closing on the caster takes
*comment           the spell away entirely, which is the whole point. No penalty of its own: the target is
*comment           never adjacent. Reserved; no spell uses it yet.
*comment   touch   Engaged (0) only. No penalty -- a touch spell IS a melee attack, and melee attacks are never
*comment           penalised for being in melee.
*comment
*comment MELEE ATTACKS ARE NEVER PENALISED. The rule is one-directional: a ranged attack made against a
*comment creature in reach is spoiled; a melee attack made by someone in reach is not. Unarmed strikes are
*comment melee (equipment.txt's "unarmed" weapon_type), so they are never penalised either.
*comment
*comment Reads combat_enemy_range -- the ACTIVE enemy's distance to the player, kept current by
*comment fight_sync_target_in. KNOWN LIMIT: that is one distance, relative to the PLAYER. A spell that hit
*comment every enemy near a point (a cone, a burst) would need a real per-enemy distance; no spell does that
*comment yet, so nothing needs it, and a later cone spell must not be quietly given this same shortcut.
*comment Defaults are the common case (ranged, in range, no penalty) so an unlisted name -- a leveled spell
*comment not yet catalogued -- behaves like a ranged spell rather than silently disabling the option.
*comment GEN:BEGIN spell_range_lookup -- generated by tools/gen_spells.js from tools/spell_book.json; edit spell_book.json, not this block
*temp spell_range_tag "ranged"
*temp spell_in_range true
*temp spell_disadv false
*comment Read the TARGET's live distance, not the shared combat_enemy_range scratch. fight_round_hub calls
*comment fight_pick_target (setting combat_active_target) but not fight_sync_target_in, so that scratch is
*comment STALE at menu-build time -- a fight opened at Long Range still read 0 on its first page. The option
*comment bodies below call fight_pick_target first, so combat_active_target is the slot this cast will hit;
*comment combat_target_range is that slot's own range, computed fresh in the hub (see the block above the
*comment *choice). The scratch is only the fallback for a call made before the hub has run.
*temp spell_range_at combat_enemy_range
*if (combat_target_range >= 0)
  *set spell_range_at combat_target_range
*if (check_skill = "Fire Bolt")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Ray of Frost")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Eldritch Blast")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Chill Touch")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Vicious Mockery")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Poison Spray")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Shocking Grasp")
  *set spell_range_tag "touch"
*if (check_skill = "Ray of Sickness")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Cause Fear")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Command")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Bane")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Dissonant Whispers")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Grease")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Hex")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Hunter's Mark")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Magic Missile")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Blindness")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Hold Person")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Web")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Shatter")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Scorching Ray")
  *set spell_range_tag "ranged"
*if (check_skill = "Ray of Frost")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Chill Touch")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Vicious Mockery")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Poison Spray")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Ray of Sickness")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Cause Fear")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Command")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Bane")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Dissonant Whispers")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Grease")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Hex")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)

  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Hold Person")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Web")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (check_skill = "Shatter")
  *if (spell_range_at > combat_reach_medium)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (spell_range_tag = "close")
  *if (spell_range_at <= 0)
    *set spell_in_range false
*if (spell_range_tag = "touch")
  *if (spell_range_at > 0)

  *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_check_engaged
  *if (combat_any_engaged)

*label combat_hidden_attack_roll
*comment Called immediately before every player attack roll (the weapon roll and each spell attack's roll). A hidden player
*comment attacks with advantage, and making the attack gives the position away whether it hits or misses, so the flag is
*comment spent here, before the roll, not after it. That is the only place advantage is applied, so a new attack option
*comment adds this one line and nothing else. Advantage still cancels against any disadvantage already set (reach, etc.),
*comment same as everywhere else. Sneak Attack reads roll_had_advantage, so it fires on its own from here.
*comment Advantage from the target itself (a prone enemy after grease, 2026-10-12) comes from combat_attack_edge in combat_dice.txt, the one rule every attacker, ally attacks included, asks.
*temp cha_tag combat_attack_tag
*set combat_attack_tag ""
*comment The enemy this roll is aimed at, for its Shield and Blade Ward (roll_d20_check, 3.9a 4c/4d).
*set combat_atk_vs_enemy combat_active_target
*gosub_scene combat_dice condition_has "combat_player_" "blinded"
*if (condition_result)
  *set disadvantage true
  *set combat_atk_blinded true
*comment Restrained (3.4): the player's own attacks have disadvantage.
*if (combat_player_restrained > 0)
  *set disadvantage true
  *set combat_atk_restrained true
*comment Frightened (3.6 step 4): so do they while the source of the fear is alive and in sight.
*gosub_scene combat_dice player_fear_source
*if (combat_player_fear_slot > 0)
  *set disadvantage true
  *set combat_atk_frightened true
*comment Poisoned (3.3, a special move or Ray of Sickness): disadvantage on the player's attack rolls.
*if (combat_player_poisoned > 0)
  *set disadvantage true
  *set combat_atk_poisoned true
*comment Bane (an enemy's, 3.6 step 4): 1d4 off the attack roll, rolled in dice_engine's lock.
*set combat_atk_baned ((combat_player_bane_expires_round > 0) and (combat_player_bane_expires_round >= combat_round))
*gosub_scene combat_dice combat_attack_edge combat_enemy_range combat_active_target cha_tag
*if (combat_edge_adv)
  *set advantage true
  *if (not(disadvantage))
    [b][🎯 ${combat_enemy_name}: ${combat_edge_label}. Your attack has advantage.][/b]
    *line_break
*comment ASSASSINATE (the Assassin, rogue level 3, PHB'24; 2026-11-22): in the first round, advantage against a foe that has not taken its turn yet.
*if ((feature_assassinate) and (combat_round = 1))
  *gosub assassinate_unacted combat_active_target
  *if (combat_assassinate_open)
    *set advantage true
    *if (not(disadvantage))
      [b][🗡️ Assassinate: ${combat_enemy_name} has not acted yet. Your attack has advantage.][/b]
      *line_break
*comment STEADY AIM (rogue level 3, PHB'24; 2026-11-22): the steadied attack rolls with advantage, and the aim is spent on it, hit or miss.
*if (combat_steady_armed)
  *set combat_steady_armed false
  *set advantage true
  [b][🎯 Your steadied aim gives this attack advantage.][/b]
  *line_break
*if (combat_player_hidden)
  *set combat_player_hidden false
  *set advantage true
  *comment The hidden attack is one USE: every use raises the next Hide's DC by 2, and Hide is not offered again until a turn has gone by (combat_hide_free_round), so a rogue cannot hide and strike every single turn.
  *set combat_hide_uses + 1
  *set combat_hide_free_round (combat_round + 2)
  [b][👤 You strike from hiding, and your cover breaks.][/b]
  *line_break
*return

*label assassinate_unacted
*comment Assassinate's test: has the enemy in slot aun_slot taken a turn yet this fight? Only asked in round 1, where the turn order positions before the
*comment current one (combat_current_turn_index) are the turns already taken. In a fight the player opens, that is none. Writes combat_assassinate_open.
*params aun_slot
*set combat_assassinate_open true
*temp aun_i 1
*temp aun_kind ""
*temp aun_s 0
*label assassinate_unacted_loop
*if (aun_i < combat_current_turn_index)
  *set aun_kind {("combat_turn_kind_" & aun_i)}
  *set aun_s {("combat_turn_slot_" & aun_i)}
  *if ((aun_kind = "enemy") and (aun_s = aun_slot))
    *set combat_assassinate_open false
  *set aun_i + 1
  *goto assassinate_unacted_loop
*return

*label combat_hide_dc_calc
*comment HIDE'S DC (2026-10-12, the user's call): no longer a flat 15. It is 10 plus the Wisdom modifier of the sharpest-eyed living enemy (their passive Perception), minus 2 when the nearest enemy is all the way out at Long Range, and, for the Hide action itself
*comment (chd_escalate true), plus 2 for every hidden attack already made this fight (combat_hide_uses), because the enemy is watching for it. The check to STAY hidden against a searching enemy uses the same number without the repeat penalty. Never below 8. Written to combat_hide_dc_value.
*params chd_escalate
*temp chd_best (0 - 5)
*temp chd_near combat_max_range
*temp chd_i 1
*label combat_hide_dc_calc_loop
*if (chd_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *temp chd_hp {(("combat_enemy" & chd_i) & "_hp")}
  *if (chd_hp > 0)
    *temp chd_wis {(("combat_enemy" & chd_i) & "_wisdom")}
    *gosub_scene startup score_to_mod chd_wis
    *comment Hex on its Wisdom: its passive Perception takes -5.
    *gosub hex_check_disadv chd_i "wisdom"
    *if (combat_hex_check_dis)
      *set sm_mod - 5
    *if (sm_mod > chd_best)
      *set chd_best sm_mod
    *temp chd_range {(("combat_enemy" & chd_i) & "_range")}
    *if (chd_range < chd_near)
      *set chd_near chd_range
  *set chd_i + 1
  *goto combat_hide_dc_calc_loop
*set combat_hide_dc_value (10 + chd_best)
*if (chd_near >= combat_max_range)
  *set combat_hide_dc_value (combat_hide_dc_value - 2)
*if (chd_escalate)
  *set combat_hide_dc_value (combat_hide_dc_value + (combat_hide_uses * 2))
*if (combat_hide_dc_value < 8)
  *set combat_hide_dc_value 8
*return

*label combat_check_hide
*comment Hide needs open ground between you and every living enemy (2026-10-01): no enemy at Close (1) or Engaged (0).
*comment Reads each slot's own range, not the shared combat_enemy_range scratch, which only follows one slot.
*set combat_hide_blocked false
*temp cch_i 1
*label combat_check_hide_loop
*if (cch_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *temp cch_hp {(("combat_enemy" & cch_i) & "_hp")}
  *temp cch_range {(("combat_enemy" & cch_i) & "_range")}
  *if ((cch_hp > 0) and (cch_range <= 1))
    *set combat_hide_blocked true
  *set cch_i + 1
  *goto combat_check_hide_loop
*return

*label combat_ally_hurt_check
*comment Sets combat_ally_alive_exists (any living ally), combat_ally_hurt_exists (a living ally below its maximum) and combat_ally_hurt_first (the first such slot, 0 for none).
*set combat_ally_alive_exists false
*set combat_ally_hurt_exists false
*set combat_ally_hurt_first 0
*temp cah_i 1
*label combat_ally_hurt_check_loop
*if (cah_i <= combat_ally_slot_cap)
  *temp cah_hp {(("combat_ally" & cah_i) & "_hp")}
  *temp cah_max {(("combat_ally" & cah_i) & "_max_hp")}
  *if (cah_hp > 0)
    *set combat_ally_alive_exists true
    *if (cah_hp < cah_max)
      *set combat_ally_hurt_exists true
      *if (combat_ally_hurt_first = 0)
        *set combat_ally_hurt_first cah_i
  *set cah_i + 1
  *goto combat_ally_hurt_check_loop
*return

*label combat_friendly_validate
*comment Checks the friendly target the player picked in the sidebar: it must be alive, wounded, and within combat_friendly_reach of the player. Sets combat_friendly_ok, combat_friendly_why
*comment (the reason it is not) and combat_friendly_name (who it is, for the cast text).
*set combat_friendly_ok false
*set combat_friendly_why ""
*set combat_friendly_name "you"
*gosub_scene combat_movement combat_refresh_ally_distances
*if (combat_friendly_target = 0)
  *if (hp_current >= hp_max)
    *set combat_friendly_why "You are not wounded."
  *else
    *set combat_friendly_ok true
*else
  *temp cfv_hp {(("combat_ally" & combat_friendly_target) & "_hp")}
  *temp cfv_max {(("combat_ally" & combat_friendly_target) & "_max_hp")}
  *temp cfv_dist {(("combat_ally" & combat_friendly_target) & "_distance")}
  *set combat_friendly_name {(("combat_ally" & combat_friendly_target) & "_name")}
  *if (cfv_hp <= 0)
    *set combat_friendly_why "${combat_friendly_name} is down, and this will not reach them."
  *elseif (cfv_hp >= cfv_max)
    *set combat_friendly_why "${combat_friendly_name} is not wounded."
  *elseif (cfv_dist > combat_friendly_reach)
    *set combat_friendly_why "${combat_friendly_name} is too far away."
  *else
    *set combat_friendly_ok true
*return

*label combat_inspire_pick
*comment Bardic Inspiration: the first living ally, or 0 when the Bard stands alone (then the die goes to the Bard).
*set combat_inspire_ally_pick 0
*temp cip_i 1
*label combat_inspire_pick_loop
*if (cip_i <= combat_ally_slot_cap)
  *temp cip_hp {(("combat_ally" & cip_i) & "_hp")}
  *if ((cip_hp > 0) and (combat_inspire_ally_pick = 0))
    *set combat_inspire_ally_pick cip_i
  *set cip_i + 1
  *goto combat_inspire_pick_loop
*return

*label combat_hadar_check
*comment Arms of Hadar (Warlock): sets combat_hadar_ok when a living enemy is Engaged (range 0), i.e. within the 10 foot emanation.
*set combat_hadar_ok false
*temp chk_i 1
*label combat_hadar_check_loop

  *temp chk_hp {(("combat_enemy" & chk_i) & "_hp")}
  *temp chk_range {(("combat_enemy" & chk_i) & "_range")}
  *if ((chk_hp > 0) and (chk_range <= 0))
    *set combat_hadar_ok true
  *set chk_i + 1
  *goto combat_hadar_check_loop
*return

*label combat_eval_spell_why
*params cesw_cost cesw_slot_why cesw_sight_need cesw_spec_why cesw_reach_ok
*comment Shared spell-availability reason evaluator (Step 7b): writes combat_why_res in priority order
*comment (armor > no slot > cannot see > spell-specific > out of reach > budget spent). Empty string means castable now.
*set combat_why_res ""
*if (spells_blocked)
  *set combat_why_res "armor"
  *return
*if (cesw_slot_why != "")
  *set combat_why_res cesw_slot_why
  *return
*if ((cesw_sight_need) and (not(combat_player_can_see)))
  *set combat_why_res "cannot_see"
  *return
*if (cesw_spec_why != "")
  *set combat_why_res cesw_spec_why
  *return
*if (not(cesw_reach_ok))
  *set combat_why_res "out_of_reach"
  *return
*if ((cesw_cost = "action") and (combat_player_actions_left <= 0))
  *set combat_why_res "no_action"
  *return
*if ((cesw_cost = "bonus") and (combat_player_bonus_left <= 0))
  *set combat_why_res "no_bonus"
  *return
*if ((cesw_cost = "reaction") and (player_oa_used))
  *set combat_why_res "no_reaction"
  *return
*return

*comment Ownership probe for cantrips (mirrors character_progression.txt refresh_known_spells).

  *set combat_has_cantrip_ok true
  *return

  *set combat_has_cantrip_ok true
  *return

  *return

  *return
