*label combat_dual_shot_check
*comment Dual Shot (2026-10-02): sets combat_dual_pair_ok when two living enemies stand at the same range tier. The engine has no distance between two
*comment particular creatures, so "within 10 feet of the first target" is approximated as "the same range tier", the same shared-bucket idea the interception
*comment and flanking checks use.
*set combat_dual_pair_ok false
*temp cdc_i 1
*label combat_dual_shot_check_i
*if (cdc_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *temp cdc_ihp {(("combat_enemy" & cdc_i) & "_hp")}
  *temp cdc_irange {(("combat_enemy" & cdc_i) & "_range")}
  *if (cdc_ihp > 0)
    *temp cdc_j (cdc_i + 1)
    *label combat_dual_shot_check_j
    *if (cdc_j <= combat_enemy_count)
      *temp cdc_jhp {(("combat_enemy" & cdc_j) & "_hp")}
      *temp cdc_jrange {(("combat_enemy" & cdc_j) & "_range")}
      *if ((cdc_jhp > 0) and (cdc_jrange = cdc_irange))
        *set combat_dual_pair_ok true
      *set cdc_j + 1
      *goto combat_dual_shot_check_j
  *set cdc_i + 1
  *goto combat_dual_shot_check_i
*return

*label combat_dual_shot_validate
*comment Reads what the player assigned in the sidebar (combat_mm_darts_N, max 2). Valid means exactly two different living enemies with one shot each, at the same
*comment range tier. Sets combat_dual_a/_b to those slots, or combat_dual_why to the reason it is not.
*set combat_dual_valid false
*set combat_dual_a 0
*set combat_dual_b 0
*set combat_dual_why ""
*gosub combat_mm_recount_darts
*temp cdv_i 1
*temp cdv_slots 0
*temp cdv_over false
*label combat_dual_shot_validate_loop
*if (cdv_i <= combat_enemy_slot_cap)
  *temp cdv_n {(("combat_mm_darts_" & cdv_i))}
  *if (cdv_n > 0)
    *set cdv_slots + 1
    *if (cdv_n > 1)
      *set cdv_over true
    *if (combat_dual_a = 0)
      *set combat_dual_a cdv_i
    *else
      *set combat_dual_b cdv_i
  *set cdv_i + 1
  *goto combat_dual_shot_validate_loop
*if (combat_mm_darts_total < 2)
  *set combat_dual_why "Place both shots first: click an enemy in the sidebar for each."
*elseif (cdv_over)
  *set combat_dual_why "The two shots must go to two different creatures."
*else
  *temp cdv_arange {(("combat_enemy" & combat_dual_a) & "_range")}
  *temp cdv_brange {(("combat_enemy" & combat_dual_b) & "_range")}
  *if (not(cdv_arange = cdv_brange))
    *set combat_dual_why "The two targets must be at the same range, close enough together to bracket with one draw."
  *else
    *set combat_dual_valid true
*return

*label combat_hm_state
*comment Hunter's Mark state, refreshed every time the hub opens (see startup.txt's *create comment). Live: the mark is unexpired and its
*comment creature still stands. Orphaned: unexpired, but that creature has dropped, so the mark can be moved with a free bonus action.
*set combat_hm_live false
*set combat_hm_orphaned false
*set combat_hm_name ""
*if (character_class = "ranger")
  *if ((favored_foe_target_slot > 0) and (favored_foe_expires_round >= combat_round))
    *temp chs_hp {(("combat_enemy" & favored_foe_target_slot) & "_hp")}
    *if (chs_hp > 0)
      *set combat_hm_live true
      *set combat_hm_name {(("combat_enemy" & favored_foe_target_slot) & "_name")}
    *else
      *set combat_hm_orphaned true
*return

*comment HEX'S CHECK PENALTY (RAW): does Hex give enemy slot hcd_slot disadvantage on a check with hcd_ability? Into combat_hex_check_dis. A rolled check
*comment rolls twice and keeps the lower; a fixed DC built from its score (a passive check: Shove, Hide, Intimidate) takes -5, the passive rule.
*label hex_check_disadv
*params hcd_slot hcd_ability
*set combat_hex_check_dis false
*if (((hex_active) and (hex_target_slot = hcd_slot)) and (hex_ability = hcd_ability))
  *if ({(("combat_enemy" & hcd_slot) & "_hp")} > 0)
    *set combat_hex_check_dis true
*return

*label combat_hex_state
*comment Hex parallels Hunter's Mark's target bookkeeping, but applies to every kind of attack roll rather than weapon hits alone.
*set combat_hex_live false
*set combat_hex_orphaned false
*set combat_hex_name ""
*if ((hex_active) and (hex_target_slot > 0))
  *temp chx_hp {(("combat_enemy" & hex_target_slot) & "_hp")}
  *if (chx_hp > 0)
    *set combat_hex_live true
    *set combat_hex_name {(("combat_enemy" & hex_target_slot) & "_name")}
  *else
    *set combat_hex_orphaned true
*return

*label find_opportunity_attacker
*comment Opportunity attacks (2026-09-28). Sets combat_oa_candidate to the enemy that gets to swing as the
*comment player leaves, or 0 for none. Must be called BEFORE the move that opens the tier, while every enemy
*comment is still at its pre-move range -- checked afterwards, range 1 cannot tell "just left melee" apart
*comment from "was never in melee", and both look identical.
*comment
*comment THE CAP IS THE WHOLE POINT. combat_shift_all_ranges moves EVERY enemy at once, so a Fall Back from three
*comment engaged enemies opens a tier on all three and RAW would fire three separate opportunity attacks. The
*comment engine has no player reaction budget at all (see the turn-budget comment: "REACTION: not a budget
*comment yet"), so that is roughly eighteen damage for one retreat step and an unwinnable spiral. **One OA per
*comment move, maximum**, which is also the most defensible reading -- you only have to get past one of them.
*comment This is the same decision as the per-round cap on combat_enemyN_oa_used, and the two together mean a
*comment single enemy can hurt you at most once per round no matter how much you move around it.
*comment
*comment CANDIDATE RULES, all required. Able to react at all (combat_dice.txt combat_can_react, 2026-11-03: living, its reaction for this round still
*comment spare, and not stunned -- a stunned enemy takes no swing until its stun has run out); melee weapon_type (a 5e opportunity attack is a MELEE
*comment attack, and a ranged enemy already at melee suffers the close-range disadvantage penalty instead, so
*comment giving it a parting shot too would double-punish the same approach); range 0, i.e. actually in reach of
*comment the player, which is the entire trigger; and oa_used false, its reaction for this round still spare.
*comment Lowest HP wins, mirroring find_intercepting_ally's tiebreak for consistency -- finish off whoever is
*comment nearly down rather than whoever stands nearest, and ties fall to the lowest slot via a strict <.
*comment
*comment Suppressed by combat_player_disengaged at the CALL SITE, not here, so the two departure options (Fall
*comment Back and Break off) cannot drift apart on when the suppression applies.
*set combat_oa_candidate 0
*temp foa_best_hp 0
*temp foa_i 1
*label find_opportunity_attacker_loop
*if (foa_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *temp foa_hp {(("combat_enemy" & foa_i) & "_hp")}
  *temp foa_weapon {(("combat_enemy" & foa_i) & "_weapon_type")}
  *temp foa_range {(("combat_enemy" & foa_i) & "_range")}
  *gosub_scene combat_dice combat_can_react "enemy" foa_i
  *if (((combat_can_react_ok) and ((foa_weapon = "melee") or (foa_weapon = "reach"))) and (foa_range <= 0))
    *if ((combat_oa_candidate = 0) or (foa_hp < foa_best_hp))
      *set combat_oa_candidate foa_i
      *set foa_best_hp foa_hp
  *set foa_i + 1
  *goto find_opportunity_attacker_loop
*return

*label resolve_opportunity_attack
*comment Resolves the swing find_opportunity_attacker picked, called AFTER the tier has actually opened and only
*comment when combat_oa_candidate is non-zero.
*comment
*comment **Deliberately reuses resolve_enemy_attack and fight_narrate_enemy_turn whole, with no parallel path.**
*comment That is what makes an opportunity attack behave like every other hit the player takes, for free: Shield
*comment can still break through it, Armor of Agathys still bites back, resistance,
*comment vulnerability, temp HP, conditions and stun all still apply, and the death and rescue checks still run.
*comment Writing a "simpler" OA resolver would have quietly forked all of that. fight_narrate_enemy_turn is
*comment equally safe to reuse: it narrates "this enemy attacks you" and nothing about closing distance, which
*comment lives in combat_next_turn's own branch instead.
*comment
*comment Deliberately does NOT call fight_pick_enemy_move, so no move-2 proc -- a reaction is a basic attack.
*comment
*comment Clears combat_oa_candidate and spends the enemy's oa_used BEFORE rolling, so a second departure later on
*comment the same page can never reach the roll at all. That also keeps it clear of the replay lock, which would
*comment otherwise find combat_enemy_atk_locked_<slot> already set from a first swing this page.
*temp roa_candidate combat_oa_candidate
*set combat_oa_candidate 0
*if (roa_candidate > 0)
  *set {(("combat_enemy" & roa_candidate) & "_oa_used")} true
  *set combat_active_target roa_candidate
  *gosub fight_sync_target_in
  *comment An opportunity attack is a reaction to movement, so it lands before the player has done anything else
  *comment on this page -- including picking a new target. The sync above is what makes that safe.
  *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_enemy_attack
  *gosub_scene combat_prose fight_narrate_enemy_turn "enemy" roa_candidate
*return

*label resolve_opportunity_attack_on_ally
*comment Ally-side rows 3-4 mirror (2026-09-29, on request). Resolves the swing find_opportunity_attacker picked,
*comment targeting a RETREATING ALLY instead of the player. The finder's own eligibility rules (living, melee
*comment weapon_type, range 0, oa_used unspent) don't depend on who's leaving, only on which enemy has a reaction
*comment spare -- so unlike the reverse-OA pair below (which needed a genuinely new finder, find_opportunity_
*comment attacker_against, because ITS eligibility rules differ by reactor kind), find_opportunity_attacker itself
*comment is reused completely unchanged. Only the resolver differs, calling resolve_enemy_attack_on_ally/
*comment fight_narrate_ally_attack instead of resolve_opportunity_attack's player-target versions -- same shape as
*comment the ordinary interception call site (combat_next_turn), which this is really just a delayed reaction
*comment version of.
*params roaa_ally_slot
*temp roaa_candidate combat_oa_candidate
*set combat_oa_candidate 0
*if (roaa_candidate > 0)
  *set {(("combat_enemy" & roaa_candidate) & "_oa_used")} true
  *set combat_active_target roaa_candidate
  *gosub fight_sync_target_in
  *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_enemy_attack_on_ally roaa_ally_slot
  *gosub_scene combat_prose fight_narrate_ally_attack "enemy" roaa_candidate "ally" roaa_ally_slot combat_enemy_atk_on_ally_hit
*return

*comment ============================================================================
*comment COMBAT: REVERSE OPPORTUNITY ATTACKS (2026-09-29, Ranged-AI Position Awareness plan §4.5, Phase 5)
*comment The reaction asymmetry closed: an enemy that Retreats can now be punished, the same way the player
*comment already can be by find_opportunity_attacker/resolve_opportunity_attack above. Two-part split (finder,
*comment then one resolver per reactor type) mirrors that pair's own shape exactly.
*comment ============================================================================
*label find_opportunity_attacker_against
*comment Sets combat_oa_against_kind ("player"/"ally"/"") and combat_oa_against_slot (0 for player, else ally
*comment slot) -- who punishes the enemy CURRENTLY dispatching as it opens the tier to retreat. Must be called
*comment BEFORE that enemy's own range changes, while combat_enemy_range/_ac are still this specific enemy's
*comment pre-move values (already synced by fight_sync_target_in earlier this turn, same precondition
*comment find_intercepting_ally and the quick-draw/chase logic already rely on).
*comment
*comment PLAYER FIRST, mirroring find_opportunity_attacker's own candidate rules in reverse: a melee or finesse
*comment weapon (RAW -- an opportunity attack is a melee attack; a bow-armed player simply never qualifies),
*comment combat_enemy_range <= 0 (this enemy is actually in the player's reach), and not(player_oa_used). Falls
*comment back to the lowest-HP eligible melee ally (same tiebreak as everywhere else in this file) only if the
*comment player itself isn't eligible -- "whoever's pool is left," per the plan's own framing, not a competition
*comment between them.
*comment Who may react at all is combat_dice.txt combat_can_react (2026-11-03): a stunned player, or ally, has no reactions, RAW, so the chance passes on.
*set combat_oa_against_kind ""
*set combat_oa_against_slot 0
*gosub_scene combat_dice combat_can_react "player" 0
*if (((((weapon_type = "melee") or (weapon_type = "finesse")) or (weapon_type = "reach")) and (combat_enemy_range <= 0)) and (combat_can_react_ok))
  *set combat_oa_against_kind "player"
*else
  *temp foaa_best_hp 0
  *temp foaa_i 1
  *label find_opportunity_attacker_against_loop
  *if (foaa_i <= combat_ally_slot_cap)
    *temp foaa_hp {(("combat_ally" & foaa_i) & "_hp")}
    *temp foaa_weapon {(("combat_ally" & foaa_i) & "_weapon_type")}
    *temp foaa_range {(("combat_ally" & foaa_i) & "_range")}
    *gosub_scene combat_dice combat_can_react "ally" foaa_i
    *if (((combat_can_react_ok) and ((foaa_weapon = "melee") or (foaa_weapon = "reach"))) and (foaa_range <= 0))
      *if ((combat_oa_against_slot = 0) or (foaa_hp < foaa_best_hp))
        *set combat_oa_against_slot foaa_i
        *set foaa_best_hp foaa_hp
    *set foaa_i + 1
    *goto find_opportunity_attacker_against_loop
  *if (combat_oa_against_slot > 0)
    *set combat_oa_against_kind "ally"
*return

*comment A MAGIC WEAPON'S ATTACK BONUS (the user, 2026-10-31, RAW): a +N weapon adds N to every attack roll made with it, on top of the usual modifiers, and the roll says so. Called right before a weapon attack roll, after any terrain label has been
*comment set (it adds to that label rather than replacing it). The matching +N on the damage is added where each weapon hit builds combat_player_dmg_bonus. weapon_* is whichever weapon is in hand now, a swapped sidearm included.
*comment replay-ok: called only from inside the weapon attack's own lock (combat_weapon_attack_locked) and from the opportunity attack's turn, once per attack, and check_flat_bonus is cleared by the roll that follows.
*label combat_apply_weapon_magic_attack
*if (weapon_magic > 0)
  *set check_flat_bonus + weapon_magic
  *set check_flat_bonus_label "${check_flat_bonus_label} +${weapon_magic} (magic weapon)"
*if (weapon_flimsy)
  *set check_flat_bonus - 1
  *set check_flat_bonus_label "${check_flat_bonus_label} -1 (flimsy weapon)"
*comment WEAPON PROFICIENCY (2026-11-24, PHB'24): a martial weapon the class is not trained in rolls without the proficiency bonus (equipment.txt weapon_prof_check).
*gosub_scene equipment weapon_prof_check weapon weapon_type
*if (not(wpc_ok))
  *set check_add_prof_bonus false
  *set check_flat_bonus_label "${check_flat_bonus_label} (not proficient)"
*comment HEAVY (2026-11-25, PHB'24): a Heavy weapon's attacks have disadvantage below STR 13, or DEX 13 for a ranged one; the scores already carry any boon. combat_atk_heavy names it on the roll.
*if (weapon_heavy)
  *if (((weapon_type = "ranged") and (dexterity < 13)) or ((not(weapon_type = "ranged")) and (strength < 13)))
    *set disadvantage true
    *set combat_atk_heavy true
*return

*label resolve_reverse_oa_player
*comment The player's own opportunity attack against the enemy currently retreating (combat_active_target is
*comment already that enemy -- fight_sync_target_in already ran for this slot this turn, so combat_enemy_ac etc.
*comment are already correct). No close-range disadvantage check: an opportunity attack is inherently melee, the
*comment penalty never applies regardless of the player's own primary weapon_type.
*comment
*comment Cannot reuse resolve_weapon_attack_choice for this -- it always *goto's away at the end (spending the
*comment player's own attack budget via fight_check_enemy_hp/fight_attack_spent), correct only for the player's
*comment own turn, never a mid-enemy-turn reaction. Attack roll and the finesse/two-handed/Dueling/Rage damage
*comment math are replicated here instead (mirroring that label's own melee branch exactly); the actual roll,
*comment resistance/vulnerability and HP application reuse resolve_player_weapon_hit whole, so Sneak Attack and
*comment Hex and Hunter's Mark still apply for free -- both already live inside that label, not duplicated here.
*comment
*comment Deliberately simpler than the player's own turn for flavor: a short, dedicated line rather than
*comment reusing pick_weapon_flavor's full per-weapon-type variant pools, which are only reachable through
*comment resolve_weapon_attack_choice's own setup. Caller checks for a kill (combat_enemy_hp <= 0) afterward,
*comment same convention as every other attack resolution call site in this file.
*if (weapon_type = "finesse")
  *if (str_mod > dex_mod)
    *set check_stat "str"
  *else
    *set check_stat "dex"
*else
  *set check_stat "str"
*comment Riposte (combat_dice.txt player_riposte_check) runs this same counter under its own name, with its own lines below.
*set check_skill combat_reaction_skill
*set check_dc combat_enemy_ac
*set check_add_prof_bonus true
*gosub combat_apply_weapon_magic_attack
*gosub_scene combat_dice combat_attack_edge 0 combat_active_target ""
*if (combat_edge_adv)
  *set advantage true
*gosub_scene combat_dice condition_has "combat_player_" "blinded"
*if (condition_result)
  *set disadvantage true
  *set combat_atk_blinded true
*comment Restrained (3.4): the player's own attacks have disadvantage.
*if (combat_player_restrained > 0)
  *set disadvantage true
  *set combat_atk_restrained true
*comment Frightened (3.6 step 4): so do they while the source of the fear is alive and in sight.
*gosub_scene combat_dice player_fear_source
*if (combat_player_fear_slot > 0)
  *set disadvantage true
  *set combat_atk_frightened true
*comment Poisoned (3.3, a special move or Ray of Sickness): disadvantage on the player's attack rolls.
*if (combat_player_poisoned > 0)
  *set disadvantage true
  *set combat_atk_poisoned true
*comment Bane (an enemy's, 3.6 step 4): 1d4 off the attack roll, rolled in dice_engine's lock.
*set combat_atk_baned ((combat_player_bane_expires_round > 0) and (combat_player_bane_expires_round >= combat_round))
*if (combat_save_quiet)
  *set check_silent true
*gosub_scene dice_engine roll_d20_check
*set check_flat_bonus 0
*set combat_roa_last_roll_banner check_last_banner
*set combat_roa_last_dmg_roll_banner ""
*if (check_success)
  *set combat_player_dmg_type weapon_damage_type
  *comment The weapon's own die (combat_dice.txt weapon_die_sides), never its category's.
  *gosub_scene combat_dice weapon_die_sides
  *if (weapon_type = "finesse")
    *if (str_mod > dex_mod)
      *set combat_player_dmg_bonus str_mod
    *else
      *set combat_player_dmg_bonus dex_mod
  *else
    *set combat_player_dmg_bonus str_mod
  *if (fighting_style = "dueling")
    *if (not(weapon_hands = "two_handed"))
      *set combat_player_dmg_bonus + 2
  *if ((character_class = "barbarian") and (is_raging))
    *if (weapon_type = "finesse")
      *if (str_mod > dex_mod)
        *set combat_player_dmg_bonus + barbarian_rage_damage_bonus
    *else
      *set combat_player_dmg_bonus + barbarian_rage_damage_bonus
  *set combat_player_dmg_bonus + weapon_magic
  *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_player_weapon_hit
  *set combat_roa_last_dmg_roll_banner combat_player_dmg_roll_banner
  *if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
    *comment A non-lethal foe yields here, as every finishing blow does in a non-lethal fight.
    *if (combat_reaction_skill = "Riposte")
      *if (combat_is_nonlethal)
        *set combat_roa_last_line "The miss leaves ${combat_enemy_epithet} open, and your answer takes the fight out of them. They stumble back and show you empty hands."
      *else
        *set combat_roa_last_line "The miss leaves ${combat_enemy_epithet} open, and your answer drops them where they stand."
    *elseif (combat_is_nonlethal)
      *set combat_roa_last_line "You catch ${combat_enemy_epithet} pulling back, and the blow takes the fight out of them. They stumble to a halt and show you empty hands."
    *else
      *set combat_roa_last_line "You catch ${combat_enemy_epithet} pulling back, and the blow drops them mid-retreat."
    *set combat_roa_last_banner "[b][@{combat_is_nonlethal 🏳️|💀} ${combat_enemy_name} @{combat_is_nonlethal Beaten|Defeated}: ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}][/b]"
  *elseif (combat_weapon_blocked)
    *if (combat_reaction_skill = "Riposte")
      *set combat_roa_last_line "You answer the miss with a clean blow of your ${weapon}, but the hit glances away as though you had struck stone."
    *else
      *set combat_roa_last_line "You catch ${combat_enemy_epithet} pulling back and land a clean blow with your ${weapon}, but the hit glances away as though you had struck stone."
    *set combat_roa_last_banner "[b][🚫 ${combat_enemy_name} is immune to ${combat_weapon_defense_text} | ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} damage][/b]"
  *elseif (combat_reaction_skill = "Riposte")
    *set combat_roa_last_line "The miss leaves ${combat_enemy_epithet} open, and you answer before they can recover."
    *set combat_roa_last_banner "[b][🩸 Riposte Hits: ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}][/b]"
  *else
    *set combat_roa_last_line "You catch ${combat_enemy_epithet} pulling back, and the opening costs them."
    *set combat_roa_last_banner "[b][🩸 Opportunity Attack Hits: ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}][/b]"
*elseif (combat_reaction_skill = "Riposte")
  *set combat_roa_last_line "You answer the miss at once, but ${combat_enemy_epithet} is already back out of line."
  *set combat_roa_last_banner "[b][💨 Riposte Misses][/b]"
*else
  *set combat_roa_last_line "$!{combat_enemy_epithet} breaks away before you can make the opening count."
  *set combat_roa_last_banner "[b][💨 Opportunity Attack Misses][/b]"
*if (not(combat_save_quiet))
  ${combat_roa_last_line}
  *line_break
  ${combat_roa_last_banner}
*comment Ends on a break unless the blow ended the fight (fight_victory opens with its own; a second would print an empty line). The caller repeats the sync and the check.
*gosub fight_sync_target_out
*gosub fight_check_all_dead
*if (not(combat_save_quiet)) and (not(combat_all_enemies_dead))
  *line_break
*return

*label resolve_reverse_oa_ally
*params droa_ally_slot
*comment An ally's own opportunity attack against the enemy currently retreating (2026-09-29). Reuses
*comment resolve_ally_attack and fight_narrate_ally_attack whole -- same reasoning as the player version reusing
*comment resolve_player_weapon_hit, and the enemy-side's own resolve_opportunity_attack reusing
*comment resolve_enemy_attack: a reaction should hit exactly like that combatant's normal attack does, no forked
*comment math or parallel narration path.
*gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_ally_attack droa_ally_slot combat_active_target
*gosub_scene combat_prose fight_narrate_ally_attack "ally" droa_ally_slot "enemy" combat_active_target combat_ally_atk_hit
*return

*label combat_cornered_decide
*comment A CORNERED CASTER WEIGHS THE OPPORTUNITY ATTACK (2026-10-23, the user). Called for the enemy now dispatching (its scratch fields are synced) when it is ranged, cornered, able to retreat and has room, and its profile counts the risk (combat_behavior_oa_weight above 0). Sets combat_cornered_choice:
*comment   "disengage"     the one attack that would answer a retreat could kill it (its most damage meets or beats this enemy's hit points): break away with no shot and no attack taken, as the wounded do;
*comment   "retreat_shoot" the shot is worth more than the attack it would cost (the old behavior for everyone);
*comment   "stay"          the sidearm in the tile it stands in is worth more than the shot less that attack (or, with no sidearm, than the same shot at disadvantage).
*comment Everything is in percent times twice the average damage, so it stays whole numbers: a shot is its hit chance against the player's AC times twice its average damage. The attack that answers a retreat is the one find_opportunity_attacker_against would pick (the player first, else the weakest melee ally), worked out with the same weapon rules resolve_reverse_oa_player uses; no such attacker means the retreat is free.
*comment The wounded rule (the flee percent) still comes first in combat_next_turn and is not touched here.
*set combat_cornered_choice "retreat_shoot"
*temp ccd_ac armor_class
*temp ccd_p_shot (5 * (21 - (ccd_ac - combat_enemy_atk_bonus)))
*if (ccd_p_shot < 5)
  *set ccd_p_shot 5
*if (ccd_p_shot > 95)
  *set ccd_p_shot 95
*temp ccd_avg_shot (((2 * combat_enemy_dmg_bonus) + combat_enemy_dmg_dice_sides) + 1)
*temp ccd_p_staff ccd_p_shot
*temp ccd_avg_staff 0
*temp ccd_has_staff false
*if (not(combat_enemy_sidearm_weapon_type = ""))
  *set ccd_has_staff true
  *set ccd_p_staff (5 * (21 - (ccd_ac - combat_enemy_sidearm_atk_bonus)))
  *if (ccd_p_staff < 5)
    *set ccd_p_staff 5
  *if (ccd_p_staff > 95)
    *set ccd_p_staff 95
  *set ccd_avg_staff (((2 * combat_enemy_sidearm_dmg_bonus) + combat_enemy_sidearm_dmg_dice_sides) + 1)
*temp ccd_oa_p 0
*temp ccd_oa_avg 0
*temp ccd_oa_max 0
*temp ccd_oa_atk 0
*temp ccd_oa_sides 0
*temp ccd_oa_bonus 0
*gosub find_opportunity_attacker_against
*if (combat_oa_against_kind = "player")
  *set ccd_oa_atk str_mod
  *set ccd_oa_sides 8
  *set ccd_oa_bonus str_mod
  *if (weapon_type = "finesse")
    *set ccd_oa_sides 4
    *if (dex_mod > str_mod)
      *set ccd_oa_atk dex_mod
      *set ccd_oa_bonus dex_mod
  *elseif (weapon_hands = "two_handed")
    *set ccd_oa_sides 12
  *if ((fighting_style = "dueling") and (not(weapon_hands = "two_handed")))
    *set ccd_oa_bonus + 2
  *if ((character_class = "barbarian") and (is_raging))
    *set ccd_oa_bonus + barbarian_rage_damage_bonus
  *set ccd_oa_atk + prof_bonus
*elseif (combat_oa_against_kind = "ally")
  *set ccd_oa_atk {(("combat_ally" & combat_oa_against_slot) & "_atk_bonus")}
  *set ccd_oa_sides {(("combat_ally" & combat_oa_against_slot) & "_dmg_dice_sides")}
  *set ccd_oa_bonus {(("combat_ally" & combat_oa_against_slot) & "_dmg_bonus")}
*if (ccd_oa_sides > 0)
  *set ccd_oa_p (5 * (21 - (combat_enemy_ac - ccd_oa_atk)))
  *if (ccd_oa_p < 5)
    *set ccd_oa_p 5
  *if (ccd_oa_p > 95)
    *set ccd_oa_p 95
  *set ccd_oa_avg (((2 * ccd_oa_bonus) + ccd_oa_sides) + 1)
  *set ccd_oa_max (ccd_oa_sides + ccd_oa_bonus)
*temp ccd_dealt ((ccd_p_shot * ccd_avg_shot) * 100)
*temp ccd_taken ((ccd_oa_p * ccd_oa_avg) * combat_behavior_oa_weight)
*temp ccd_stay (((ccd_p_shot * ccd_p_shot) * ccd_avg_shot))
*if (ccd_has_staff)
  *set ccd_stay ((ccd_p_staff * ccd_avg_staff) * 100)
*comment A spell it can cast from Engaged (touch reach, or an area around itself) can be worth staying for. Each assigned place with a use and a chance left is read
*comment from its data (ccd_spell_value): an attack roll by its hit chance, a save by the player's chance to fail (their save's ability modifier as the estimate),
*comment always-hits in full; the best one counts if it beats the shot.
*temp ccd_ep (("combat_enemy" & combat_active_target) & "_")
*temp ccd_spell_stay 0
*temp ccd_k 1
*if (combat_active_target > 0)
  *gosub ccd_spell_loop
*if ((ccd_oa_sides > 0) and (ccd_oa_max >= combat_enemy_hp))
  *set combat_cornered_choice "disengage"
*elseif ((ccd_dealt - ccd_taken) < ccd_stay)
  *set combat_cornered_choice "stay"
*if (combat_ai_trace)
  *comment In expected damage (the scores are percent times twice the average, times 100: / 20000), to one decimal.
  *temp ccd_t_shot (round(ccd_dealt / 2000) / 10)
  *temp ccd_t_taken (round(ccd_taken / 2000) / 10)
  *temp ccd_t_net (round((ccd_dealt - ccd_taken) / 2000) / 10)
  *temp ccd_t_stay (round(ccd_stay / 2000) / 10)
  *gosub_scene combat_ai ai_trace combat_enemy_name "cornered: retreat and shoot ${ccd_t_net} (its shot ${ccd_t_shot}, less the attack it draws ${ccd_t_taken} as the profile weighs it), stay ${ccd_t_stay}; that attack can deal up to ${ccd_oa_max} of its ${combat_enemy_hp} HP → ${combat_cornered_choice}"
*return

*comment combat_cornered_decide's loop over the slot's three spell places (ccd_ep, ccd_k), keeping the best score in ccd_stay.
*label ccd_spell_loop
*if (ccd_k > 3)
  *return
*temp ccd_sid {(ccd_ep & ("cast_spell_" & ccd_k))}
*if (((not(ccd_sid = "")) and ({(ccd_ep & ("cast_uses_" & ccd_k))} > 0)) and ({(ccd_ep & ("cast_chance_" & ccd_k))} > 0))
  *gosub ccd_spell_value ccd_sid
  *if (ccd_spell_stay > ccd_stay)
    *set ccd_stay ccd_spell_stay
*set ccd_k + 1
*goto ccd_spell_loop

*comment combat_cornered_decide's spell score (combat_ai's units: percent chance times average damage, times 100). 0 for a spell that cannot be cast from Engaged
*comment or does no damage. Reads the slot in ccd_ep and the player's AC in ccd_ac.
*label ccd_spell_value
*params csv_id
*set ccd_spell_stay 0
*gosub_scene combat_spells spell_facts_load csv_id
*if (spell_fact_dice_sides <= 0)
  *return
*if ((not(spell_fact_reach = "touch")) and (not(spell_fact_target = "engaged")))
  *return
*temp csv_n spell_fact_dice_count
*if (csv_n < 1)
  *set csv_n 1
*temp csv_avg (((csv_n * (spell_fact_dice_sides + 1)) / 2) + spell_fact_flat)
*if (spell_fact_resolution = "auto")
  *set ccd_spell_stay ((100 * csv_avg) * 100)
*elseif (spell_fact_resolution = "attack")
  *temp csv_atk {(ccd_ep & "cast_atk_bonus")}
  *if (csv_atk <= 0)
    *set csv_atk combat_enemy_atk_bonus
  *temp csv_p (5 * (21 - (ccd_ac - csv_atk)))
  *if (csv_p < 5)
    *set csv_p 5
  *if (csv_p > 95)
    *set csv_p 95
  *set ccd_spell_stay ((csv_p * csv_avg) * 100)
*elseif (spell_fact_resolution = "save")
  *temp csv_dc {(ccd_ep & "cast_dc")}
  *if (csv_dc <= 0)
    *set csv_dc 10
  *temp csv_mod 0
  *if (spell_fact_save_ability = "strength")
    *set csv_mod str_mod
  *elseif (spell_fact_save_ability = "dexterity")
    *set csv_mod dex_mod
  *elseif (spell_fact_save_ability = "constitution")
    *set csv_mod con_mod
  *elseif (spell_fact_save_ability = "intelligence")
    *set csv_mod int_mod
  *elseif (spell_fact_save_ability = "wisdom")
    *set csv_mod wis_mod
  *elseif (spell_fact_save_ability = "charisma")
    *set csv_mod cha_mod
  *temp csv_fail (5 * ((csv_dc - 1) - csv_mod))
  *if (csv_fail < 5)
    *set csv_fail 5
  *if (csv_fail > 95)
    *set csv_fail 95
  *temp csv_exp (csv_fail * csv_avg)
  *if (spell_fact_half_on_save)
    *set csv_exp (csv_exp + (((100 - csv_fail) * csv_avg) / 2))
  *set ccd_spell_stay (csv_exp * 100)
*return

*comment COMMAND, THE TURN IT OBEYS (2026-11-20, 2024 rules). fight_sync_target_in has already loaded this slot. Prints one line and one banner.
*comment   approach  one step toward the player (its move, no Dash, no attack); none if already beside them
*comment   drop      drops what it holds; the turn ends (RAW it picks the weapon up for free next turn, so nothing lasts)
*comment   flee      Dashes away from the player, two steps (one if it had to stand up first)
*comment   grovel    falls Prone (prone_apply); the turn ends
*comment   halt      no move, no action; the turn ends
*comment A restrained or held foe cannot move, so Approach and Flee only strain against it. Leaving the reach of the player or an ally on its tile provokes an
*comment opportunity attack (find_opportunity_attacker_against), as a retreat does. The caller ends the turn at cnt_boom_tail if the foe died on the way.
*label fight_enemy_obey_command
*params feoc_slot feoc_word
*temp feoc_p (("combat_enemy" & feoc_slot) & "_")
*gosub_scene combat_prose command_word_name feoc_word
*temp feoc_word_name combat_command_word_name
*temp feoc_moves ((feoc_word = "approach") or (feoc_word = "flee"))
*temp feoc_held (({(feoc_p & "restrained")} > 0) or ({(feoc_p & "pinned")} > 0))
*if ((feoc_moves) and (feoc_held))
  *gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_command_obey "held"
  *line_break
  [b][👑 ${combat_enemy_name} obeys Command: ${feoc_word_name} — held fast, turn lost][/b]
  *line_break
  *return
*if ((feoc_word = "approach") and (combat_enemy_range <= 0))
  *gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_command_obey "beside"
  *line_break
  [b][👑 ${combat_enemy_name} obeys Command: Approach — already beside you, turn lost][/b]
  *line_break
  *return
*if (feoc_moves)
  *comment Someone in its reach: an ally on its tile, or the player when it flees from beside them.
  *temp feoc_ally_here false
  *temp feoc_i 1
  *label fight_enemy_obey_command_ally_loop
  *if (feoc_i <= combat_ally_slot_cap)
    *if (({(("combat_ally" & feoc_i) & "_hp")} > 0) and ({(("combat_ally" & feoc_i) & "_position")} = {(feoc_p & "position")}))
      *set feoc_ally_here true
    *set feoc_i + 1
    *goto fight_enemy_obey_command_ally_loop
  *if ((feoc_ally_here) or ((feoc_word = "flee") and (combat_enemy_range <= 0)))
    *gosub find_opportunity_attacker_against
    *if (combat_oa_against_kind = "player")
      *set player_oa_used true
      *gosub resolve_reverse_oa_player
      *gosub fight_sync_target_out
    *elseif (combat_oa_against_kind = "ally")
      *set {(("combat_ally" & combat_oa_against_slot) & "_oa_used")} true
      *gosub resolve_reverse_oa_ally combat_oa_against_slot
      *gosub fight_sync_target_in
    *if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
      *return
  *temp feoc_steps 1
  *if ((feoc_word = "flee") and (not({(feoc_p & "prone")})))
    *set feoc_steps 2
  *set {(feoc_p & "prone")} false
  *if (feoc_word = "approach")
    *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_lane_move_enemy_relative_to_player feoc_slot "toward"
  *else
    *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_lane_move_enemy_relative_to_player feoc_slot "away"
    *if (feoc_steps > 1)
      *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_lane_move_enemy_relative_to_player feoc_slot "away"
  *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_range_name combat_enemy_range
  *gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_command_obey feoc_word
  *line_break
  *if ((feoc_word = "approach") and (combat_enemy_range <= 0))
    [b][👑 ${combat_enemy_name} obeys Command: Approach — comes to you][/b]
  *else
    [b][👑 ${combat_enemy_name} obeys Command: ${feoc_word_name} — ${combat_range_display_name}][/b]
  *line_break
  *gosub fight_booming_trigger
  *return
*if (feoc_word = "grovel")
  *gosub_scene combat_dice prone_apply feoc_p
  *gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_command_obey feoc_word
  *line_break
  [b][👑 ${combat_enemy_name} obeys Command: Grovel — falls prone, turn lost][/b]
  *line_break
  *return
*gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_command_obey feoc_word
*line_break
[b][👑 ${combat_enemy_name} obeys Command: ${feoc_word_name} — turn lost][/b]
*line_break
*return

*label fight_check_all_dead
*comment A bounded loop over combat_enemy_count, via indirection, instead of one hand-written branch per slot.
*set combat_all_enemies_dead true
*temp fcd_i 1
*label fight_check_all_dead_loop
*if (fcd_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *temp fcd_hp {(("combat_enemy" & fcd_i) & "_hp")}
  *if (fcd_hp > 0)
    *set combat_all_enemies_dead false
  *else
    *gosub fiend_blessing_check fcd_i
  *set fcd_i + 1
  *goto fight_check_all_dead_loop
*comment A fight with a leader (combat_leader_slot) is won when the leader falls, whoever else is still on their feet.
*temp fcd_leader_down false
*if (combat_leader_slot > 0)
  *temp fcd_lhp {(("combat_enemy" & combat_leader_slot) & "_hp")}
  *if (fcd_lhp <= 0)
    *set fcd_leader_down true
*comment Everyone on the field is down but reinforcements are still on their way (REINFORCEMENTS): they arrive now, and the fight goes on.
*if ((combat_all_enemies_dead) and (not(fcd_leader_down)))
  *gosub combat_reinforcements_arrive true
  *if (combat_reinforcement_arrived)
    *set combat_all_enemies_dead false
*if (fcd_leader_down)
  *set combat_all_enemies_dead true
*return

*comment DARK ONE'S BLESSING (the Fiend warlock, level 3, PHB'24; 2026-11-20). An enemy that has just dropped to 0 HP, seen once here by fight_check_all_dead,
*comment which runs after every blow. Killed by the player (its last hit, record_last_hit), or by an ally while it stood on the player's tile (10 feet: the
*comment tile, as Arms of Hadar's radius is): temp HP = CHA mod + warlock level, at least 1. Temp HP never stack, so it is kept only when it is more than
*comment what is held, and replacing Armor of Agathys's temp HP ends Agathys. One that surrendered on morale or fled was never reduced to 0 by a blow.
*comment Once per enemy per fight (_blessed). The line waits in the end-of-action queue (concentration_pending_add, combat_dice.txt) and prints on its own
*comment line when the turn ends (combat_next_turn, the hub) or the fight is won (fight_victory, fight_victory_quiet), so it never joins whatever prints next.
*label fiend_blessing_check
*params fbc_slot
*temp fbc_p (("combat_enemy" & fbc_slot) & "_")
*if ({(fbc_p & "blessed")})
  *return
*set {(fbc_p & "blessed")} true
*if (not(feature_dark_ones_blessing))
  *return
*temp fbc_out {(fbc_p & "out")}
*if ((fbc_out = "surrendered") or (fbc_out = "fled"))
  *return
*temp fbc_kind {(fbc_p & "last_hit_kind")}
*temp fbc_ok (fbc_kind = "player")
*if ((fbc_kind = "ally") and ({(fbc_p & "position")} = combat_lane_player_position))
  *set fbc_ok true
*if (not(fbc_ok))
  *return
*temp fbc_amount (cha_mod + character_level)
*if (fbc_amount < 1)
  *set fbc_amount 1
*if (temp_hp < fbc_amount)
  *temp fbc_ends ""
  *if ((agathys_active) and (temp_hp > 0))
    *set agathys_active false
    *set fbc_ends " | Armor of Agathys ends"
  *set temp_hp fbc_amount
  *temp fbc_line "[b][😈 Dark One's Blessing: ${fbc_amount} temp HP${fbc_ends}][/b]"
  *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_pending_add fbc_line
*return

*label do_enemy_chase_step
*params decs_slot
*comment The shared movement step, called both the turn a chase is freshly committed (from the closing-toward-
*comment player branch, immediately after roll_enemy_chase_commit succeeds) and every turn after (from
*comment combat_next_turn's chase-continuation *elseif) -- same step either way. Closes ONE tier per turn toward
*comment combat_enemy_chase_target, no Dash-equivalent -- deliberately simpler than the player-closing branch's
*comment free-move-plus-dash shape: a chasing enemy trades away some of its aggressive tempo for breaking
*comment formation, which is both a reasonable in-fiction cost and avoids a third copy of that logic.
*comment Chase movement is a real shared-lane step, not a private counter. This lets a later terrain rule see
*comment the pursuer's crossing just like any other movement action.
*temp decs_enemy_pos {(("combat_enemy" & decs_slot) & "_position")}
*temp decs_ally_pos {(("combat_ally" & combat_enemy_chase_target) & "_position")}
*if (decs_enemy_pos < decs_ally_pos)
  *set decs_enemy_pos + 1
*if (decs_enemy_pos > decs_ally_pos)
  *set decs_enemy_pos - 1
*set {(("combat_enemy" & decs_slot) & "_position")} decs_enemy_pos
*comment Lane step 3: a chasing enemy can cross the player's tile on its way to an ally, and then stands on the other side of them (before, the legacy bridge put it back ahead).
*temp decs_off (decs_enemy_pos - combat_lane_player_position)
*if (decs_off > 0)
  *set {(("combat_enemy" & decs_slot) & "_side")} 1
*if (decs_off < 0)
  *set {(("combat_enemy" & decs_slot) & "_side")} (0 - 1)
*set combat_enemy_chase_range (decs_enemy_pos - decs_ally_pos)
*if (combat_enemy_chase_range < 0)
  *set combat_enemy_chase_range (0 - combat_enemy_chase_range)
*set {(("combat_enemy" & decs_slot) & "_chase_range")} combat_enemy_chase_range
*temp decs_player_range (decs_enemy_pos - combat_lane_player_position)
*if (decs_player_range < 0)
  *set decs_player_range (0 - decs_player_range)
*if (decs_player_range > combat_max_range)
  *set decs_player_range combat_max_range
*set {(("combat_enemy" & decs_slot) & "_range")} decs_player_range
*set combat_enemy_range decs_player_range
*temp decs_ally_name {(("combat_ally" & combat_enemy_chase_target) & "_name")}
*temp decs_ally_epithet {(("combat_ally" & combat_enemy_chase_target) & "_epithet")}
*temp decs_rv 0
*rand decs_rv 1 3
*if (combat_enemy_chase_range <= 0)
  *comment Dedicated new prose (2026-09-29, on request) -- never the existing closing-toward-player lines, which
  *comment are hardcoded second-person ("right on top of YOU") and would be flatly wrong for a chase that never
  *comment involves the player at all.
  *if (decs_rv = 1)
    $!{combat_enemy_epithet} finally closes the last of the distance, and ${decs_ally_epithet} has nowhere left to retreat.
  *if (decs_rv = 2)
    A last hard burst of speed, and ${combat_enemy_epithet} is suddenly right on top of ${decs_ally_epithet}.
  *if (decs_rv = 3)
    The gap disappears, and ${combat_enemy_epithet} is finally within striking distance of ${decs_ally_epithet}.
  *line_break
  [b][🏃 ${combat_enemy_name} Closes on ${decs_ally_name}][/b]
  *line_break
  *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
  *gosub_scene combat_ai fight_pick_enemy_move
  *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_enemy_attack_on_ally combat_enemy_chase_target
  *gosub_scene combat_prose fight_narrate_ally_attack "enemy" decs_slot "ally" combat_enemy_chase_target combat_enemy_atk_on_ally_hit
  *comment Arrived and fought -- Layer 1's ordinary target selection takes back over from here (plan §11.3:
  *comment "it's not chasing anymore, it's fighting"), not locked onto this one ally for the rest of the fight.
  *set combat_enemy_chase_target 0
  *set {(("combat_enemy" & decs_slot) & "_chase_target")} 0
*else
  *if (decs_rv = 1)
    Breaking off from the fight in front of it, ${combat_enemy_epithet} cuts hard toward ${decs_ally_epithet}, closing the gap fast.
  *if (decs_rv = 2)
    $!{combat_enemy_epithet} shoulders past the melee and bears down on ${decs_ally_epithet}, ground vanishing underfoot.
  *if (decs_rv = 3)
    Ignoring everything else, ${combat_enemy_epithet} presses toward ${decs_ally_epithet}, closing the distance step by hard step.
  *line_break
  [b][🏃 ${combat_enemy_name} Closes on ${decs_ally_name}][/b]
  *line_break
  *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*comment ============================================================================
*comment COMBAT: COMMIT-AND-CHASE, ALLY SIDE (2026-09-29, Ranged-AI Position Awareness plan §11.3, "6a" mirror)

*comment ============================================================================
*comment REINFORCEMENTS (2026-11-03, the user). An enemy can join the fight late: combat_enemyN_arrives is the round it arrives on (0 or 1:
*comment there from the start; an encounter template's "arrives"). Slot 1 is always there from the start.
*comment   SETUP (combat_reinforcements_hold, after initiative, so it already has its place in the turn order): it steps off the field: _out
*comment     "reserve", its HP kept in _reserve_hp and set to 0, so targeting, the AI and every "who is still standing" check pass over it. The
*comment     morale and Intimidate checks leave it out of its side altogether.
*comment   ARRIVAL (combat_reinforcements_arrive): at the start of its round (combat_next_turn, as the round turns over), on its own edge of the
*comment     lane with its HP back. ENTERS AT (2026-11-22, the user): combat_enemyN_enters_at, a range tier from the player (0 Engaged to the fight's
*comment     combat_max_range; an encounter template's "enters_at"), puts it there on its own side instead; 99 (the default) keeps the edge. If everyone else on its side is down first (fight_check_all_dead), every one still on its way arrives at
*comment     once and the fight goes on, unless the fight's leader fell (that still ends it).
*comment   A reinforcement that never arrived (you fled first) counts as "standing" in the FIGHT RESULTS.
*comment ============================================================================
*label combat_reinforcements_hold
*temp crh_i 2
*label combat_reinforcements_hold_loop
*if (crh_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *temp crh_p (("combat_enemy" & crh_i) & "_")
  *if ({(crh_p & "arrives")} > 1)
    *set {(crh_p & "reserve_hp")} {(crh_p & "hp")}
    *set {(crh_p & "hp")} 0
    *set {(crh_p & "out")} "reserve"
  *set crh_i + 1
  *goto combat_reinforcements_hold_loop
*return

*comment Bring in every reinforcement whose round has come (cra_now true: all of them, now). Output: combat_reinforcement_arrived.

  *temp cra_p (("combat_enemy" & cra_i) & "_")
  *if (({(cra_p & "out")} = "reserve") and ((cra_now) or ({(cra_p & "arrives")} <= combat_round)))
    *set {(cra_p & "out")} ""
    *set {(cra_p & "hp")} {(cra_p & "reserve_hp")}

      *gosub fight_sync_target_in

    *if ((cra_at < 99) and (cra_range = 0))

    *elseif ((cra_at < 99) and (cra_range = 1))

    *else

*comment ============================================================================
*comment FIGHT RESULTS (2026-11-03, the user). How each enemy ended the last fight, written by fight_cleanup and kept until the next fight
*comment ends, for the story to read after *gosub_scene combat returns:
*comment   combat_result_count        how many enemies the fight had
*comment   combat_result_down         how many were dropped (killed; knocked out in a non-lethal fight)
*comment   combat_result_fled         how many ran off the field
*comment   combat_result_surrendered  how many gave up
*comment   combat_result_standing     how many were still fighting when it ended (you fled, lost, or the leader fell; or a reinforcement never arrived)
*comment   combat_resultN_type / _name / _group / _outcome  slot N's type from the catalog ("beast", "archer"; "" for a hand-built slot),
*comment     its name ("Boar 2"), its group (an encounter template's "Boar"; "" otherwise), and "down", "fled", "surrendered" or "standing"
*comment To count one kind, *gosub_scene combat count_results "Boar" "down" sets combat_result_n; the first is a type, a name or a group
*comment ("" for any), the second an outcome ("" for any). Three boars, two dead and one run off:
*comment   *gosub_scene combat count_results "Boar" "down"
*comment   *if (combat_result_n > 0)
*comment     #Butcher the boar@{(combat_result_n > 1) s|}.
*comment   *if (combat_result_fled > 0) ... the one that got away.
*comment Morale is off in story fights (MORALE OFF at each call site); leave out *set combat_no_morale true for a fight whose enemies may run.
*comment ============================================================================
*label combat_record_results
*set combat_result_count combat_enemy_count
*set combat_result_down 0
*set combat_result_fled 0
*set combat_result_surrendered 0
*set combat_result_standing 0
*gosub fight_sync_target_out
*temp crr_i 1
*label combat_record_results_loop
*if (crr_i <= combat_enemy_slot_cap)
  *temp crr_p (("combat_enemy" & crr_i) & "_")
  *temp crr_r ("combat_result" & crr_i)
  *temp crr_out "standing"
  *temp crr_name ""
  *temp crr_type ""
  *temp crr_group ""
  *if (crr_i <= combat_enemy_count)
    *set crr_name {(crr_p & "name")}
    *set crr_type {(crr_p & "type_id")}
    *set crr_group {(crr_p & "group")}
    *if ({(crr_p & "out")} = "reserve")
      *comment A reinforcement that never arrived (REINFORCEMENTS) was still out there.
      *set combat_result_standing + 1
    *elseif ({(crr_p & "out")} = "fled")
      *set crr_out "fled"
      *set combat_result_fled + 1
    *elseif ({(crr_p & "out")} = "surrendered")
      *set crr_out "surrendered"
      *set combat_result_surrendered + 1
    *elseif ({(crr_p & "hp")} <= 0)
      *set crr_out "down"
      *set combat_result_down + 1
    *else
      *set combat_result_standing + 1
  *else
    *set crr_out ""
  *set {(crr_r & "_name")} crr_name
  *set {(crr_r & "_type")} crr_type
  *set {(crr_r & "_group")} crr_group
  *set {(crr_p & "group")} ""
  *set {(crr_r & "_outcome")} crr_out
  *set {(crr_p & "type_id")} ""
  *set crr_i + 1
  *goto combat_record_results_loop
*return

*comment Count the last fight's enemies of one type, name or group (cr_who, "" for any) that ended one way (cr_how, "" for any). Output: combat_result_n.
*label count_results
*params cr_who cr_how
*set combat_result_n 0
*temp cr_i 1
*label count_results_loop
*if (cr_i <= combat_result_count)
  *temp cr_r ("combat_result" & cr_i)
  *temp cr_match ((cr_who = "") or ((cr_who = {(cr_r & "_type")}) or ((cr_who = {(cr_r & "_name")}) or (cr_who = {(cr_r & "_group")}))))
  *if ((cr_match) and ((cr_how = "") or (cr_how = {(cr_r & "_outcome")})))
    *set combat_result_n + 1
  *set cr_i + 1
  *goto count_results_loop
*return

*set combat_outcome "victory"

*return

*label fight_player_died
*comment Only reachable when this fight set combat_player_can_die true AND
*comment hp_current actually hit 0. Doesn't *goto_scene death itself --
*comment that would fire from inside this *gosub_scene call and corrupt the
*comment subscene stack (see narrative_guidelines.md Section 13). Instead
*comment reports combat_outcome exactly like every other exit and *returns;
*comment the CALLER checks it after the *gosub_scene returns and does the
*comment *goto_scene death death_screen itself, setting death_cause "combat"
*comment first. Every call site (*gosub_scene combat fight_<name>) needs
*comment this check even for fights that are non-lethal today, in case a
*comment later edit flips combat_player_can_die true on it.
*set combat_outcome "player_died"
*gosub fight_cleanup
*return

*comment ============================================================================
*comment THE TURN BUDGET
*comment The player has three budgets each round: combat_player_actions_left, combat_player_bonus_left and
*comment combat_player_attacks_left, each refilled from its own _max (startup.txt, all 1 by default). A round is
*comment the player's turn plus the enemies' turns today; when the fight becomes turn-ordered these become the
*comment acting creature's budget and combat_begin_round becomes combat_begin_turn.
*comment   ATTACK (the weapon/sidearm options): gated on combat_player_attacks_left > 0, left through
*comment     fight_attack_spent, which spends one attack and, only on the FIRST attack of the round, also spends
*comment     one action (Attack IS your action; Extra Attack, a future level-5 feature, lets combat_player_
*comment     attacks_max go above 1 so several swings come out of that one action instead of several actions).
*comment   ACTION (a damage spell, Agathys, False Life, Brace, an item): leaves the hub through fight_action_spent,
*comment     which spends one action.
*comment   BONUS ACTION (Hex, Healing Word, Second Wind, Rage): gated on combat_player_bonus_left > 0 (plus its
*comment     own combat_can_X availability flag, below) and left through fight_bonus_spent, which spends one.
*comment   REACTION: not a budget yet. Shield is an "armed" flag that fires on the next hit, which is how a reaction behaves.
*comment None of the three spends ends the turn by itself -- every spend just returns to the hub, regardless of
*comment what's left. The hub itself auto-ends the turn once the action and attack budgets are gone AND nothing
*comment bonus-gated is available either (see combat_bonus_available below) -- a new bonus-action feature needs
*comment its own combat_can_X flag added there, same as Hex/Healing Word/Second Wind/Rage, or it silently blocks
*comment auto-end from ever firing while it's on cooldown. The always-available "End Turn" option in the hub's
*comment own *choice block remains the only way to end the turn early, on request, with resources still unspent.
*comment Extras: an ongoing effect changes a _max value (Haste: combat_player_actions_max 2, back to 1 when it
*comment ends; its extra action is restricted in 5e to one weapon attack, Dash, Disengage, Hide or Use an Object,
*comment which a spell option can check with a flag when it is built). A one-off extra is *gosub combat_grant_
*comment action, combat_grant_bonus or combat_grant_attack (Action Surge, etc.).
*comment Every spend and grant is replay-locked (the page is replayed when the Stats screen closes), and
*comment combat_begin_round is a set of fixed values, so it is safe to repeat.
*comment ============================================================================

*label combat_begin_round

*if (combat_player_no_heal_slot > 0)
  *if ({(("combat_enemy" & combat_player_no_heal_slot) & "_hp")} <= 0)
    *set combat_player_no_heal_slot 0

  *goto combat_begin_round_mc_loop

*set combat_player_disengaged false
*set combat_hide_checked false
*comment Start of a round: refill the budgets. Enemy disadvantage/advantage (Ray of Frost, Vicious Mockery, Brace,

*set combat_player_actions_left combat_player_actions_max

  *set combat_terrain1_type "none"

*label blade_ward_tick
*comment Advances Blade Ward's 10-round concentration count by ONE player turn (fight_end_player_turn only).
*comment Clears blade_ward_just_cast without decrementing on the turn cast, covering 10 rounds of enemy turns.
*comment A turn lost to a stun still counts as a turn here.
*comment Replay-locked on the page_id idiom: an unguarded *set here re-runs on page replay and would
*comment expire the ward early.
*if (not(blade_ward_tick_locked)) or (not(locked_blade_ward_tick_page_id = page_id))
  *if (blade_ward_just_cast)
    *set blade_ward_just_cast false
  *elseif (blade_ward_rounds > 0)
    *set blade_ward_rounds - 1
    *if (blade_ward_rounds <= 0)
      *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_reconcile
  *set blade_ward_tick_locked true
  *set locked_blade_ward_tick_page_id page_id
*return

*comment ============================================================================
*comment REPLAY-SAFE TURN CHAINS (2026-11-03). The engine saves the CURRENT variables with the page's START line, so a browser refresh, or the
*comment Stats screen and back, runs the page again on top of its own results (web/scene.js refreshSavedProgress). A page of enemy and ally turns
*comment used to be guarded one effect at a time (the attack, narration and stun locks), and everything without a lock happened twice: an ally or
*comment an enemy stepped again, a chase moved on, an ally lost HP again.
*comment The chain now guards itself as a whole. combat_chain_begin runs where every chain starts (fight_end_player_turn, and the fight's opening
*comment dispatch in fight_round_hub). The first time on a page it notes every variable's value (combat_chain_snap) and draws a seed; on a replay
*comment of that page it puts those values back first. Either way it seeds the dice from combat_chain_seed for the length of the chain, so the
*comment replay rolls the same numbers, makes the same choices and prints the same lines. combat_chain_end runs where the chain hands back to the
*comment player, or the fight ends (fight_cleanup): it trims the note to just the variables the chain changed (usually a few hundred bytes in the
*comment save) and hands the dice back. The per-effect locks are kept; with the chain restored they simply roll their first-pass values again.
*comment Engine bookkeeping (choice_*) and the chain's own fields are never noted. A test harness that fixes the dice itself sets
*comment __combatFixedRandom, so its fixed rolls are left alone.
*label combat_chain_begin

*comment Nearest-eligible range per direction (2026-09-28, button projection): which tier the move will act on,

*comment Deliberately does NOT call fight_pick_nearest_target to work this out. That label is the right authority
*comment for actually performing the move, but it cannot serve as a read-only preview: it overwrites
*comment combat_active_target and re-syncs the scratch the weapon-attack gates at the top of this choice have
*comment already read, and its *else clears combat_player_chosen_target when the clicked slot is alive but
*comment ineligible for that direction -- so previewing "push" while your clicked target is Engaged would
*comment silently drop that click and change who the next attack goes to. The eligibility rules are mirrored here
*comment as plain arithmetic over the slots instead, and the option bodies still call fight_pick_nearest_target
*comment as the single authority that performs the move.
*comment The chosen-target precedence is the subtle part and must match that label exactly: when
*comment combat_player_chosen_target is alive AND eligible, fight_pick_nearest_target takes it and SKIPS the
*comment nearest-scan outright (guarded by `*if (combat_active_target = 0)`). Taking the minimum anyway would
*comment mislabel the button whenever a clicked target sat at a wider tier than some other enemy -- e.g. clicking
*comment the Long Range archer while a swordsman is Close reads as pushable-from-Close, when the move that actually
*comment fires is on the archer. So the *_lock temps freeze the projection at the chosen target's tier and stop
*comment the scan from touching that direction, exactly reproducing the skip.

*comment Auto-end: silently loops back to the queue once there's truly nothing left to spend a resource on, not
*comment just once every pool counter happens to read zero -- attacks_left and bonus_left both sit at their full
*comment value on a turn that never touched them (a caster who casts instead of swinging, or any turn where no
*comment bonus feature got used), so testing combat_can_attack and combat_bonus_available instead of the raw
*comment counters is what tells "full because unavailable" apart from "full because unspent." Movement
*comment (2026-09-28) needs no such distinction -- unlike a bonus action, moves_left > 0 is ALWAYS usable: with
*comment two or more enemies at different ranges, at least one of Push Forward/Fall Back is always legal for SOME
*comment living enemy (proven: an enemy can't be simultaneously at range 0 and range 3), so a plain
*comment moves_left <= 0 check is sufficient. Skips past "Use an item" (not pool-gated) -- use it before running
*comment the tank dry. "Break off and run" was also skipped by that reasoning while it cost nothing at all
*comment (2026-09-28); it now costs a movement tier like any other departure and provokes an opportunity attack, so
*comment the same plain moves_left <= 0 check covers it and no separate flag is needed in that *if. Disengage is
*comment gated on the ACTION budget instead, which keeps the turn alive on its own -- and correctly so, since the
*comment *if already refuses to auto-end while actions_left > 0.
*if ((((((combat_player_actions_left <= 0) and (not(combat_can_attack))) and ((combat_player_bonus_left <= 0) or (not(combat_bonus_available)))) and (combat_player_moves_left <= 0)) and (not(combat_can_action_surge))) and (not(combat_can_shield)))
  *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page false
  *comment Ends the player's turn for Blade Ward's duration too, exactly like the End your turn option
  *comment -- running out of things to spend on IS a turn-end, so the ward must not sit un-aged.
  *goto fight_end_player_turn

*choice
  *if ((combat_can_attack) and ((combat_wpn_far_ok) or (combat_primary_reach)))
    # [b][ACTION][/b] [b][Attack with your ${weapon}][/b]${combat_weapon_tag}.
      *if (not(weapon_type = "ranged"))
        *gosub_scene combat_movement fight_weapon_reach_check
        *if (not(combat_reach_ok))
          ${combat_reach_msg}
          *line_break
          *goto fight_round_hub
      *goto resolve_weapon_attack_choice

  *comment GEN:BEGIN combat_arrow_options -- generated by tools/gen_gear.js from tools/gear_catalog.json; edit the catalog, not this block
  *if (has_fire_arrow)
    *if (((combat_can_attack) and (weapon_ammo = "arrow")) and ((combat_wpn_far_ok) or (combat_primary_reach)))
      # [b][ACTION][/b] [b][Shoot: Fire Arrow][/b] Loose it at your target.@{show_stat_hints  [Shot: your bow attack plus 1d4 fire, then burns for 2 turns]|}
        *set combat_arrow_kind "fire"
        *goto resolve_weapon_attack_choice
  *if (has_ice_arrow)

      # [b][ACTION][/b] [b][Shoot: Ice Arrow][/b] Loose it at your target.@{show_stat_hints  [Shot: your bow attack plus 1d4 cold, and the target is slowed]|}

      *comment combat_player_moves_left, not the action, so a player who's exactly one tier out can push forward
      *comment AND still attack the same turn once Engaged, same as real 5e letting you move up to your speed and
      *comment still take your action. Closes exactly one range tier, not straight to Engaged -- a fight that opens
      *comment someone at Mid-Range or Long Range still costs multiple moves to close (Dash, below, grants a

      *comment before the step, while the enemy is still in reach; the swing lands after the move is narrated. With nothing farther to close on, the engaged enemy is the one the step is narrated against.
      *if (not(combat_pushpast_locked)) or (not(locked_combat_pushpast_page_id = page_id))

      *comment The tier change itself is GLOBAL (combat_shift_all_ranges) -- stepping toward one enemy in a

    # Fall back ${combat_fall_phrase} (${combat_fall_from_name} → ${combat_fall_to_name})
      *comment Symmetric with Push Forward just above: free movement (2026-09-28), spends combat_player_moves_left
      *comment not the action -- 5e's own speed budget doesn't care which direction you move in. Opens exactly one
      *comment range tier, not straight to Long Range. Distinct from "Break off and run" below, which ends the

      *comment
      *comment OPPORTUNITY ATTACKS (2026-09-28). This used to say "still no Attack of Opportunity (out of scope)".
      *comment Falling back is precisely the trigger 5e defines -- you leave a creature's reach -- so it is now the
      *comment FIRST place the rule bites, and "retreat is free" stops being true. Order matters: the candidate has
      *comment to be chosen while everyone is still at their pre-move range (find_opportunity_attacker's own
      *comment header explains why), which is why this runs BEFORE the shift rather than after.

      *comment Symmetric with Push Forward: the tier change is GLOBAL (combat_shift_all_ranges), for the same
      *comment reason -- backing off from one enemy is backing off from all of them, and the nearest eligible one
      *comment is still what the epithet below names, so the narration is unchanged.

      *gosub fight_sync_target_in
      *if (not(combat_move_opens))
        *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_range_name combat_enemy_range
        You turn back toward ${combat_enemy_epithet}, closing the gap.

        *line_break
        [b][🏃 You Fall Back — Long Range][/b]

        *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_range_name combat_enemy_range
        You give ground, putting distance between yourself and ${combat_enemy_epithet}.
        *line_break
        [b][🏃 You Fall Back — ${combat_range_display_name}][/b]
      *line_break
      *comment The opportunity attack lands AFTER the retreat is narrated, so the player sees the consequence of
      *comment their own move rather than an attack appearing before the thing that caused it. No-op when
      *comment find_opportunity_attacker found nobody, or when Disengage covered the round.
      *gosub resolve_opportunity_attack
      *goto fight_move_spent

  *comment Slowed (an enemy's Ray of Frost, 3.6 step 2): no Dash until the player has taken their next turn, as for an enemy.
  *if ((((((combat_player_actions_left > 0) or (combat_cunning)) and (not(combat_player_on_difficult_terrain))) and (combat_player_restrained <= 0)) and (((combat_player_slowed <= 0) and (combat_player_pinned <= 0)) and (combat_player_hobbled <= 0))) and ((not(combat_player_stood_round = combat_round)) and (not((combat_steady_round > 0) and (combat_steady_round = combat_round)))))
    # [b][@{combat_cunning BONUS ACTION|ACTION}][/b] [b][Dash][/b] Push yourself for extra ground this turn@{combat_cunning .|, giving up anything else.}
      *comment Dash, RAW (2026-09-28): spends the action, grants ONE extra combat_player_moves_left, so a player
      *comment starting more than one tier out can close/open two tiers this turn -- at the cost of the action, so
      *comment no attack (or spell, or item) alongside it. Doesn't move by itself -- Push Forward/Fall Back still
      *comment do the actual moving, this just buys a second one. Always offered regardless of current range: worth
      *comment it even from Engaged, to Fall Back twice and put real distance between you and a melee threat.
      *gosub combat_grant_move
      *if (combat_cunning)

    *comment Tagged "ranged" in tools/spell_book.json, but deliberately NOT wired to spell_range_of's penalty
    *comment and deliberately NOT gated on reach: Vicious Mockery resolves as a Wisdom SAVE (below), so the

    *comment to gate. It stays in the catalog so the tag is visible to a future RAW "it must be within 60 feet"
    *comment implementation, not so this option behaves differently from the others.

      *comment has the full explanation) -- same bug shape, same one-action-per-page exclusivity. resolve_save

      *comment same index 0 -- also mutually exclusive with this by the same one-action-per-page reasoning), but

  *if (combat_can_dodge)
    # [b][ACTION][/b] [b][Dodge][/b] +5 AC until your next turn; you cannot move.
      *comment DODGE, RAW 2014 "Actions in Combat": +5 AC for the current turn, and you cannot move. Replaces
      *comment the homebrew "Brace" option, which was not a 5e action at all and was never documented as a
      *comment house rule; mechanically it was also worse, making ONE chosen enemy roll at disadvantage for a
      *comment whole action where this covers every attack from every enemy. Brace's own button text claimed it
      *comment "giv[es] ground" when it moved nothing, had no cost label, and printed no banner -- so it read as
      *comment a defensive option that did nothing, which is why it was never picked.
      *comment RAW's "you cannot move" half is enforced in the movement gates below (Push Forward / Fall Back /
      *comment Dash / Break off all test combat_can_move), so dodging genuinely costs mobility -- the trade that
      *comment makes it a decision rather than a free defensive pick.
      *comment No lock of its own needed, unlike the budget spends: `*set dodge_active true` is idempotent, so a
      *comment Stats-screen page replay re-running it changes nothing. fight_action_spent handles the action
      *comment cost with its own existing lock.
      *set dodge_active true
      *goto fight_action_spent

  *if (combat_can_intimidate)
    # [b][ACTION][/b] [b][Intimidate][/b] Shake ${combat_intim_epithet}'s nerve.@{show_stat_hints  [Intimidation (${combat_intim_dc_text}); success forces a morale check]|}
      *comment INTIMIDATE (2026-11-03, MORALE): an Intimidation check against combat_intimidate_dc (WIS, proficiency, resolve and the moment); on a success the target makes its morale save at
      *comment once (a steady one with advantage, a skittish one with disadvantage) and, if it breaks, runs or yields on its own turn. One try per
      *comment enemy per fight. This page is the player's own, not a chain of turns, so the morale dice are drawn once behind a page lock and passed
      *comment in (combat_morale_forced_a/_b): a refresh rolls the same.
      *temp cin_slot combat_active_target
      *if (not(combat_intimidate_locked)) or (not(locked_combat_intimidate_page_id = page_id))
        *rand combat_intimidate_a 1 20
        *rand combat_intimidate_b 1 20
        *set combat_intimidate_locked true
        *set locked_combat_intimidate_page_id page_id
      *set {(("combat_enemy" & cin_slot) & "_intimidated")} true
      *gosub_scene dice_engine roll_skill_check (combat_intim_dc + 0) "intimidation" "" "" "" "" ""
      *if (check_success)
        *gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_intimidate_success
        *line_break
        *set combat_morale_forced_a combat_intimidate_a
        *set combat_morale_forced_b combat_intimidate_b
        *gosub combat_morale_check "enemy" cin_slot "intimidated"
        *if (combat_morale_broke)
          [b][⚑ Its nerve is gone: it will run or yield on its turn][/b]
          *line_break
      *else
        *gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_intimidate_fail
        *line_break
        [b][😠 $!{combat_intim_epithet} is not impressed][/b]
        *line_break
      *goto fight_action_spent

      *comment HIDE (2026-10-01). Universal ACTION; a rogue's Cunning Action makes it a BONUS action (combat_cunning, above).
      *comment Success arms combat_player_hidden, which combat_hidden_attack_roll spends on the next attack roll. A

  *if (combat_can_disengage)
    # [b][@{combat_cunning BONUS ACTION|ACTION}][/b] [b][Disengage][/b] Move without provoking a strike for the rest of this round.
      *comment DISENGAGE (2026-09-28). 5e makes this a 3rd-level Battle Master maneuver; there is no Battle Master
      *comment in this game, and building a whole class-resource system for one maneuver is not worth it, so it is
      *comment a universal ACTION instead -- which drops straight into the existing budget (combat_player_actions_left)
      *comment and needs no new economy of any kind. The cost is the interesting part: retreat now provokes an
      *comment opportunity attack (Fall Back, and Break off below), so without this the answer to being caught in
      *comment melee is "you may leave, and eat a hit for it" with no way to decline. Now there is a real choice --
      *comment spend the action and leave clean, or keep the action and take your chances.
      *comment Covers the WHOLE round rather than one move, matching 5e's "your movement doesn't provoke opportunity
      *comment attacks" for the turn: Dash afterwards is still covered. Once per round, and the round boundary is
      *comment combat_begin_round, which clears it alongside the enemies' own reaction flags.
      *set combat_player_disengaged true
      You shift your weight off the line and square up, refusing to give anyone a clean angle on you.
      *line_break
      [b][🌀 Disengaged — nothing can strike you as you move for the rest of this round][/b]
      *line_break
      *if (combat_cunning)
        *goto fight_bonus_spent
      *goto fight_action_spent

  *if (combat_can_break_off)
    # Break off and run, out of the fight for good.
      *comment Renamed from "Break off and disengage" (2026-09-28) purely because the new Disengage action above
      *comment now owns that word and two different options answering to it is worse than a reword.
      *comment
      *comment No longer free (2026-09-28). It used to cost nothing at all -- no action, no movement -- and simply
      *comment jumped to fight_fled, which made running away strictly better than fighting. It now costs one
      *comment movement tier, like any other departure, and provokes an opportunity attack from anything that was
      *comment in reach: leaving reach is leaving reach whether you intend to keep fighting or not, and 5e is
      *comment explicit that it is. It is NOT suppressed by Disengage above -- that says your movement doesn't
      *comment provoke, and this is still movement -- so a player surrounded by melee has a real decision here:
      *comment eat the swing to escape, or accept you can't break contact on your own.
      *if (not(combat_player_disengaged))
        *gosub find_opportunity_attacker
      *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_shift_all_ranges "fallback"
      *label break_off_resolve_oa
      *gosub resolve_opportunity_attack
      *goto fight_fled

  # End your turn.
    *comment The only thing that actually ends the player's turn -- see THE TURN BUDGET above. Always offered,
    *comment whether or not anything is left to spend, so it stays correct as new resource types get added.
    *comment Resets combat_turn_advanced_this_page so next round's page-break gating starts fresh.
    *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page false
    *goto fight_end_player_turn

*comment THE GNARLED BRAMBLE HEART (the user, 2026-10-20): an ACTION under the HUD's Items fly-out, once a campaign day (bramble_heart_day), that raises 1d2 Blight Twigs as allies for the rest of the fight. Future unique combat items join that fly-out by using "Items: <name>" in their action label and an items_<name> entry in gen_action_icons.js. The Blight Twigs are ordinary ally-library creatures (blight_twig, combat_types.txt) seated into the next free ally slots on the player's own lane tile and added to the END of the turn order (so they act last this round and in their place after it). The count is rolled and the allies built inside one page lock, so a replay (the dossier closing) prints the same page and builds nothing twice. Fewer than the roll arrive if the ally slots run short.
*label fight_summon_blights
*temp fsb_roll 1
*rand fsb_roll 1 2
*temp fsb_free (combat_ally_slot_cap - combat_ally_count)
*if (fsb_roll > fsb_free)
  *set fsb_roll fsb_free
*set combat_summon_n fsb_roll
*temp fsb_i 0
*label fsb_loop

*comment Seats one summoned ally on the lane and in the turn order. Position: the player's tile. *comment THROWN ITEMS (2026-10-12): an item whose catalog effect type is "throw" (a grease flask) is thrown at an enemy, so its menu line comes here, not to fight_use_do. It costs the ACTION, works on a target up to Mid-Range away (the clicked enemy, else
*comment the one with the least health, else whoever is in range), and the flask is used up by resolve_throw_item (combat_spells.txt) under the combat_cast lock. Nothing is spent if nothing is in range.
*comment THROWN WEAPONS (2026-11-25, the user). A throw is a weapon attack with the thrown weapon's own numbers, so it goes through resolve_weapon_attack_choice like a swing: STR, or the better of STR and DEX
*comment for a Finesse weapon, for the roll and the damage, its own damage die, a ranged attack for the penalties (disadvantage while anyone is Engaged with you, and past the normal range). The copy thrown
*comment is the pack's while one is left; the last one goes from the hand, the sidearm's before the main weapon's, and when the main weapon goes the sidearm comes to hand (or bare fists). Nothing is
*comment left on the ground: each copy is counted (combat_thrown_<id>) and comes back on a win (fight_thrown_return). A flight, a rescue or a death gives nothing back (fight_cleanup zeroes the counts).

      The glass shatters, and the sticky fire hisses out across ${combat_throw_epithet}'s soaked shoulders.
  *else
    *if (combat_throw_v = 1)
      The flask breaks against ${combat_throw_epithet}, and the mixture meets the air and catches with a soft roar.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 2)
      It bursts on ${combat_throw_epithet}, and a sheet of amber flame runs over them, clinging to whatever it touches.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 3)
      The glass shatters, and the sticky fire spreads across ${combat_throw_epithet} before they can beat it out.
  *line_break
  *if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
    [b][@{combat_is_nonlethal 🏳️|💀} ${combat_throw_name} @{combat_is_nonlethal Beaten|Defeated}: ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} Fire damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}][/b]
  *else
    [b][🔥 Alchemist's Fire: Hit | ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} Fire damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}@{combat_throw_greased  (the grease caught, +2)|}][/b]
    *line_break
    *if (combat_throw_wet = "soaked")
      [b][💧 ${combat_throw_name} is Wet: the fire cannot take hold][/b]
    *else
      [b][🔥 ${combat_throw_name} is burning: 1d4 fire damage at the start of each of its next three turns][/b]
*else
  *if (combat_throw_patch = "doused")
    *if (combat_throw_v = 1)
      The flask goes wide and bursts on the ground beside ${combat_throw_epithet}, and the spilled fire smokes and hisses in the wet.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 2)
      ${combat_throw_name} ducks the throw, and the flask smashes at their feet. The mixture spits once on the wet ground and goes out.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 3)

  *elseif (combat_throw_patch = "")

      The flask goes wide and bursts on the ground, and the fire gutters out on bare stone.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 2)
      ${combat_throw_name} ducks the throw, and the flask burns itself out on the ground.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 3)

  *else
    *if (combat_throw_v = 1)
      The flask goes wide and bursts on the ground beside ${combat_throw_epithet}, and the mixture catches and spreads in a low, spitting sheet.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 2)
      ${combat_throw_name} ducks the throw, and the flask smashes at their feet. The mixture catches where it lands and keeps burning.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 3)

  *line_break
  [b][💨 Miss][/b]

*comment THE WATER JUG, THROWN (2026-10-19, the user's design, copying Alchemist's Fire's hit rule): a ranged attack, Dexterity + proficiency against AC, at Mid-Range, the whole jug's water flung at the target. No damage. A hit leaves the enemy Wet for two of its turns
*comment (combat_enemyN_wet): it cannot catch fire, resists fire, and takes double from cold and lightning (combat_dice.txt). A burning enemy cannot be soaked: the water goes to steam and it stays as it was. The water is spent hit or miss (jug_full - 1), the jug stays. Shares the
*comment cantrip replay lock because it costs the same action; the target is picked and ranged inside the lock.
*label fight_throw_water

  Nothing is close enough to throw at. Close the distance first.

  *goto fight_round_hub
*comment replay-ok: inside the cantrip replay lock below (fight_throw_water).
*set jug_full (jug_full - 1)
*set combat_throw_name combat_enemy_name

*rand combat_throw_v 1 3

  *set disadvantage true
  *set combat_reach_pinned true

*set combat_throw_wet ""
*if (check_success)
  *temp ftw_burning {(ftw_p & "burning")}
  *if (ftw_burning > 0)
    *set combat_throw_wet "steam"
  *else
    *set combat_throw_wet "soaked"
    *set {(ftw_p & "wet")} 2
*if (check_success)
  *if (combat_throw_wet = "steam")
    *if (combat_throw_v = 1)
      The water slaps into the flames on ${combat_throw_epithet} and flashes to steam, and the fire rolls on through it.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 2)
      You fling the water, and it hisses away to a white cloud before it can soak anything. The fire on ${combat_throw_epithet} burns on.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 3)
      A sheet of water hits ${combat_throw_epithet}, and the flames drink it down in a gout of steam.
    *line_break
    [b][🔥 Water Jug: Hit | ${combat_throw_name} is burning too fiercely to be soaked][/b]
  *else
    *if (combat_throw_v = 1)

      The water takes ${combat_throw_epithet} full in the chest and soaks them to the skin, streaming down onto the ground.
    *if (combat_throw_v = 3)
      You swing the jug and the water leaves it in one cold arc, drenching ${combat_throw_epithet} from head to foot.
    *line_break
    [b][💧 Water Jug: Hit | ${combat_throw_name} is Wet for 2 turns: it cannot catch fire, resists fire, and takes double cold and lightning damage][/b]
*else
  *if (combat_throw_v = 1)
    The water goes wide and slaps onto the ground, leaving a dark stain and ${combat_throw_epithet} dry.
  *if (combat_throw_v = 2)
    ${combat_throw_name} twists aside, and the whole gallon spatters across the dirt.
  *if (combat_throw_v = 3)

  *line_break
  [b][💨 Miss][/b]
*line_break
*goto fight_action_spent

*comment The scroll's target, worked out as the spell's is on the hub page (the clicked foe when it qualifies, otherwise the weakest that does), into combat_read_hp_slot.
*comment Its own scan, since the hub's runs only for a character who knows the spell and after the scroll checks.
*label fight_read_hold_person_target

  *set frhp_reach 3
