*temp frhp_chosen false
*temp frhp_i 1
*label fight_read_hold_person_target_loop
*if (frhp_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *temp frhp_p (("combat_enemy" & frhp_i) & "_")
  *temp frhp_hp {(frhp_p & "hp")}
  *temp frhp_kind {(frhp_p & "creature_type")}
  *temp frhp_now {(frhp_p & "paralyzed")}
  *temp frhp_rng {(frhp_p & "range")}
  *if ((((frhp_hp > 0) and (frhp_kind = "humanoid")) and (frhp_now <= 0)) and (frhp_rng <= frhp_reach))
    *if (frhp_i = combat_player_chosen_target)
      *set combat_read_hp_slot frhp_i
      *set frhp_chosen true
    *elseif ((not(frhp_chosen)) and ((combat_read_hp_slot = 0) or (frhp_hp < frhp_best_hp)))
      *set combat_read_hp_slot frhp_i
      *set frhp_best_hp frhp_hp
  *set frhp_i + 1
  *goto fight_read_hold_person_target_loop
*return

*comment so it ends at fight_action_spent; the others check for a kill. Scorching Ray: each ray is a dart on the enemy it is
*comment aimed at, and the rays can be split or stacked (fight_resolve_scorching_ray). Cause Fear's 2nd level: a dart only marks

*comment AN ATTACK ROLL PER PROJECTILE (2026-11-17), for a spell whose projectiles the player shares out on the targeting page
*comment (fight_target_multi, combat_mm_darts_1..6). Parameter: the spell id. Every projectile is its own ranged spell attack and
*comment its own hit, in slot order. The name and damage type come from the spell data (spell_facts_load); a hit goes through
*comment resolve_spell_hit, so Hex and the other riders land on each projectile that hits (RAW). The ranged penalty, terrain,
*comment cover and a hidden caster's advantage are worked out per projectile, the same calls Fire Bolt makes. The page's attack
*comment and damage locks are opened before each one so each rolls its own dice; inside a fight the page replay guard
*comment (pageReplayGuard, web/scene.js) is what keeps a refresh from rolling again. A projectile aimed at a foe an earlier one
*comment already dropped is spent. The last banner's line break is printed only if the fight goes on (fight_victory opens with
*comment its own). The caller spends the slot and prints the opening; a new spell here adds its banner word and icon below.
*label fight_resolve_projectile_attacks
*params frpa_id
*gosub_scene combat_spells spell_facts_load frpa_id
*temp frpa_name spell_fact_name
*temp frpa_type spell_fact_dmg_type
*temp frpa_word "Ray"
*temp frpa_icon "🔥"
*if (frpa_id = "eldritch_blast")
  *set frpa_word "Beam"
  *set frpa_icon "✨"
*temp frpa_keep combat_active_target
*temp frpa_any false
*temp frpa_i 1
*label fight_resolve_projectile_attacks_slot_loop
*if (frpa_i <= combat_enemy_slot_cap)
  *temp frpa_n {(("combat_mm_darts_" & frpa_i))}
  *temp frpa_j 1
  *label fight_resolve_projectile_attacks_one_loop
  *if (frpa_j <= frpa_n)
    *set combat_active_target frpa_i
    *gosub fight_sync_target_in
    *if (frpa_any)
      *line_break
    *set frpa_any true
    *if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
      [b][💨 ${frpa_word} at ${combat_enemy_name}: already down][/b]
    *else
      *set roll_locked false
      *set combat_player_hit_locked false
      *set check_stat casting_stat
      *set check_dc combat_enemy_ac
      *set check_skill frpa_name
      *set check_add_prof_bonus true
      *gosub spell_range_of
      *gosub combat_apply_player_ranged_terrain
      *if (spell_penalty_result)
        *set disadvantage true
        *set combat_reach_pinned true
      *set combat_attack_tag frpa_type
      *gosub combat_hidden_attack_roll
      *gosub_scene dice_engine roll_d20_check
      *if (check_success)
        *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_spell_hit frpa_id true
        *gosub fight_sync_target_out
        *set combat_repel_result 0
        *if (frpa_id = "eldritch_blast")
          *gosub fight_repelling_blast
        *if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
          *set combat_current_spell_id frpa_id
          *gosub_scene combat_prose pick_spell_death_flavor
          ${fdh}
          *line_break
          [b][@{combat_is_nonlethal 🏳️|💀} ${combat_enemy_name} @{combat_is_nonlethal Beaten|Defeated}: ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} $!{frpa_type} damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}][/b]
        *else
          [b][${frpa_icon} ${frpa_word} at ${combat_enemy_name}: Hit | ${combat_player_dmg_dealt} $!{frpa_type} damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}][/b]
          *if (combat_repel_result = 1)
            *line_break
            [b][💥 Repelling Blast: ${combat_enemy_name} is driven back to ${combat_repel_name}][/b]
      *else
        [b][💨 ${frpa_word} at ${combat_enemy_name}: Miss][/b]
    *set frpa_j + 1
    *goto fight_resolve_projectile_attacks_one_loop
  *set frpa_i + 1
  *goto fight_resolve_projectile_attacks_slot_loop
*if (frpa_keep > 0)
  *set combat_active_target frpa_keep
  *gosub fight_sync_target_in
*gosub fight_check_all_dead
*if (not(combat_all_enemies_dead))
  *line_break
*return

        *if (combat_arrow_v = 3)
          The burning cord catches on ${combat_enemy_epithet} and flares higher.
        *line_break
        *if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
          [b][🔥 Fire Arrow: +${combat_arrow_dealt} Fire damage${combat_arrow_suffix} | @{combat_is_nonlethal 🏳️|💀} ${combat_enemy_name} @{combat_is_nonlethal Beaten|Defeated}][/b]
        *else
          [b][🔥 Fire Arrow: +${combat_arrow_dealt} Fire damage${combat_arrow_suffix}@{combat_arrow_grease  (the grease caught, +2)|}][/b]
          *if (combat_arrow_burn)
            *line_break
            [b][🔥 ${combat_enemy_name} is burning: 1d4 fire damage at the start of each of its next two turns][/b]
      *else
        *if (combat_arrow_v = 1)
          The frost-flint cracks on impact, and a pale rime spreads out from the shaft.
        *if (combat_arrow_v = 2)
          Cold bites out of the arrowhead, and frost whitens ${combat_enemy_epithet} around the shaft.
        *if (combat_arrow_v = 3)
          The arrowhead shatters into white splinters, and ice crackles across ${combat_enemy_epithet}.
        *line_break
        *if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
          [b][❄️ Ice Arrow: +${combat_arrow_dealt} Cold damage${combat_arrow_suffix} | @{combat_is_nonlethal 🏳️|💀} ${combat_enemy_name} @{combat_is_nonlethal Beaten|Defeated}][/b]
        *else
          [b][❄️ Ice Arrow: +${combat_arrow_dealt} Cold damage${combat_arrow_suffix} | ⚠️ Slowed — ${combat_enemy_epithet} can't Dash on its next turn][/b]
  *else
    *line_break
    *if (combat_arrow_used = "fire")
      *if (combat_arrow_v = 1)
        The burning arrow flies wide and burns itself out where it lands.
      *if (combat_arrow_v = 2)
        The flame streams past ${combat_enemy_epithet} as they sidestep the arrow.
      *if (combat_arrow_v = 3)
        The arrow hisses past, and its flame gutters out.
    *else
      *if (combat_arrow_v = 1)
        The arrow flies wide, and its frosted head shatters where it lands.
      *if (combat_arrow_v = 2)
        The arrow skims past ${combat_enemy_epithet}, and a thin trail of frost hangs in the air.
      *if (combat_arrow_v = 3)
        The arrow misses, and the cold bleeds out of its head.
*if ((check_success) and (combat_ensnare_result > 0))
  *line_break
  Thorned vines burst from the blow and lash at ${combat_enemy_epithet}.
  *line_break
  ${combat_ensnare_banner}
  *line_break
  *if (not(combat_ensnare_banner2 = ""))
    [b][⚖️ ${combat_enemy_name} is ${combat_enemy_size}: advantage on the save, a second roll][/b]
    *line_break
    ${combat_ensnare_banner2}
    *line_break
  *if (combat_ensnare_result = 2)
    [b][🌿 ${combat_enemy_name} is restrained: 1d6 piercing at the start of each of its turns, attacks against it have advantage, until it tears free (up to 10 rounds)][/b]
  *else
    [b][💨 ${combat_enemy_name} tears clear of the vines][/b]
*if ((check_success) and (combat_maneuver_result > 0))
  *line_break
  *if (combat_maneuver_result = 3)
    $!{combat_enemy_epithet} is too massive to topple.
    *line_break
    [b][🦶 ${combat_enemy_name} is too big to trip][/b]
  *else
    ${combat_maneuver_banner}
    *line_break
    *if (combat_maneuver_used = "trip")
      *if (combat_maneuver_result = 2)
        *if (combat_maneuver_v = 1)
          The blow takes ${combat_enemy_epithet}'s legs out, and they go down hard.
        *else
          $!{combat_enemy_epithet} stumbles, loses their footing and crashes to the ground.
        *line_break
        [b][🦶 ${combat_enemy_name} is knocked Prone][/b]
      *else
        *if (combat_maneuver_v = 1)
          $!{combat_enemy_epithet} staggers but catches their balance.
        *else
          $!{combat_enemy_epithet} lurches sideways and stays upright.
        *line_break
        [b][💨 ${combat_enemy_name} keeps their feet][/b]
    *else
      *if (combat_maneuver_result = 2)
        *if (combat_maneuver_v = 1)
          $!{combat_enemy_epithet} flinches back, eyes wide.
        *else
          $!{combat_enemy_epithet} recoils from you, shaken.
        *line_break
        [b][😨 ${combat_enemy_name} is Frightened of you until the end of your next turn][/b]
      *else
        *if (combat_maneuver_v = 1)
          $!{combat_enemy_epithet} shakes off the menace and comes on.
        *else
          $!{combat_enemy_epithet} grits their teeth and holds their ground.
        *line_break
        [b][💪 ${combat_enemy_name} holds their nerve][/b]
*comment The attack's last banner ends here with a break, unless this blow ended the fight: fight_victory opens with its own break, so a second one would print an empty line. The sync and the dead check are
*comment repeated by fight_check_enemy_hp below and change nothing a second time.
*gosub fight_sync_target_out
*gosub fight_check_all_dead
*if (not(combat_all_enemies_dead))
  *line_break
*comment The one and only entry point for both weapon and sidearm attacks -- flagging it here, right before the

*comment instead of an action for this specific one.
*set combat_arrow_kind ""
*set combat_pending_spend_is_attack true

*comment The break under a damage option's last banner, unless the blow ended the fight: fight_victory opens with its own break,

*label fight_check_enemy_hp
*comment Shared tail for every damage-dealing option (weapon/sidearm attacks AND every damage spell/cantrip).
*comment combat_pending_spend_is_attack is set true by resolve_weapon_attack_choice alone, just above, right
*comment before it *goto's here -- read and reset immediately so it can never leak into whatever reaches this
*comment label next (a spell, which must still spend the general action, not an attack).
*temp fceh_is_attack combat_pending_spend_is_attack
*set combat_pending_spend_is_attack false

*temp fceh_offhand combat_offhand_on
*gosub fight_undead_fortitude

*if (combat_all_enemies_dead)
  *goto fight_victory
*comment A throw is over and the fight goes on: the hands go back to how fight_throw_weapon worked out they end up (the thrown copy gone). Here, after the dead check and Undead Fortitude, and not
*comment in resolve_weapon_attack_choice: a call into another scene closes the open paragraph, and a win's fight_victory opens with the break that ends the last banner's line (fight_thrown_return does it then).
*comment The off-hand attack puts the hands back the same way (fight_offhand_attack).
*if ((combat_throw_on) or (combat_offhand_on))
  *set combat_throw_on false
  *set combat_offhand_on false

  *set equipped_sidearm_id combat_throw_side_after
  *gosub_scene equipment apply_weapon_loadout
  *gosub_scene equipment apply_sidearm_loadout
  *gosub_scene equipment recalculate_armor_class
*comment WEB BURNS (3.4): fire this option dealt to a webbed enemy burns the patch, and that fire can finish the fight.
*gosub_scene combat_spells web_burn_check "player"
*comment An enemy's Armor of Agathys answering the player's melee hit on this page (resolve_player_weapon_hit) can drop them too (3.9a 5).
*gosub_scene combat_spells enemy_agathys_print "player"
*if (combat_enemy_agathys_page = page_id)
  *if (combat_player_can_die) and (hp_current <= 0)
    *goto fight_player_died
  *if (not(combat_player_can_die)) and (hp_current <= 1)
    *goto fight_rescued
*comment An enemy's Web burning can catch the player in its fire (3.9a 5).
*if ((combat_web_burned) and (combat_foe_web_burn_hit_player))

  *gosub fight_check_all_dead
  *if (combat_all_enemies_dead)
    *goto fight_victory
*gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_reconcile
*gosub_scene combat_movement fight_pick_target
*if (combat_dual_shot_active)
  *comment Dual Shot: the first shot is done. Start the second one, on its own page, at the second target. The shot spends the one attack once the
  *comment second shot comes back through here (the second pass has combat_dual_second_slot cleared and falls through to the spend below).
  *if (combat_dual_second_slot > 0)
    *temp fceh_dual_slot combat_dual_second_slot
    *temp fceh_dual_hp {(("combat_enemy" & fceh_dual_slot) & "_hp")}
    *set combat_dual_second_slot 0
    *if (fceh_dual_hp > 0)
      *set combat_player_chosen_target fceh_dual_slot
      *page_break Loose the second shot...
      *goto resolve_weapon_attack_choice
  *set combat_dual_shot_active false
  *set combat_player_chosen_target 0
*if (fceh_offhand)
  *goto fight_bonus_spent
*if (fceh_is_attack)
  *goto fight_attack_spent
*goto fight_action_spent

*comment UNDEAD FORTITUDE (2026-10-31, the user: RAW). When the player's damage drops an enemy holding the undead_fortitude trait (the zombie type's default ability) to 0 HP it makes a Constitution save with a DC of 5 + the damage taken, and on a success stays up at 1 HP instead. It is skipped when the damage is radiant or the
*comment attack roll was a natural 20 (combat_hit_nat20; the engine has no extra critical-hit damage, so a natural 20 is the critical hit here). It triggers every time the zombie reaches 0 HP. Called from fight_check_enemy_hp, the shared tail of every player damage option, before the HP is
*comment synced out, so the one hook covers weapons, sidearms and every damage spell. NOT covered yet: damage from an ally, a thrown item or a start-of-turn effect (they do not pass through that tail). The save is guarded by a once-per-page lock; the one line of text it prints is combat_fort_banner.
*label fight_undead_fortitude_roll
*if (not(combat_fort_locked)) or (not(locked_combat_fort_page_id = page_id))
  *set combat_fort_banner ""
  *set combat_fort_shown false
  *comment The rule belongs to whoever holds the undead_fortitude trait (3.4a step 5; the zombie type's default), not to the zombie type itself.
  *gosub_scene combat_abilities ability_has_trait combat_active_target "undead_fortitude"
  *if ((ability_has_trait_result) and (combat_enemy_hp <= 0))
    *if ((combat_hit_nat20) or ((combat_player_dmg_type = "radiant") or (combat_player_extra_dmg_type = "radiant")))
      *set combat_fort_banner "[b][💀 Undead Fortitude cannot save ${combat_enemy_epithet} from that blow.][/b]"
    *else
      *temp uf_dc (5 + combat_player_dmg_dealt)
      *gosub_scene startup score_to_mod combat_enemy_constitution
      *temp uf_mod sm_mod
      *temp uf_roll 0
      *rand uf_roll 1 20
      *temp uf_total (uf_roll + uf_mod)
      *if (uf_total >= uf_dc)
        *set combat_enemy_hp 1
        *set combat_fort_banner "[b][💀 Undead Fortitude: ${combat_enemy_epithet} makes a Constitution save (DC ${uf_dc}, total ${uf_total}) and keeps standing with 1 HP.][/b]"
      *else
        *set combat_fort_banner "[b][💀 Undead Fortitude: ${combat_enemy_epithet} fails a Constitution save (DC ${uf_dc}, total ${uf_total}).][/b]"
  *set combat_hit_nat20 false
  *set combat_fort_locked true
  *set locked_combat_fort_page_id page_id
*return

*comment The tail's call: the save for any damage source that did not roll it earlier (a spell with its own damage code), and the line of text if no hit banner has carried it yet.
*label fight_undead_fortitude
*gosub fight_undead_fortitude_roll
*if ((not(combat_fort_banner = "")) and (not(combat_fort_shown)))
  *set combat_fort_shown true
  ${combat_fort_banner}
  *line_break
*return

*label combat_ensnare_apply
*comment ENSNARING STRIKE (2026-10-12): called inside the weapon-hit lock right after the hit is resolved. If the spell is armed and still running, it is spent on this hit whatever happens; a target still standing makes a Strength save against the player's spell save DC and, on a failure, is restrained
*comment (a shared condition: restrained 1, its escape DC, and the spell as its source) until the spell ends (its last round, ensnaring_expires_round, ends the concentration) or until it tears free. The save banner is kept (combat_save_quiet) and printed by the attack's report; check_success is the ATTACK's result and is put back, because the report that follows reads it.
*set combat_ensnare_result 0
*if (ensnaring_armed)
  *set ensnaring_armed false
  *if ((ensnaring_expires_round >= combat_round) and (combat_enemy_hp > 0))
    *temp cea_cs check_success
    *temp cea_p (("combat_enemy" & combat_active_target) & "_")
    *set combat_save_quiet true
    *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_save cea_p "strength" "Strength" spell_save_dc 0
    *set combat_save_quiet false
    *set combat_ensnare_banner {("combat_save_banner_0")}
    *set combat_ensnare_banner2 ""
    *comment The spell's own size rule: a Large or larger creature has advantage on the save, so a failure is rolled again (on the target's own save lock, so it is a fresh d20) and the better of the two stands.
    *gosub combat_size_rank combat_enemy_size
    *if ((check_success) and (combat_size_rank_value >= 3))
      *set combat_save_quiet true
      *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_save cea_p "strength" "Strength" spell_save_dc combat_active_target
      *set combat_save_quiet false
      *set combat_ensnare_banner2 {(("combat_save_banner_" & combat_active_target))}
    *set combat_ensnare_result 1
    *if (check_success)
      *set combat_ensnare_result 2
      *gosub_scene combat_dice condition_apply cea_p "restrained" 1 spell_save_dc "combat_player_ensnaring_strike"
    *set check_success cea_cs
  *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_reconcile
*return

*label combat_maneuver_apply
*comment MANEUVERS (the Battle Master, 2026-11-21): called inside the weapon-hit lock right after the hit, like combat_ensnare_apply. resolve_player_weapon_hit has already spent the
*comment die and added it to the damage (combat_maneuver_used names the maneuver). A target still standing saves against 8 + proficiency + the higher of STR and DEX mods:
*comment Trip Attack, Strength, or Prone (no save for Huge or larger: it cannot be tripped); Menacing Attack, Wisdom, or Frightened of the player until the end of the
*comment player's next turn (1 round, no repeat save: condition_tick ends it at the end of the foe's own next turn, which always falls before the player's next turn ends).
*comment The save banner is kept (combat_save_quiet) for the attack's report; check_success is the ATTACK's result and is put back.
*set combat_maneuver_result 0
*if (combat_maneuver_used = "")
  *return
*if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
  *return

*temp cma_p (("combat_enemy" & combat_active_target) & "_")
*temp cma_mod str_mod
*if (dex_mod > cma_mod)
  *set cma_mod dex_mod
*temp cma_dc ((8 + prof_bonus) + cma_mod)
*rand combat_maneuver_v 1 2
*if (combat_maneuver_used = "trip")
  *gosub combat_size_rank combat_enemy_size
  *if (combat_size_rank_value >= 4)
    *set combat_maneuver_result 3
  *else
    *set combat_save_quiet true
    *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_save cma_p "strength" "Strength" cma_dc 0
    *set combat_save_quiet false
    *set combat_maneuver_banner {("combat_save_banner_0")}
    *set combat_maneuver_result 1
    *if (check_success)
      *set combat_maneuver_result 2
      *gosub_scene combat_dice prone_apply cma_p
*else
  *set combat_save_quiet true
  *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_save cma_p "wisdom" "Wisdom" cma_dc 0
  *set combat_save_quiet false
  *set combat_maneuver_banner {("combat_save_banner_0")}
  *set combat_maneuver_result 1
  *if (check_success)
    *set combat_maneuver_result 2
    *gosub_scene combat_dice condition_apply cma_p "frightened" 1 0 "combat_player_menacing_attack"
*set check_success cma_cs
*return

*comment SPECIAL ARROWS (2026-10-15, Fire Arrow and Ice Arrow). Called inside the weapon-hit lock right after the weapon's own hit is resolved. combat_arrow_used is the arrow loosed ("fire", "ice" or ""). On a hit that leaves the target standing it adds 1d4 of its
*comment element as a second damage packet (resistances and vulnerabilities apply to that packet alone; fire is +2 on a greased target, which burns well) and a rider: fire sets the target burning for two of its turns (or keeps a longer burn it already
*comment has), ice slows it (the same Slowed as Ray of Frost, combat_enemyN_slowed). The weapon hit's own damage, type and suffix are kept and put back, since the report below reads them. Everything is stored in combat_arrow_* for the replay.
*label combat_arrow_apply
*if (combat_arrow_used = "")
  *return
*if (not(check_success))
  *return
*if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
  *return
*temp caa_p (("combat_enemy" & combat_active_target) & "_")
*temp caa_main combat_player_dmg_dealt
*temp caa_type combat_player_dmg_type
*temp caa_suffix combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix
*temp caa_die 1
*rand caa_die 1 4
*if (combat_arrow_used = "fire")
  *temp caa_greased {(caa_p & "greased")}
  *comment Standing on a Grease patch counts too (3.9a, flammable as in BG3).
  *temp gat_pos_4 {(caa_p & "position")}
  *gosub_scene combat_spells grease_at gat_pos_4
  *if (combat_grease_here)
    *set caa_greased 1
  *if (caa_greased > 0)
    *set caa_die + 2
    *set combat_arrow_grease true
  *set combat_player_dmg_type "fire"
*else
  *set combat_player_dmg_type "cold"
*set combat_player_dmg_dealt caa_die
*gosub_scene combat_dice apply_outgoing_damage_resist_vuln
*set combat_arrow_dealt combat_player_dmg_dealt
*set combat_arrow_suffix combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix
*set combat_player_dmg_dealt caa_main
*set combat_player_dmg_type caa_type
*set combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix caa_suffix
*set combat_enemy_hp - combat_arrow_dealt
*if (combat_enemy_hp < 0)
  *set combat_enemy_hp 0
*gosub fight_sync_target_out
*if (combat_enemy_hp > 0)
  *if (combat_arrow_used = "fire")
    *temp caa_burn {(caa_p & "burning")}
    *temp caa_wet {(caa_p & "wet")}
    *if (caa_wet <= 0)
      *if (caa_burn < 2)
        *set {(caa_p & "burning")} 2
      *set combat_arrow_burn true
  *else
    *gosub_scene combat_dice condition_apply caa_p "slowed" 1 0 ""
    *set combat_arrow_slow true
*return

*label combat_ally_restrain_turn
*comment RESTRAINED, ally side (3.4, 2026-11-18): called on a held ally's turn in place of its usual one (no movement at all). A ranged ally, or a melee ally with a foe on its own tile, attacks from the spot,
*comment with disadvantage (resolve_ally_attack). Otherwise it spends the turn on a Strength check against the stored escape DC (restrained_dc). The check, its DC and its prose variant are stored against the page id,
*comment so a replay prints the same result (a success has already cleared the condition and its DC by then).
*params cart_slot
*temp cart_p (("combat_ally" & cart_slot) & "_")
*set combat_attacker_name {(cart_p & "name")}
*set combat_attacker_epithet {(cart_p & "epithet")}
*temp cart_weapon {(cart_p & "weapon_type")}
*temp cart_pos {(cart_p & "position")}
*gosub_scene combat_ai select_ally_attack_target cart_slot
*temp cart_target combat_active_target
*temp cart_in_reach false
*if (cart_target > 0)
  *if (cart_weapon = "ranged")
    *set cart_in_reach true
  *else
    *temp cart_tpos {(("combat_enemy" & cart_target) & "_position")}
    *if (cart_tpos = cart_pos)
      *set cart_in_reach true
*if (cart_in_reach)
  *set {(cart_p & "target")} cart_target
  *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_ally_attack cart_slot cart_target
  *set combat_ally_break_deferred true
  *gosub_scene combat_prose fight_narrate_ally_attack "ally" cart_slot "enemy" cart_target combat_ally_atk_hit
  *set combat_ally_break_deferred false
  *set combat_ally_atk_used_sidearm false
  *line_break
  *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
  *return
*comment PINNED (3.3) with no foe in reach: it holds where it stands, with no Strength check (nothing holds it but the wound).
*if (({(cart_p & "pinned")} > 0) and ({(cart_p & "restrained")} <= 0))
  [b][📌 ${combat_attacker_name} is Pinned — holds its ground][/b]
  *line_break
  *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
  *return
*temp cart_page {(cart_p & "restrain_page")}
*temp cart_dc 0
*temp cart_res 0
*temp cart_roll 0
*temp cart_v 1
*if (cart_page = page_id)
  *set cart_res {(cart_p & "restrain_res")}
  *set cart_roll {(cart_p & "restrain_roll")}
  *set cart_v {(cart_p & "restrain_v")}
  *set cart_dc {(cart_p & "restrain_dc")}
*else
  *set cart_dc {(cart_p & "restrained_dc")}
  *rand cart_v 1 3
  *if ({(cart_p & "restrained_src")} = "net")
    *set cart_v (cart_v + 3)
  *temp cart_str {(cart_p & "strength")}
  *gosub_scene startup score_to_mod cart_str
  *rand cart_roll 1 20
  *comment Off-Balance and Poisoned (3.3): the Strength check is made with disadvantage.
  *if (({(cart_p & "off_balance")} > 0) or ({(cart_p & "poisoned")} > 0))
    *temp cart_roll_b 0
    *rand cart_roll_b 1 20
    *if (cart_roll_b < cart_roll)
      *set cart_roll cart_roll_b
  *set cart_roll (cart_roll + sm_mod)
  *set cart_res 1
  *if (cart_roll >= cart_dc)
    *set cart_res 2
  *set {(cart_p & "restrain_res")} cart_res
  *set {(cart_p & "restrain_roll")} cart_roll
  *set {(cart_p & "restrain_v")} cart_v
  *set {(cart_p & "restrain_dc")} cart_dc
  *set {(cart_p & "restrain_page")} page_id
  *if (cart_res = 2)
    *gosub_scene combat_dice condition_end cart_p "restrained"
*gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_ally_break_free cart_v cart_res
*line_break
*if (cart_res = 2)
  [b][⛓️ ${combat_attacker_name} breaks free (Strength check ${cart_roll} vs DC ${cart_dc}) — turn spent][/b]
*else
  [b][⛓️ ${combat_attacker_name} fails to break free (Strength check ${cart_roll} vs DC ${cart_dc}) — turn spent][/b]
*line_break
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*label combat_restrain_tick
*comment RESTRAINED (2026-10-12, Ensnaring Strike): called at the start of the enemy's turn while it is held. It takes 1d6 piercing from the thorns (resistances applied). Then, if it means to move this turn, it spends its action on a Strength check against the restraint's stored DC (restrained_dc) to tear free (a success or a miss, the turn is
*comment spent); if it only wanted to fight where it stands, it attacks with disadvantage instead. Stored per slot against the page id, so a replay prints the same tick and never rolls twice. Sets combat_restrain_stop when the turn is used up, and combat_burn_killed when the thorns finish the enemy.
*params crt_slot crt_wants
*set combat_restrain_stop false
*set combat_burn_killed false
*temp crt_p (("combat_enemy" & crt_slot) & "_")
*temp crt_page {(crt_p & "restrain_page")}
*temp crt_res 0
*temp crt_dealt 0
*temp crt_roll 0
*temp crt_v 1
*temp crt_dc 0
*comment Held by a Web (the spell) rather than Ensnaring's vines: no thorns, and its own lines. Cached with the tick, since tearing free clears the source.
*temp crt_web false
*if (crt_page = page_id)
  *set crt_dc {(crt_p & "restrain_dc")}
  *set crt_res {(crt_p & "restrain_res")}
  *set crt_dealt {(crt_p & "restrain_dealt")}
  *set crt_roll {(crt_p & "restrain_roll")}
  *set crt_v {(crt_p & "restrain_v")}
  *set crt_web {(crt_p & "restrain_web")}
*else
  *comment The escape DC was stored when the restraint landed. Cached with the tick's other results, since tearing free clears the condition (and its DC) before the banner prints.
  *set crt_dc {(crt_p & "restrained_dc")}
  *comment The player's Web or an enemy's (3.9a 5): both hold without thorns.
  *temp crt_src {(crt_p & "restrained_src")}
  *gosub_scene combat_spells web_src_test crt_src
  *set crt_web combat_web_src_is
  *rand crt_v 1 3
  *comment The thorns' die and damage type come from the Ensnaring Strike data (tools/spell_book.json).
  *gosub_scene combat_spells spell_facts_load "ensnaring_strike"
  *comment Quicktest walks this body without running the fact lookup (sides 0); *rand needs max above min, so the range is clamped to at least 2.
  *temp crt_sides spell_fact_dice_sides
  *if (crt_sides < 2)
    *set crt_sides 2
  *temp crt_die 1
  *rand crt_die 1 crt_sides
  *set combat_player_dmg_type spell_fact_dmg_type
  *set combat_player_dmg_dealt crt_die
  *gosub_scene combat_dice apply_outgoing_damage_resist_vuln
  *set crt_dealt combat_player_dmg_dealt
  *if (crt_web)
    *set crt_dealt 0
  *set combat_enemy_hp - crt_dealt
  *if (combat_enemy_hp < 0)
    *set combat_enemy_hp 0
  *gosub fight_sync_target_out
  *if (combat_enemy_hp > 0)
    *if (crt_wants)
      *temp crt_str {(crt_p & "strength")}
      *gosub_scene startup score_to_mod crt_str
      *rand crt_roll 1 20
      *comment Hex on its Strength: the check is rolled twice and the lower kept.
      *gosub hex_check_disadv crt_slot "strength"
      *if (combat_hex_check_dis)
        *temp crt_roll2 1
        *rand crt_roll2 1 20
        *if (crt_roll2 < crt_roll)
          *set crt_roll crt_roll2
      *set crt_roll (crt_roll + sm_mod)
      *if (crt_roll >= crt_dc)
        *set crt_res 2
        *gosub_scene combat_dice condition_end crt_p "restrained"
      *else
        *set crt_res 1
    *else
      *set {(crt_p & "disadvantage")} true
  *set {(crt_p & "restrain_res")} crt_res
  *set {(crt_p & "restrain_dealt")} crt_dealt
  *set {(crt_p & "restrain_roll")} crt_roll
  *set {(crt_p & "restrain_v")} crt_v
  *set {(crt_p & "restrain_web")} crt_web
  *set {(crt_p & "restrain_dc")} crt_dc
  *set {(crt_p & "restrain_page")} page_id
*set combat_hex_tag ""
*gosub hex_check_disadv crt_slot "strength"
*if (combat_hex_check_dis)
  *set combat_hex_tag " (Disadvantage — Hex)"
*if (crt_web)
  *if (crt_res = 1)
    *if (crt_v = 1)
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} hauls against the webbing, and the strands stretch and hold.
    *if (crt_v = 2)
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} claws at the sticky strands, and fresh threads catch at every pull.
    *if (crt_v = 3)
      The web clings tight, and ${combat_enemy_epithet} spends the turn twisting against it.
    *line_break
    [b][🕸️ ${combat_enemy_name} fails to break free (Strength check ${crt_roll}${combat_hex_tag} vs DC ${crt_dc}) — turn spent][/b]
    *line_break
    *set combat_restrain_stop true
  *elseif (crt_res = 2)
    *if (crt_v = 1)
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} rips loose with a wet tearing sound, trailing strands.
    *if (crt_v = 2)
      With one hard wrench, ${combat_enemy_epithet} tears through the webbing.
    *if (crt_v = 3)
      The strands snap one after another, and ${combat_enemy_epithet} staggers free.
    *line_break
    [b][🕸️ ${combat_enemy_name} breaks free of the web (Strength check ${crt_roll}${combat_hex_tag} vs DC ${crt_dc}) — turn spent][/b]
    *line_break
    *set combat_restrain_stop true
  *else
    The web holds ${combat_enemy_epithet} where they stand, and they fight from the spot.
    *line_break
*elseif (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
  The thorns close tight, and ${combat_enemy_epithet} goes still in the vines.
  *line_break
  [b][🌿 ${combat_enemy_name} takes ${crt_dealt} piercing damage from the thorns${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix} | @{combat_is_nonlethal 🏳️ Beaten|💀 Defeated}][/b]
  *line_break
  *set combat_burn_killed true
*else
  [b][🌿 ${combat_enemy_name} takes ${crt_dealt} piercing damage from the thorns${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}][/b]
  *line_break
  *if (crt_res = 1)
    *if (crt_v = 1)
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} heaves at the vines and gets nowhere.
    *if (crt_v = 2)
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} tears at the thorns with both hands, and the vines tighten.
    *if (crt_v = 3)
      The vines hold, and ${combat_enemy_epithet} spends the turn straining against them.
    *line_break
    [b][🌿 ${combat_enemy_name} fails to break free (Strength check ${crt_roll}${combat_hex_tag} vs DC ${crt_dc}) — turn spent][/b]
    *line_break
    *set combat_restrain_stop true
  *elseif (crt_res = 2)
    *if (crt_v = 1)
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} wrenches an arm free, and the rest of the vines tear away after it.
    *if (crt_v = 2)
      With a roar, ${combat_enemy_epithet} rips the vines out by the root.
    *if (crt_v = 3)
      The thorns give way at last, and ${combat_enemy_epithet} stumbles free.
    *line_break
    [b][🌿 ${combat_enemy_name} breaks free of the vines (Strength check ${crt_roll}${combat_hex_tag} vs DC ${crt_dc}) — turn spent][/b]
    *line_break
    *set combat_restrain_stop true
  *else
    The vines hold ${combat_enemy_epithet} where they stand, and they fight from the spot.
    *line_break
*gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_reconcile
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*comment CONTROL FLAMES: the scan (and, with the argument true, the spread itself). For each living burning enemy, every OTHER living enemy on the same lane tile whose fire is lower than it takes the burning enemy's turns. combat_spread_n counts the enemies that
*comment catch (or catch more). Only the same tile spreads: an archer at Long range when the burning enemies are at Mid does not catch.
*comment replay-ok: combat_spread_n is a scratch count that this label resets to 0 at its top before counting, so a replay recomputes it and never doubles it (the cast reads the copy it saved inside its replay lock).
*label combat_spread_scan
*params csp_apply
*set combat_spread_n 0
*set combat_spread_epithet ""
*temp csp_i 1
*label csp_loop_i
*if (csp_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *temp csp_src_burn {(("combat_enemy" & csp_i) & "_burning")}
  *temp csp_src_hp {(("combat_enemy" & csp_i) & "_hp")}
  *if ((csp_src_burn > 0) and (csp_src_hp > 0))
    *temp csp_src_pos {(("combat_enemy" & csp_i) & "_position")}
    *temp csp_j 1
    *comment replay-ok: combat_spread_n is a scratch count reset to 0 at the top of this scan, so a replay recomputes it and never doubles it.
    *label csp_loop_j
    *if (csp_j <= combat_enemy_count)
      *if (not(csp_j = csp_i))
        *temp csp_dst_hp {(("combat_enemy" & csp_j) & "_hp")}
        *temp csp_dst_pos {(("combat_enemy" & csp_j) & "_position")}
        *temp csp_dst_burn {(("combat_enemy" & csp_j) & "_burning")}
        *temp csp_dst_wet {(("combat_enemy" & csp_j) & "_wet")}
        *if (((csp_dst_hp > 0) and (csp_dst_wet <= 0)) and ((csp_dst_pos = csp_src_pos) and (csp_dst_burn < csp_src_burn)))
          *comment replay-ok: combat_spread_n is a scratch count reset to 0 at the top of this scan, so a replay recomputes it and never doubles it.
          *set combat_spread_n + 1
          *if (combat_spread_epithet = "")
            *set combat_spread_epithet {(("combat_enemy" & csp_i) & "_epithet")}
          *if (csp_apply)
            *set {(("combat_enemy" & csp_j) & "_burning")} csp_src_burn
      *set csp_j + 1
      *goto csp_loop_j
  *set csp_i + 1
  *goto csp_loop_i
*return

*label combat_burn_tick
*comment BURNING (2026-10-12, Alchemist's Fire): called at the start of an enemy's turn when it is burning. It takes 1d4 fire damage (+1 if it is still greased, which burns well), resistances and vulnerabilities applied, and the fire runs down one turn. Sets combat_burn_killed when the fire finishes it.
*comment The tick is stored per slot against the page id (burn_page / burn_dealt), so a replay of the page prints the same tick and never burns twice. An enemy that dies is skipped by the caller exactly like a Booming Blade kill.
*comment DOUSING (the user's call, 2026-10-12): a creature that can think of it spends its ACTION putting the fire out, two turns in three, and does nothing else that turn (combat_burn_doused, the caller skips the rest of its turn). Beasts and the like (the feral behavior profile)
*comment never do: they burn the full three turns.
*params cb_slot
*set combat_burn_killed false
*set combat_burn_doused false
*temp cb_p (("combat_enemy" & cb_slot) & "_")
*temp cb_page {(cb_p & "burn_page")}
*temp cb_left {(cb_p & "burning")}
*temp cb_dealt 0
*temp cb_douse false
*if (cb_page = page_id)
  *set cb_dealt {(cb_p & "burn_dealt")}
  *set cb_douse {(cb_p & "burn_douse")}
*else
  *temp cb_die 1
  *rand cb_die 1 4
  *temp cb_greased {(cb_p & "greased")}
  *if (cb_greased > 0)
    *set cb_die + 1
  *set combat_player_dmg_type "fire"
  *set combat_player_dmg_dealt cb_die
  *gosub_scene combat_dice apply_outgoing_damage_resist_vuln
  *set cb_dealt combat_player_dmg_dealt
  *set combat_enemy_hp - cb_dealt
  *if (combat_enemy_hp < 0)
    *set combat_enemy_hp 0
  *gosub fight_sync_target_out
  *set cb_left (cb_left - 1)
  *set {(cb_p & "burning")} cb_left
  *set {(cb_p & "burn_dealt")} cb_dealt
  *set {(cb_p & "burn_page")} page_id
  *temp cb_profile {(cb_p & "behavior_profile")}
  *temp cb_dv 1
  *rand cb_dv 1 3
  *comment STANDING IN FIRE (2026-11-23): putting the flames out on a burning patch only lights them again at the end of the turn (fire_end_turn), so it gets clear first
  *comment and lets its terrain step (leave_fire) take it off the patch; it can douse on a later turn, on clear ground.
  *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_fire_at {(cb_p & "position")}
  *if (combat_fire_here)
    *set cb_dv 3
  *comment WATER (2026-11-25): its terrain step (combat_ai.txt, already chosen) is into water. A ranged enemy that is not cornered takes that step as part of its turn, and the
  *comment water puts the fire out at the end of it, so it keeps its action and does not douse. A melee one closing on the player would Dash straight through, so for it the
  *comment water takes the place of dousing: on the same two-in-three roll it walks into the water instead of beating at the flames (the turn is spent either way, but it
  *comment ends a step nearer), and the fire is out now (the water's rule, Wet 2). The walk is kept in burn_water for a replay.
  *temp cb_to_water false
  *temp cb_walk false
  *set {(cb_p & "burn_water")} false
  *if (combat_ai_terrain_step_reason = "douse_in_water")
    *if ((combat_enemy_weapon_type = "ranged") and (not(combat_ranged_cornered)))
      *set cb_to_water true
      *set cb_dv 3
    *elseif (((not(combat_enemy_weapon_type = "ranged")) and (combat_enemy_range > 0)) and (combat_intercepting_ally = 0))
      *set cb_walk true
  *if (((combat_enemy_hp > 0) and (cb_left > 0)) and ((not(cb_profile = "feral")) and (cb_dv <= 2)))
    *set cb_douse true
    *set {(cb_p & "burning")} 0
    *set {(cb_p & "burn_douse")} true
    *if (cb_walk)
      *set {(cb_p & "burn_water")} true
      *set {(cb_p & "wet")} 2
      *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_lane_move_enemy_to_position cb_slot combat_ai_terrain_step_destination
  *if ((combat_ai_trace) and ((combat_enemy_hp > 0) and (cb_left > 0)))
    *temp cb_tr "rolled ${cb_dv} of 3 (1-2 puts it out)"
    *if (cb_profile = "feral")
      *set cb_tr "a beast never puts it out"
    *if (combat_fire_here)
      *set cb_tr "standing in fire, gets clear first"
    *if (cb_to_water)
      *set cb_tr "heads into the water to put it out, keeping its action"
    *if ((cb_walk) and (cb_douse))
      *set cb_tr "${cb_tr}; water in reach, so it walks in rather than beating at the flames"
    *temp cb_tp "keeps burning"
    *if (cb_douse)
      *set cb_tp "puts it out"
    *gosub_scene combat_ai ai_trace combat_enemy_name "burning: ${cb_tr} → ${cb_tp}"
*if (combat_enemy_hp <= 0)
  The fire finishes ${combat_enemy_epithet}, and they drop, still burning.
  *line_break
  [b][🔥 ${combat_enemy_name} burns: ${cb_dealt} Fire damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix} | @{combat_is_nonlethal 🏳️ Beaten|💀 Defeated}][/b]
  *line_break
  *set combat_burn_killed true
*else
  [b][🔥 ${combat_enemy_name} burns: ${cb_dealt} Fire damage${combat_outgoing_dmg_suffix}@{((cb_left <= 0) and (not(cb_douse)))  — the flames die out|}][/b]
  *line_break
  *if (cb_douse)
    *set combat_burn_doused true
    *if ({(cb_p & "burn_water")})
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} breaks off and throws themselves into the water, and the flames hiss out in a burst of steam.
      *line_break
      [b][💧 ${combat_enemy_name} puts out the fire in the water — turn spent][/b]
    *else
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} throws themselves down and beats at the flames with a cloak and both hands, spending the whole turn putting the fire out.
      *line_break
      [b][💨 ${combat_enemy_name} puts out the fire — turn spent][/b]
    *line_break
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*comment BLEEDING (3.3, an axeman's Bleeding Cut): 2 slashing at the start of each of the bearer's turns, resistances applied, with a concentration save for it. The damage
*comment ends Off-Balance. Stored against the page id (_bleed_page, _bleed_dealt, and the player's _bleed_conc), so a replay prints the same lines and never bleeds twice.
*label combat_player_bleed_tick
*if (not(combat_player_bleed_page = page_id))
  *gosub_scene combat_dice adjust_incoming_damage_packet 2 "slashing"
  *set combat_player_bleed_dealt combat_packet_damage
  *set hp_current - combat_player_bleed_dealt
  *if (hp_current < 0)
    *set hp_current 0
  *if ((not(combat_player_can_die)) and (hp_current < 1))
    *set hp_current 1
  *set combat_player_bleed_conc ""
  *if (combat_player_bleed_dealt > 0)
    *gosub_scene combat_dice condition_end "combat_player_" "off_balance"
    *set combat_conc_save_triggered false
    *set combat_conc_save_banner ""
    *set combat_conc_ended_banner ""
    *temp cpbt_quiet combat_save_quiet
    *set combat_save_quiet true
    *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_damage_check "combat_player_" combat_player_bleed_dealt 7
    *set combat_save_quiet cpbt_quiet
    *if (combat_conc_save_triggered)
      *set combat_player_bleed_conc combat_conc_save_banner
    *if (not(combat_conc_ended_banner = ""))
      *set combat_player_bleed_conc ((combat_player_bleed_conc & "[n/]") & combat_conc_ended_banner)
  *set combat_player_bleed_page page_id
[b][🩸 You bleed: ${combat_player_bleed_dealt} Slashing damage | HP: ${hp_current} / ${hp_max}][/b]
*line_break
*if (not(combat_player_bleed_conc = ""))
  ${combat_player_bleed_conc}
  *line_break
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*comment BURNING, player side (2026-11-23): 1d4 fire at the start of the player's turn, resistances applied (Wet, a Fire Resistance Potion), with a concentration save for it, and the fire
*comment runs down one turn. Stored against the page id (_burn_page, _burn_dealt, _burn_suffix, _burn_conc), so a replay prints the same lines and never burns twice.
*label combat_player_burn_tick
*if (not(combat_player_burn_page = page_id))
  *temp cpbn_die 1
  *rand cpbn_die 1 4
  *gosub_scene combat_dice adjust_incoming_damage_packet cpbn_die "fire"
  *set combat_player_burn_dealt combat_packet_damage
  *set combat_player_burn_suffix combat_packet_suffix
  *set hp_current - combat_player_burn_dealt
  *if (hp_current < 0)
    *set hp_current 0
  *if ((not(combat_player_can_die)) and (hp_current < 1))
    *set hp_current 1
  *set combat_player_burning - 1
  *set combat_player_burn_conc ""
  *if (combat_player_burn_dealt > 0)
    *set combat_conc_save_triggered false
    *set combat_conc_save_banner ""
    *set combat_conc_ended_banner ""
    *temp cpbn_quiet combat_save_quiet
    *set combat_save_quiet true
    *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_damage_check "combat_player_" combat_player_burn_dealt 7
    *set combat_save_quiet cpbn_quiet
    *if (combat_conc_save_triggered)
      *set combat_player_burn_conc combat_conc_save_banner
    *if (not(combat_conc_ended_banner = ""))
      *set combat_player_burn_conc ((combat_player_burn_conc & "[n/]") & combat_conc_ended_banner)
  *set combat_player_burn_page page_id
*temp cpbn_out ""
*if (combat_player_burning <= 0)
  *set cpbn_out " | The flames die out"
[b][🔥 You burn: ${combat_player_burn_dealt} Fire damage${combat_player_burn_suffix} | HP: ${hp_current} / ${hp_max}${cpbn_out}][/b]
*line_break
*if (not(combat_player_burn_conc = ""))
  ${combat_player_burn_conc}
  *line_break
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*comment BURNING, ally side (2026-11-23): as the player's, and as an enemy's, an ally that can think of it spends its turn putting the fire out two turns in three (a feral one
*comment never does); combat_burn_doused tells the caller the turn is spent. The choice is kept in _burn_douse for a replay of the page.
*label combat_ally_burn_tick
*params cabn_slot
*set combat_burn_doused false
*temp cabn_p (("combat_ally" & cabn_slot) & "_")
*temp cabn_name {(cabn_p & "name")}
*temp cabn_epi {(cabn_p & "epithet")}
*if (not({(cabn_p & "burn_page")} = page_id))
  *temp cabn_die 1
  *rand cabn_die 1 4
  *set combat_packet_is_weapon false
  *gosub_scene combat_dice adjust_damage_packet cabn_p cabn_die "fire" 0
  *temp cabn_dealt combat_packet_damage
  *temp cabn_hp ({(cabn_p & "hp")} - cabn_dealt)
  *if (cabn_hp < 0)
    *set cabn_hp 0
  *set {(cabn_p & "hp")} cabn_hp
  *if (cabn_dealt > 0)
    *if (cabn_hp <= 0)
      *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_end cabn_p
    *else
      *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_damage_check cabn_p cabn_dealt 8
  *temp cabn_left ({(cabn_p & "burning")} - 1)
  *set {(cabn_p & "burning")} cabn_left
  *set {(cabn_p & "burn_douse")} false
  *temp cabn_profile {(cabn_p & "behavior_profile")}
  *temp cabn_dv 1
  *rand cabn_dv 1 3
  *comment STANDING IN FIRE (2026-11-23): as an enemy's (combat_burn_tick): it gets off the patch first, and douses on a later turn on clear ground.
  *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_fire_at {(cabn_p & "position")}
  *if (combat_fire_here)
    *set cabn_dv 3
  *set {(cabn_p & "burn_water")} false
  *if (((cabn_hp > 0) and (cabn_left > 0)) and ((not(cabn_profile = "feral")) and (cabn_dv <= 2)))
    *set {(cabn_p & "burning")} 0
    *set {(cabn_p & "burn_douse")} true
    *comment WATER (2026-11-25): with a water patch one step away inside the lane, it walks into the water instead of beating at the flames, as a melee enemy does
    *comment (combat_burn_tick): the turn is spent either way, but it ends Wet (the water's rule). Kept in burn_water for a replay.
    *temp cabn_pos {(cabn_p & "position")}
    *temp cabn_wto 0
    *temp cabn_wi 1
    *label cabn_water_loop
    *if ((cabn_wi <= combat_terrain_slot_cap) and (cabn_wto = 0))
      *temp cabn_wpos {(("combat_terrain" & cabn_wi) & "_position")}
      *if ({(("combat_terrain" & cabn_wi) & "_type")} = "water")
        *if ((((cabn_wpos = (cabn_pos + 1)) or (cabn_wpos = (cabn_pos - 1))) and (cabn_wpos >= combat_lane_min_position)) and (cabn_wpos <= combat_lane_max_position))
          *set cabn_wto cabn_wpos
      *set cabn_wi + 1
      *goto cabn_water_loop
    *if (not(cabn_wto = 0))
      *set {(cabn_p & "burn_water")} true
      *set {(cabn_p & "wet")} 2
      *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_lane_move_ally_to_position cabn_slot 0 cabn_wto
  *set {(cabn_p & "burn_dealt")} cabn_dealt
  *set {(cabn_p & "burn_page")} page_id
*temp cabn_shown {(cabn_p & "burn_dealt")}
*temp cabn_hp_now {(cabn_p & "hp")}
*temp cabn_max {(cabn_p & "max_hp")}
*temp cabn_left_now {(cabn_p & "burning")}
*temp cabn_douse {(cabn_p & "burn_douse")}
*if (cabn_hp_now <= 0)
  [b][🔥 ${cabn_name} burns: ${cabn_shown} Fire damage | Down][/b]
*else
  *temp cabn_out ""
  *if ((cabn_left_now <= 0) and (not(cabn_douse)))
    *set cabn_out " | The flames die out"
  [b][🔥 ${cabn_name} burns: ${cabn_shown} Fire damage | HP: ${cabn_hp_now} / ${cabn_max}${cabn_out}][/b]
*line_break
*if ((cabn_hp_now > 0) and (cabn_douse))
  *set combat_burn_doused true
  *if ({(cabn_p & "burn_water")})
    $!{cabn_epi} breaks off and throws themselves into the water, and the flames hiss out in a burst of steam.
    *line_break
    [b][💧 ${cabn_name} puts out the fire in the water | Turn spent][/b]
  *else
    $!{cabn_epi} drops and rolls, beating at the flames until the last of them is out, and spends the whole turn on it.
    *line_break
    [b][💨 ${cabn_name} puts out the fire | Turn spent][/b]
  *line_break
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*label combat_ally_bleed_tick
*params cabt_slot
*temp cabt_p (("combat_ally" & cabt_slot) & "_")
*temp cabt_name {(cabt_p & "name")}
*if (not({(cabt_p & "bleed_page")} = page_id))
  *set combat_packet_is_weapon false
  *gosub_scene combat_dice adjust_damage_packet cabt_p 2 "slashing" 0
  *temp cabt_dealt combat_packet_damage
  *temp cabt_hp ({(cabt_p & "hp")} - cabt_dealt)
  *if (cabt_hp < 0)
    *set cabt_hp 0
  *set {(cabt_p & "hp")} cabt_hp
  *if (cabt_dealt > 0)
    *gosub_scene combat_dice condition_end cabt_p "off_balance"
    *if (cabt_hp <= 0)
      *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_end cabt_p
    *else
      *gosub_scene combat_dice concentration_damage_check cabt_p cabt_dealt 8
  *set {(cabt_p & "bleed_dealt")} cabt_dealt
  *set {(cabt_p & "bleed_page")} page_id
*temp cabt_shown {(cabt_p & "bleed_dealt")}
*temp cabt_hp_now {(cabt_p & "hp")}
*temp cabt_max {(cabt_p & "max_hp")}
*if (cabt_hp_now <= 0)
  [b][🩸 ${cabt_name} bleeds: ${cabt_shown} Slashing damage | Down][/b]
*else
  [b][🩸 ${cabt_name} bleeds: ${cabt_shown} Slashing damage | HP: ${cabt_hp_now} / ${cabt_max}][/b]
*line_break
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*comment STUN GATE (2026-11-01). Called at the start of an enemy's turn: when combat_enemyN_stunned is above 0 the enemy loses the turn, the count drops by one, and combat_stun_stop is true. The finished result is stored against the page id (_stun_page, _stun_left, _stun_v), so a replay of the page
*comment prints the same line and never counts a second turn. _stun_last_round marks the round of the last lost turn, which combat_attack_edge (combat_dice.txt) reads. The lines depend on _stun_kind: "memory" is the Philter of Remembrance.
*label combat_stun_gate
*comment sg_side is "enemy" or "ally" (2026-11-03): an ally loses its turns to a stun exactly as an enemy does, through the same fields (combat_allyN_stunned and the rest).
*params sg_side sg_slot
*set combat_stun_stop false
*temp sg_p (("combat_enemy" & sg_slot) & "_")
*temp sg_epi combat_enemy_epithet
*temp sg_name combat_enemy_name
*if (sg_side = "ally")
  *set sg_p (("combat_ally" & sg_slot) & "_")
  *set sg_epi {(sg_p & "epithet")}
  *set sg_name {(sg_p & "name")}
*temp sg_n {(sg_p & "stunned")}
*temp sg_page {(sg_p & "stun_page")}
*temp sg_left 0
*temp sg_v 1
*temp sg_kind {(sg_p & "stun_kind")}
*if (sg_page = page_id)
  *set sg_left {(sg_p & "stun_left")}
  *set sg_v {(sg_p & "stun_v")}
  *set combat_stun_stop true
*elseif (sg_n > 0)
  *set sg_left (sg_n - 1)
  *rand sg_v 1 3
  *set {(sg_p & "stunned")} sg_left
  *set {(sg_p & "stun_left")} sg_left
  *set {(sg_p & "stun_v")} sg_v
  *set {(sg_p & "stun_page")} page_id
  *set {(sg_p & "stun_last_round")} combat_round
  *set combat_stun_stop true
*if (combat_stun_stop)
  *if (sg_kind = "memory")
    *if (sg_v = 1)
      $!{sg_epi} stares at nothing, lips moving around a name, and the turn slips away.
    *if (sg_v = 2)
      $!{sg_epi} sits back on their heels and rocks gently, lost in a scent that is not in the room.
    *if (sg_v = 3)
      A whimper rises from ${sg_epi} as they reach for someone who is not there, and they make no move at anyone.
  *else
    *if (sg_v = 1)
      $!{sg_epi} stands dazed and swaying, and the turn goes by.
    *if (sg_v = 2)
      $!{sg_epi} blinks at nothing and does not move.
    *if (sg_v = 3)
      $!{sg_epi} shakes a heavy head and cannot gather themselves to act.
  *line_break
  *if (sg_left > 0)
    [b][💫 ${sg_name} is Stunned — turn lost | ${sg_left} more turn(s)][/b]
  *else
    [b][💫 ${sg_name} is Stunned — turn lost | the daze is lifting][/b]
  *line_break
  *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*comment ============================================================================
*comment MORALE (2026-11-03, the user's design). A combatant's nerve can break when the fight turns against it.
*comment WHEN: at the start of its own turn, each of these is checked once per fight, the first time it is true:
*comment   badly hurt     its HP is below half
*comment   strongest down the strongest of its own side (the most maximum HP, and more than its own) is down; for an ally, the player is below a quarter of their HP
*comment   half down      half or more of its side is down (out of the fight, by any means)
*comment and Intimidate (the player's action) can force a check at any time.
*comment THE CHECK: a Wisdom save, d20 + WIS + proficiency + resolve (an enemy's) against DC 10, with advantage when its side is winning (the larger share of its side's HP left, both sides counted whole). The type's morale (combat_<side>N_morale, from tools/enemy_catalog.json and the ally
*comment types in combat_types.txt) decides how it rolls: fearless never checks, steady rolls with advantage, skittish with disadvantage.
*comment combat_no_morale (a duel, a boss) turns all of it off for the fight. A failed save BREAKS it (_broken):
*comment   An ENEMY is done fighting. A humanoid that is cornered (an opponent on its tile) or in a non-lethal fight surrenders on the spot. Anything else flees: it Dashes away from the player toward its own edge of the lane, taking the usual free swing
*comment   if it leaves someone's reach, and when it starts a turn on that edge it tries to get away: a contested roll, proficiency against
*comment   proficiency (combat_escape_contest). Win and it leaves the fight; lose and it is cut off for the turn. Until then it can be chased and cut down.
*comment   Fled or surrendered, it is out (_out "fled" / "surrendered", HP 0, so everything that counts the living counts it as beaten), and the story
*comment   can read how each enemy ended (FIGHT RESULTS, combat_record_results).
*comment   An ALLY never leaves the player. It wavers: it falls back a step toward the player and covers up (attacks on it have disadvantage until its
*comment   next turn) instead of attacking, and at the start of each later turn it makes the same save to rally and fight on as normal.
*comment Every roll here happens inside a chain of turns, so a refreshed page rolls it again the same (REPLAY-SAFE TURN CHAINS); Intimidate, on the
*comment player's own page, passes its stored dice in through combat_morale_forced_a/_b.
*comment ============================================================================

*comment One morale save for the slot (cmc_side "enemy" or "ally"), with a banner. Output: combat_morale_broke (and _broken set on a failure).
*label combat_morale_check
*params cmc_side cmc_slot cmc_reason
*set combat_morale_broke false
*temp cmc_p (("combat_enemy" & cmc_slot) & "_")
*if (cmc_side = "ally")
  *set cmc_p (("combat_ally" & cmc_slot) & "_")
*temp cmc_kind {(cmc_p & "morale")}
*if ((cmc_kind = "fearless") or (combat_no_morale))
  *set combat_morale_forced_a 0
  *return
*temp cmc_name {(cmc_p & "name")}
*temp cmc_score {(cmc_p & "wisdom")}
*gosub_scene startup score_to_mod cmc_score
*temp cmc_mod sm_mod
*temp cmc_a 0
*temp cmc_b 0
*rand cmc_a 1 20
*rand cmc_b 1 20
*if (combat_morale_forced_a > 0)
  *set cmc_a combat_morale_forced_a
  *set cmc_b combat_morale_forced_b
  *set combat_morale_forced_a 0
*comment Nerve is experience (2026-11-03, the user): proficiency and, for an enemy, its resolve add to the save, as they do to Intimidate's DC.
*temp cmc_prof {(cmc_p & "prof")}
*if (cmc_prof <= 0)
  *set cmc_prof combat_lib_prof_bonus
*temp cmc_resolve 0
*if (cmc_side = "enemy")
  *set cmc_resolve {(("combat_enemy" & cmc_slot) & "_resolve")}
*comment Advantage when its own side is winning (2026-11-03, the user): nobody walks away from a fight they are about to win.
*comment WINNING (2026-11-22, the user): the two sides are compared whole, by the share of their HP left. The player's side is the player and every
*comment ally (a downed ally adds 0); the enemy side is every enemy on the field (fled or surrendered add 0; a reinforcement still on its way is not
*comment counted). A side is winning when its share is the larger: cross-multiplied (cmc_ec * cmc_pm against cmc_pc * cmc_em) so no division.
*comment It replaced "the player below half, or half the allies down" for an enemy, which gave a lone foe at 1 HP advantage over a winning party.
*temp cmc_winning false
*temp cmc_i 1
*temp cmc_v 0
*temp cmc_pc hp_current
*if (cmc_pc < 0)
  *set cmc_pc 0
*temp cmc_pm hp_max
*label combat_morale_check_ally_loop
*if (cmc_i <= combat_ally_count)
  *set cmc_v {(("combat_ally" & cmc_i) & "_hp")}
  *if (cmc_v > 0)
    *set cmc_pc + cmc_v
  *set cmc_pm + {(("combat_ally" & cmc_i) & "_max_hp")}
  *set cmc_i + 1
  *goto combat_morale_check_ally_loop
*temp cmc_ec 0
*temp cmc_em 0
*set cmc_i 1
*label combat_morale_check_enemy_loop
*if (cmc_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *if (not({(("combat_enemy" & cmc_i) & "_out")} = "reserve"))
    *set cmc_v {(("combat_enemy" & cmc_i) & "_hp")}
    *if (cmc_v > 0)
      *set cmc_ec + cmc_v
    *set cmc_em + {(("combat_enemy" & cmc_i) & "_max_hp")}
  *set cmc_i + 1
  *goto combat_morale_check_enemy_loop
*if ((cmc_pm > 0) and (cmc_em > 0))
  *if ((cmc_side = "enemy") and ((cmc_ec * cmc_pm) > (cmc_pc * cmc_em)))
    *set cmc_winning true
  *if ((cmc_side = "ally") and ((cmc_pc * cmc_em) > (cmc_ec * cmc_pm)))
    *set cmc_winning true
*comment Advantage (steady, or winning) and disadvantage (skittish) cancel out, as in 5e.
*temp cmc_adv ((cmc_kind = "steady") or (cmc_winning))
*temp cmc_dis (cmc_kind = "skittish")
*temp cmc_why_adv "steady"
*if (cmc_winning)
  *set cmc_why_adv "its side is winning"
  *if (cmc_kind = "steady")
    *set cmc_why_adv "steady, its side is winning"
*temp cmc_roll cmc_a
*temp cmc_note ""
*if ((cmc_adv) and (cmc_dis))
  *set cmc_note " (skittish, but ${cmc_why_adv}: a straight roll)"
*elseif (cmc_adv)
  *if (cmc_b > cmc_roll)
    *set cmc_roll cmc_b
  *set cmc_note " (Advantage: ${cmc_why_adv})"
*elseif (cmc_dis)
  *if (cmc_b < cmc_roll)
    *set cmc_roll cmc_b
  *set cmc_note " (Disadvantage: skittish)"
*temp cmc_total (((cmc_roll + cmc_mod) + cmc_prof) + cmc_resolve)
*temp cmc_mod_str "+${cmc_mod} WIS"
*if (cmc_mod < 0)
  *set cmc_mod_str "${cmc_mod} WIS"
*set cmc_mod_str "${cmc_mod_str} +${cmc_prof} prof"
*if (cmc_resolve > 0)
  *set cmc_mod_str "${cmc_mod_str} +${cmc_resolve} resolve"
*set combat_morale_broke (cmc_total < 10)
*temp cmc_verdict "HOLDS"
*if (combat_morale_broke)
  *set cmc_verdict "BREAKS"
  *set {(cmc_p & "broken")} true
*if (cmc_reason = "rally")
  *set cmc_verdict "RALLIES"
  *if (combat_morale_broke)
    *set cmc_verdict "STILL SHAKEN"
[b][🎲 ${cmc_name} Morale (${cmc_reason}): ${cmc_roll} ${cmc_mod_str}${cmc_note} = ${cmc_total} vs DC 10 — ${cmc_verdict}][/b]
*line_break
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*comment THE DC TO INTIMIDATE an enemy (2026-11-03, the user): 10 + its WIS modifier + its proficiency bonus + its resolve (a type's own extra
*comment nerve: a guard +2, a captain +5; tools/enemy_catalog.json, or a fight's combat_enemyN_resolve_override), then the moment:
*comment   -2 it is badly hurt (below half its HP)        -2 half or more of its side is down
*comment   +2 someone tougher on its side still stands      +2 your side is the one losing (you below half, or half your allies down)
*comment Never below 5. Output: combat_intim_dc, and combat_intim_dc_note naming what moved it ("badly hurt -2, guard +2"), for the option.
*label combat_intimidate_dc
*params cid_slot
*temp cid_p (("combat_enemy" & cid_slot) & "_")
*temp cid_score {(cid_p & "wisdom")}
*gosub_scene startup score_to_mod cid_score
*temp cid_prof {(cid_p & "prof")}
*if (cid_prof <= 0)
  *set cid_prof combat_lib_prof_bonus
*temp cid_resolve {(cid_p & "resolve")}
*temp cid_dc (((10 + sm_mod) + cid_prof) + cid_resolve)
*temp cid_note ""
*temp cid_sep ""
*if (cid_resolve > 0)
  *set cid_note "resolve +${cid_resolve}"
  *set cid_sep ", "
*temp cid_hp {(cid_p & "hp")}
*temp cid_max {(cid_p & "max_hp")}
*if ((cid_hp * 2) < cid_max)
  *set cid_dc - 2
  *set cid_note "${cid_note}${cid_sep}badly hurt -2"
  *set cid_sep ", "
*comment Hex on its Wisdom: the DC is its passive Wisdom check, so it takes -5.
*gosub hex_check_disadv cid_slot "wisdom"
*if (combat_hex_check_dis)
  *set cid_dc - 5
  *set cid_note "${cid_note}${cid_sep}Hex -5"
  *set cid_sep ", "
*temp cid_total 0
*temp cid_down 0
*temp cid_tougher false
*temp cid_i 1
*label combat_intimidate_dc_loop
*if (cid_i <= combat_enemy_count)
  *temp cid_ihp {(("combat_enemy" & cid_i) & "_hp")}
  *if (not({(("combat_enemy" & cid_i) & "_out")} = "reserve"))
    *set cid_total + 1
  *if ({(("combat_enemy" & cid_i) & "_out")} = "reserve")
    *comment A reinforcement still on its way (REINFORCEMENTS) is neither down nor beside it yet.
  *elseif (cid_ihp <= 0)
    *set cid_down + 1
  *elseif ((not(cid_i = cid_slot)) and ({(("combat_enemy" & cid_i) & "_max_hp")} > cid_max))
    *set cid_tougher true
  *set cid_i + 1
  *goto combat_intimidate_dc_loop
*if ((cid_total > 1) and ((cid_down * 2) >= cid_total))
  *set cid_dc - 2
  *set cid_note "${cid_note}${cid_sep}its side half down -2"
  *set cid_sep ", "
*if (cid_tougher)
  *set cid_dc + 2
  *set cid_note "${cid_note}${cid_sep}someone tougher beside it +2"
  *set cid_sep ", "
*temp cid_ours 1
*temp cid_ours_down 0
*set cid_i 1
*label combat_intimidate_dc_ally_loop
*if (cid_i <= combat_ally_count)
  *set cid_ours + 1
  *if ({(("combat_ally" & cid_i) & "_hp")} <= 0)
    *set cid_ours_down + 1
  *set cid_i + 1
  *goto combat_intimidate_dc_ally_loop
*if (((hp_current * 2) < hp_max) or ((cid_ours > 1) and ((cid_ours_down * 2) >= cid_ours)))
  *set cid_dc + 2
  *set cid_note "${cid_note}${cid_sep}your side is losing +2"
*if (cid_dc < 5)
  *set cid_dc 5
*set combat_intim_dc cid_dc
*set combat_intim_dc_note cid_note
*comment The option's bracket ("DC 14: resolve +2"), built here because a multireplace cannot nest inside the show_stat_hints one.
*set combat_intim_dc_text "DC ${cid_dc}"
*if (not(cid_note = ""))
  *set combat_intim_dc_text "DC ${cid_dc}: ${cid_note}"
*return

*comment The start of an enemy's or ally's turn: check the triggers, then act on a broken nerve. combat_morale_stop is true when that spent the turn.
*comment For an enemy, the caller has synced its scratch fields (fight_sync_target_in), as for the rest of its turn.
*label combat_morale_turn
*params cmt_side cmt_slot
*set combat_morale_stop false
*set combat_boom_killed false
*temp cmt_p (("combat_enemy" & cmt_slot) & "_")
*if (cmt_side = "ally")
  *set cmt_p (("combat_ally" & cmt_slot) & "_")
  *set {(cmt_p & "dodging")} false
*temp cmt_hp {(cmt_p & "hp")}
*temp cmt_max {(cmt_p & "max_hp")}
*if (((cmt_hp <= 0) or (combat_no_morale)) or ({(cmt_p & "morale")} = "fearless"))
  *return
*temp cmt_just_broke false
*comment Badly hurt.
*if ((not({(cmt_p & "broken")})) and (not({(cmt_p & "morale_bloodied")})))
  *if ((cmt_hp * 2) < cmt_max)
    *set {(cmt_p & "morale_bloodied")} true
    *gosub combat_morale_check cmt_side cmt_slot "badly hurt"
    *set cmt_just_broke combat_morale_broke
*comment The strongest of its side down (an enemy), or the player in a bad way (an ally).
*if ((not({(cmt_p & "broken")})) and (not({(cmt_p & "morale_strong")})))
  *temp cmt_strong_down false
  *if (cmt_side = "ally")
    *set cmt_strong_down ((hp_current * 4) < hp_max)
  *else
    *temp cmt_best 0
    *temp cmt_best_max 0
    *temp cmt_i 1
    *label combat_morale_turn_strong_loop
    *if (cmt_i <= combat_enemy_count)
      *if (((not(cmt_i = cmt_slot)) and (not({(("combat_enemy" & cmt_i) & "_out")} = "reserve"))) and ({(("combat_enemy" & cmt_i) & "_max_hp")} > cmt_best_max))
        *set cmt_best cmt_i
        *set cmt_best_max {(("combat_enemy" & cmt_i) & "_max_hp")}
      *set cmt_i + 1
      *goto combat_morale_turn_strong_loop
    *comment Only someone TOUGHER than this one counts as "the strongest" (a captain, a bear among wolves): among equals, a rat's nerve does not
    *comment hang on another rat; a pack of equals checks when half of it is down instead.
    *if ((cmt_best > 0) and (cmt_best_max > cmt_max))
      *set cmt_strong_down ({(("combat_enemy" & cmt_best) & "_hp")} <= 0)
  *if (cmt_strong_down)
    *set {(cmt_p & "morale_strong")} true
    *temp cmt_why "the strongest has fallen"
    *if (cmt_side = "ally")
      *set cmt_why "you are badly hurt"
    *gosub combat_morale_check cmt_side cmt_slot cmt_why
    *set cmt_just_broke combat_morale_broke
*comment Half its side down.
*if ((not({(cmt_p & "broken")})) and (not({(cmt_p & "morale_half")})))
  *temp cmt_total 0
  *temp cmt_down 0
  *temp cmt_j 1
  *if (cmt_side = "ally")
    *set cmt_total 1
    *if (hp_current <= 0)
      *set cmt_down 1
    *label combat_morale_turn_ally_side_loop
    *if (cmt_j <= combat_ally_count)
      *set cmt_total + 1
      *if ({(("combat_ally" & cmt_j) & "_hp")} <= 0)
        *set cmt_down + 1
      *set cmt_j + 1
      *goto combat_morale_turn_ally_side_loop
  *else
    *label combat_morale_turn_enemy_side_loop
    *if (cmt_j <= combat_enemy_count)
      *comment A reinforcement still on its way (REINFORCEMENTS) is not part of the side yet.
      *if (not({(("combat_enemy" & cmt_j) & "_out")} = "reserve"))
        *set cmt_total + 1
        *if ({(("combat_enemy" & cmt_j) & "_hp")} <= 0)
          *set cmt_down + 1
      *set cmt_j + 1
      *goto combat_morale_turn_enemy_side_loop
  *if ((cmt_total > 1) and ((cmt_down * 2) >= cmt_total))
    *set {(cmt_p & "morale_half")} true
    *gosub combat_morale_check cmt_side cmt_slot "half its side is down"
    *set cmt_just_broke combat_morale_broke
*if (not({(cmt_p & "broken")}))
  *return
*set combat_morale_stop true
*if (cmt_side = "ally")
  *comment A wavering ally tries to rally first, unless its nerve went only this moment.
  *if (not(cmt_just_broke))
    *gosub combat_morale_check "ally" cmt_slot "rally"
    *if (not(combat_morale_broke))
      *set {(cmt_p & "broken")} false
      *set combat_morale_stop false
      *temp cmt_epi {(cmt_p & "epithet")}
      $!{cmt_epi} steadies, and comes back into the fight.
      *line_break
      *return
  *gosub combat_ally_waver cmt_slot
  *return
*gosub combat_enemy_broken_act cmt_slot
*return

*comment A wavering ally's turn: a step toward the player (if it is not already beside them and the step is legal), then it covers up.
*label combat_ally_waver
*params caw_slot
*temp caw_p (("combat_ally" & caw_slot) & "_")
*temp caw_pos {(caw_p & "position")}
*temp caw_name {(caw_p & "name")}
*temp caw_epi {(caw_p & "epithet")}
*set combat_ally_waver_epithet caw_epi
*temp caw_dest caw_pos
*if (caw_pos > combat_lane_player_position)
  *set caw_dest (caw_pos - 1)
*if (caw_pos < combat_lane_player_position)
  *set caw_dest (caw_pos + 1)

  *gosub_scene combat_ai combat_ai_terrain_destination_is_legal "ally" caw_slot caw_dest

    *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_lane_move_ally_to_position caw_slot 0 caw_dest
    *set caw_moved true
*set {(caw_p & "dodging")} true
*temp caw_v 1
*rand caw_v 1 3

  $!{caw_epi} gives ground, shield arm up, no longer looking for a fight, only for a way through this one.
*if (caw_v = 2)
  Shaken, ${caw_epi} backs toward you and does nothing more than keep every blow away.
*if (caw_v = 3)

*comment A broken enemy's turn: surrender, flee a step further, or leave the field. Its scratch fields are synced (the active target is this slot).
*comment Output for the caller: the slot may now be out (HP 0), or have died to a free swing on the way; the turn tail checks for victory either way.
*label combat_enemy_broken_act
*params ceba_slot

  *set ceba_edge combat_lane_min_position

  *goto combat_enemy_broken_act_corner_loop

  *if (ceba_v = 1)

  *if (ceba_v = 2)

  *if (ceba_v = 3)

  *set {(ceba_p & "hp")} 0
  *set combat_enemy_hp 0
  *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
  *return
*if (ceba_at_edge)
  *gosub combat_escape_contest ceba_slot
  *if (not(combat_escape_ok))
    *if (ceba_v = 1)
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} makes for the open ground, but you are on it first and it has to turn back.
    *if (ceba_v = 2)
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} tries to slip away, and you cut it off before it can.
    *if (ceba_v = 3)
      $!{combat_enemy_epithet} runs for it, finds the way blocked, and backs up against the edge.
    *line_break
    [b][⚑ ${combat_enemy_name} Is Cut Off — still in the fight, it tries again next turn][/b]
    *line_break
    *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true

    With one last look over a shoulder, ${combat_enemy_epithet} is gone.
  *if (ceba_v = 3)
    $!{combat_enemy_epithet} breaks into a flat run and is lost from sight.
  *line_break

*comment THE ESCAPE (2026-11-03, the user): a broken enemy that starts its turn on its own edge of the lane does not simply leave; it has to
*comment get away from you. A contested roll, proficiency against proficiency: it rolls d20 + its proficiency + its DEX modifier, you roll
*comment d20 + your proficiency + your DEX modifier, and it gets away only if it beats you (a tie is yours). If you are hidden there is no
*comment chase and it simply goes. A failed escape spends its turn, still broken, on the edge, and it tries again next turn, which gives
*comment you a turn to close on it. Rolled inside the enemy's turn chain, so a refresh rolls it again the same. Output: combat_escape_ok.
*label combat_escape_contest
*params cec_slot
*set combat_escape_ok true
*if (combat_player_hidden)
  *return
*temp cec_p (("combat_enemy" & cec_slot) & "_")
*temp cec_prof {(cec_p & "prof")}
*if (cec_prof <= 0)
  *set cec_prof combat_lib_prof_bonus
*temp cec_score {(cec_p & "dexterity")}
*gosub_scene startup score_to_mod cec_score
*temp cec_dex sm_mod
*gosub_scene startup score_to_mod dexterity
*temp cec_pdex sm_mod
*temp cec_a 1
*temp cec_b 1
*rand cec_a 1 20
*rand cec_b 1 20
*comment Hex on its Dexterity: its side of the contest is rolled twice, the lower kept.
*temp cec_hex ""
*gosub hex_check_disadv cec_slot "dexterity"
*if (combat_hex_check_dis)
  *temp cec_a2 1
  *rand cec_a2 1 20
  *if (cec_a2 < cec_a)
    *set cec_a cec_a2
  *set cec_hex " (Disadvantage — Hex)"
*temp cec_them ((cec_a + cec_prof) + cec_dex)
*temp cec_you ((cec_b + prof_bonus) + cec_pdex)
*set combat_escape_ok (cec_them > cec_you)
*temp cec_dex_str "+${cec_dex}"
*if (cec_dex < 0)
  *set cec_dex_str "${cec_dex}"
*temp cec_pdex_str "+${cec_pdex}"
*if (cec_pdex < 0)
  *set cec_pdex_str "${cec_pdex}"
*temp cec_verdict "CUT OFF"
*if (combat_escape_ok)
  *set cec_verdict "GETS AWAY"
[b][🎲 ${combat_enemy_name} Escape: ${cec_a}${cec_hex} +${cec_prof} prof ${cec_dex_str} DEX = ${cec_them} vs your ${cec_b} +${prof_bonus} prof ${cec_pdex_str} DEX = ${cec_you} — ${cec_verdict}][/b]
*line_break
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*label combat_grease_gate
*comment GREASE GATE (2026-10-12). Called at the start of an enemy's turn when it is greased or prone, with whether the enemy means to move this turn. Prints what happens and sets combat_grease_stop true when the turn is spent.
*comment Prone: it stands up (its movement), and the turn is spent if it meant to move; one that only wanted to swing gets up and swings. Greased and moving: DC 10 Dexterity check; a pass scrapes the grease off and it moves normally, a miss drops it prone with the turn lost and one fewer attempt on the grease
*comment (it starts with two, and wears off after combat_enemyN_grease_until rounds). Standing still never rolls. The result is stored per slot against the page id, so a replay of the page reprints the same outcome and never rolls or falls twice.
*params gg_slot gg_wants
*set combat_grease_stop false
*temp gg_p (("combat_enemy" & gg_slot) & "_")
*temp gg_page {(gg_p & "grease_page")}
*temp gg_res 0
*temp gg_roll 0
*temp gg_v 1
*if (gg_page = page_id)
  *set gg_res {(gg_p & "grease_res")}
  *set gg_roll {(gg_p & "grease_roll")}
  *set gg_v {(gg_p & "grease_v")}
*else
  *temp gg_prone {(gg_p & "prone")}
  *temp gg_greased {(gg_p & "greased")}
  *temp gg_until {(gg_p & "grease_until")}
  *if ((gg_greased > 0) and (combat_round > gg_until))
    *set gg_greased 0
    *set {(gg_p & "greased")} 0
  *rand gg_v 1 3
  *if (gg_prone)
    *comment Prone (3.9a): standing costs half its movement, not the turn. It acts as normal, with no Dash (combat_grease_stood), and a greased one tests its footing only on a later turn.
    *set {(gg_p & "prone")} false
    *set gg_res 5
  *elseif ((gg_greased > 0) and (gg_wants))
    *temp gg_dex {(gg_p & "dexterity")}
    *gosub_scene startup score_to_mod gg_dex
    *rand gg_roll 1 20
    *comment Hex on its Dexterity: rolled twice, the lower kept.
    *gosub hex_check_disadv gg_slot "dexterity"
    *if (combat_hex_check_dis)
      *temp gg_roll2 1
      *rand gg_roll2 1 20
      *if (gg_roll2 < gg_roll)
        *set gg_roll gg_roll2
    *set gg_roll (gg_roll + sm_mod)
    *if (gg_roll >= 10)
      *set gg_res 4
      *set {(gg_p & "greased")} 0
    *else
      *set gg_res 3
      *set {(gg_p & "prone")} true
      *set {(gg_p & "greased")} (gg_greased - 1)
  *set {(gg_p & "grease_res")} gg_res
  *set {(gg_p & "grease_roll")} gg_roll
  *set {(gg_p & "grease_v")} gg_v
  *set {(gg_p & "grease_page")} page_id
*set combat_grease_stood false
*if (gg_res = 5)
  [b][🧎 ${combat_enemy_name} gets back up][/b]
  *line_break
  *set combat_grease_stood true
*if (gg_res = 3)
  *if (gg_v = 1)
    $!{combat_enemy_epithet} takes a step, and the grease takes the foot out from under them. They go down hard.
  *if (gg_v = 2)
    The slick ground betrays ${combat_enemy_epithet} mid-stride, and they land on their back in the mess.
  *if (gg_v = 3)
    $!{combat_enemy_epithet} lunges forward, finds nothing to push against, and goes sprawling.
  *line_break
  *set combat_hex_tag ""
  *gosub hex_check_disadv gg_slot "dexterity"
  *if (combat_hex_check_dis)
    *set combat_hex_tag " (Disadvantage — Hex)"
  [b][🧴 ${combat_enemy_name} slips and falls prone (Dexterity check ${gg_roll}${combat_hex_tag} vs DC 10) — turn lost][/b]
  *line_break
  *set combat_grease_stop true
*elseif (gg_res = 1)
  *if (gg_v = 1)
    $!{combat_enemy_epithet} claws their way upright on the slick ground, and that is the whole of their turn.
  *if (gg_v = 2)
    $!{combat_enemy_epithet} scrabbles to get their feet under them, with no strength left to cross the ground.
  *if (gg_v = 3)
    Just standing takes the whole of ${combat_enemy_epithet}'s turn.
  *line_break
  [b][🧴 ${combat_enemy_name} struggles to its feet — turn spent standing][/b]
  *line_break
  *set combat_grease_stop true
*elseif (gg_res = 2)
  $!{combat_enemy_epithet} rolls over and gets up, mud and grease to the elbows.
  *line_break
  [b][🧴 ${combat_enemy_name} stands up][/b]
  *line_break
*elseif (gg_res = 4)
  $!{combat_enemy_epithet} feels the grease go and catches their balance, scraping a boot clean.
  *line_break
  [b][🧴 ${combat_enemy_name} keeps its footing (Dexterity check ${gg_roll} vs DC 10) — the grease is spent][/b]
  *line_break
*if (gg_res > 0)
  *set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*label combat_hidden_enemy_turn
*comment One enemy's turn against a hidden player (2026-10-01). Reads the active slot's scratch fields, like every enemy-turn
*comment branch. The enemy never attacks the player: a melee type closes one tier toward where it last saw them (no Dash, no
*comment attack at the end), a ranged type holds and searches. It loses the player if it ends its own move at Close or
*comment Engaged (no roll), and otherwise the player rolls ONE Stealth check per round, DC 15, when an enemy has moved or
*comment any living enemy is at Close or Engaged (disadvantage then, for being that near). The one check is spent by the first
*comment enemy that triggers it, so a group does not mean a roll each; combat_begin_round clears it.
*temp chet_moved false

  *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_lane_move_enemy_relative_to_player combat_active_target "toward"

  *line_break

  *set combat_player_hidden false
  *gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_hidden_found_auto
  *line_break
  [b][👁️ ${combat_enemy_name} spots you][/b]
  *line_break
*elseif (not(combat_hide_checked))
  *gosub combat_check_hide
  *if ((chet_moved) or (combat_hide_blocked))
    *set combat_hide_checked true
    *if (combat_hide_blocked)
      *set disadvantage true
    *gosub combat_hide_dc_calc false
    *gosub_scene dice_engine roll_skill_check combat_hide_dc_value "stealth" "" "" "" "" ""
    *if (check_success)
      *gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_hidden_holds
      *line_break
      [b][👤 Still hidden][/b]
      *line_break
    *else
      *set combat_player_hidden false
      *gosub_scene combat_prose flavor_hidden_found_check
      *line_break
      [b][👁️ ${combat_enemy_name} spots you][/b]
      *line_break
*set combat_turn_advanced_this_page true
*return

*comment THE SHOVE (2026-11-25, PHB'24 Shove): the player makes a Strength or Dexterity save, whichever is better, against DC 8 + the shover's STR modifier + its proficiency
*comment bonus. A failure pushes the player one step straight back (combat_shift_all_ranges, combat_shove_dir from enemy_shove_pick): forced movement, so no opportunity
*comment attack, and the landing is the hazard's own check at the next menu (combat_spells.txt player_step_in_check: grease's slip, fire's burn, a web's hold). A success
*comment holds the ground. The roll, its result and the line are kept per slot against the page (shove_page, shove_failed, shove_v), so a replay prints the same shove.
*comment AN ALLY'S SHOVE (2026-11-25): the foe saves with Strength or Dexterity, whichever is better, against DC 8 + the ally's STR modifier + its proficiency; a failure
*comment pushes it one step to combat_shove_spot (forced movement). The hazard does the rest on the foe's own turn (grease and fire are checked when a turn ends there, a
*comment web holds whoever moves). The roll and the line are kept per ally against the page (shove_page, shove_failed, shove_v, shove_banner).
*label fight_ally_shove
*params fas_a fas_e
*temp fas_p (("combat_ally" & fas_a) & "_")

*temp fas_str {(fas_p & "strength")}

  *set fas_prof combat_lib_prof_bonus

*temp fas_esm sm_mod
*temp fas_ed {(fas_ep & "dexterity")}

*temp fas_label "Strength"

  *set fas_stat "dexterity"
  *set fas_label "Dexterity"
*temp fas_failed {(fas_p & "shove_failed")}

  *set fas_v 1
  *rand fas_v 1 2
  *set {("combat_save_locked_" & fas_e)} false
  *set combat_save_quiet true
  *gosub_scene combat_dice resolve_save fas_ep fas_stat fas_label fas_dc fas_e

    *gosub_scene combat_movement combat_lane_move_enemy_to_position fas_e combat_shove_spot

*temp fas_en {(fas_ep & "name")}
*if (fas_v = 1)
  $!{fas_ae} drops a shoulder into ${fas_ee} and drives hard, trying to shove them back ${combat_shove_onto}.

${fas_banner}

  *line_break

*label combat_next_turn

    *comment GREASE (2026-10-12, the user's call): a greased enemy that tries to MOVE must pass a DC 10 Dexterity check or fall prone and lose its turn; a prone one spends its next turn standing. Standing still and swinging never trips it.
    *comment Whether this enemy would move this turn is worked out here from the same facts the branches below use (close on the player or an ally, back off from a corner, take a terrain step); combat_grease_gate does the rest.
